@q ar1-11 | co-arith#1 | 2 | 計算
8 位元 `11001000`（0xC8）當無號數和當二補數有號數各是多少？
== 200,-56
?? 無號,有號
---
無號 $128+64+8=200$；有號 $200-256=-56$。同一組位元，解讀方式不同。

@q ar1-12 | co-arith#1 | 2 | 計算
8 位元二補數 0x8F 符號延伸成 16 位元，十六進位是？
== 0xFF8F
== FF8F
---
最高位是 1（負數），補 8 個 1。數值不變，都是 $-113$。

@q ar1-13 | co-arith#1 | 2 | 比較
記憶體裡一個位元組是 0xF0。用「有號載入位元組」（lb）和「無號載入位元組」（lbu）讀進 32 位元暫存器，各是多少（十進位）？
== -16,240
?? lb,lbu
---
`lb` 做符號延伸，0xFFFFFFF0＝$-16$；`lbu` 補 0，0x000000F0＝240。

@q ar1-14 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 計算
4 位元二補數 $(-8)+(-1)$，硬體算出的 4 位元結果（十進位）是？有沒有溢位？
== 7,有
?? 結果,溢位
---
`1000`＋`1111`＝`1 0111`，留下 `0111`＝7。兩個負數加出正數，溢位。

@q ar1-15 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 觀念
An 8-bit addition unit computes `01111111 + 00000001`. Let $c_7$ be the carry into the sign position, bit 7, and $c_8$ the carry out of it. The same 8 bits can be read either as an unsigned number, from 0 to 255, or as a two's-complement signed number, from $-128$ to 127, and the unit reports a carry flag for unsigned overflow and an overflow flag for signed overflow. A student works out the sum bit by bit:
```
  01111111   (127)
+ 00000001   (1)
----------
  10000000
```
and then interprets the result in both ways, and asks how each kind of overflow is detected in hardware. A classmate claims that signed overflow can always be detected by testing $c_8$ alone, as for unsigned overflow, and another claims that unsigned overflow occurs whenever the result's highest bit is set, as it is here. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $c_7=1$ and $c_8=0$.
[x] Signed overflow is detected because $c_7\ne c_8$.
[ ] Unsigned overflow occurs because the retained result has its highest bit set.
[ ] Signed overflow can always be detected solely by testing whether $c_8=1$.
[ ] The exact mathematical signed sum is minus 128.
---
「進一出零」對：低七位的連續進位送到符號位，0＋0＋1 產生符號一而沒有向外進位。「兩進位不同為有號溢位」對：127＋1＝128 超過 127。「無號溢位」錯：128 在 0 到 255 內，無號看向外進位，此例為零。「只看向外進位」錯：本例有號溢位但 c₈＝0。「數學和負 128」錯：那是截斷圖樣的有號解讀，數學和為正 128。

@q ar1-16 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 計算
8 位元無號減法 $3-5$，得到的 8 位元結果（無號十進位）？
== 254
---
$3-5+256=254$，硬體有借位（無號下溢）。當有號數讀就是 $-2$。

@q ar1-17 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 陷阱
In C11, `int` and `unsigned int` are both 32-bit types on a student's machine, and the student writes
```
if (-1 < 1u) printf("less\n"); else printf("not less\n");
```
expecting the program to print `less`, since $-1$ is smaller than 1. A classmate warns that the comparison is not done on mathematical values, and asks the student to work out, under the usual arithmetic conversions, what each operand becomes before the comparison and what the program prints on this machine, before running it, and whether the conversion itself is allowed. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The signed minus one is converted to unsigned int for this comparison.
[x] Its converted value is 4294967295.
[ ] The comparison evaluates true because mathematical minus one is less than one.
[ ] This conversion itself is undefined behavior merely because the input value is negative.
[ ] The comparison evaluates true after both operands become unsigned.
---
「轉無號」對：同等級 int 與 unsigned int 混合，採無號類型。「4294967295」對：−1 按模 2³² 轉換。「按實數直比為真」錯：程式先做類型轉換，4294967295 小於一為假。「負轉無號未定義」錯：這項轉換明定為模轉換，不能和有號加法溢位混為一談。「轉後仍真」錯：大於一，結果為假。

@q ar1-18 | co-arith#1 | 2 | 計算
8 位元的偏移表示法（偏移量 127），存的是 130，代表多少？存的是 0 呢？
== 3,-127
?? 130,0
---
值＝存的數 − 127。浮點數的指數就是用偏移表示，好處是可以直接當無號數比大小。

@q ar1-19 | co-arith#1 | 1 | 觀念
8 位元的符號大小表示法，0 有幾種寫法？
== 2
---
`00000000`（+0）和 `10000000`（−0）。一補數也有兩個 0，二補數只有一個，這是二補數被採用的原因之一。

@q ar1-20 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 情境
A student writes two small C11 programs on a machine where `int` is a 32-bit two's-complement type with maximum 2147483647 and `unsigned int` is a 32-bit type with maximum 4294967295; the compiler provides no extension that defines signed overflow:
```
int x = 2147483647;            unsigned u = 4294967295u;
x = x + 1;                     u = u + 1;
```
With optimizations off, the first program prints $-2147483648$; with optimizations on, the compiler removes a later check `if (x < 0)` entirely. The student asks which results the C11 language actually guarantees, and why the hardware's two's-complement wraparound does not settle the question for the signed case, and whether the unsigned result is also left undefined by the language. The student must explain both results in a code review. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The signed addition has undefined behavior in this language model.
[x] The unsigned calculation produces zero under modulo $2^{32}$ arithmetic.
[ ] The signed calculation is guaranteed by C11 to produce minus 2147483648.
[ ] A two's complement hardware representation alone makes signed overflow defined by C11.
[ ] The unsigned calculation produces 2147483648.
---
「有號未定義」對：2147483648 超出指定 int 範圍，題設未採用定義環繞的擴充。「無號為零」對：4294967295＋1 對 2³² 取模。「標準保證最小負數」錯：某些機器指令可能如此，但未定義的程式沒有此保證。「硬體二補數就有定義」錯：語言規則與底層位元表示是兩回事。「無號得 2147483648」錯：把最高位的值與整個模數混淆。

@q ar3-08 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 追蹤
4 位元 Booth 乘法 $6\times(-5)$（乘數 `1011`）。四步裡做了幾次加或減？結果（十進位）？
== 3,-30
?? 加減次數,結果
---
看 $(Q_0,Q_{-1})$：10 減、11 不動、01 加、10 減。程式模擬驗算，8 位元結果 `11100010`＝$-30$。

@q ar3-09 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 計算
Radix-two Booth recodes positive multiplier `0111` using signed digits with ordinary binary position weights. It writes seven as $2^3-2^0$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] From high to low positions, the recoded digits are plus one, zero, zero, minus one.
[x] For multiplicand five, the two nonzero contributions are 40 and minus five.
[ ] The recoded multiplier equals nine.
[ ] Three nonzero arithmetic contributions are required for this recoding.
[ ] Assigning all signed digits unit weight preserves the multiplier seven.
---
「正一零零負一」對：高位權重八，低位權重一。「40 與負五」對：5×8−5＝35。「乘數九」錯：低位是減一，不是加一。「三項」錯：普通 0111 有三個一，但此編碼只有兩個非零數字。「每位權重一」錯：會變成 1−1＝0，位值不能省略。

@q ar3-10 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Use four rounds of radix-two Booth recoding with initial previous bit zero. Count one arithmetic action whenever adjacent inspected bits differ, and no action when they agree. Compare four-bit multiplier patterns `0101`, `1010`, `1111`, and `0000`. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Pattern `0101` requests four arithmetic actions.
[x] Pattern `1010` requests three arithmetic actions.
[ ] Pattern `1111` requests four arithmetic actions.
[ ] Pattern `0000` requests one arithmetic action.
[ ] Every alternating four-bit pattern requests an action in all four rounds when the initial previous bit is zero.
---
「0101 四次」對：由低位看是 10、01、10、01。「1010 三次」對：第一次是 00 不動，再來才是 10、01、10。「1111 四次」錯：第一次 10 減一次，後續都是 11。「0000 一次」錯：全是 00，零次。「交錯就必四輪」錯：還得看最低位與初始零是否不同；1010 就少了第一輪。負乘數圖樣也能依相同相鄰位元規則處理。

@q ar3-11 | co-arith#3 | 2 | 計算
用「移位相加」算 $13\times11$，乘數 11＝`1011`。要做幾次加法（加部分積）？
== 3
---
乘數有 3 個 1，每個 1 加一次左移過的被乘數：$13+26+104=143$。

@q ar3-12 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 情境
Treat x as a 32-bit unsigned value, with every result reduced modulo $2^{32}$. A compiler considers replacing multiplication by ten with shifts and additions. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] `(x << 3) + (x << 1)` equals `x * 10` in this arithmetic.
[x] For x equal to seven, the mathematical product before discarding high bits is 70.
[ ] `x << 10` equals `x * 10` for every input.
[ ] `(x << 2) + x` multiplies every input by ten.
[ ] Shifts and additions are guaranteed to be faster than a multiply instruction on every processor.
---
「左移三加左移一」對：8x＋2x＝10x，模運算也保持等式。「七乘十為七十」對：56＋14。「左移十」錯：乘的是 1024，例如七會得 7168。「左移二再加自身」錯：4x＋x＝5x。「每台都較快」錯：取決於指令延遲、可同時執行程度與編譯器選擇，代數等價不等於固定效能結論。題目用無號，避開有號左移與溢位的額外語言限制。

@q ar3-13 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 陷阱
二補數 $-7$ 做算術右移 1 位（`-7 >> 1`），結果是？
== -4
---
算術右移是向下取整（往 $-\infty$）：$\lfloor-3.5\rfloor=-4$；C 的除法 `-7/2` 則是向 0 取整得 $-3$。用右移代替除法時負數要小心。

@q ar3-14 | co-arith#3 | 2 | 計算
C 語言（C99 之後）`7 / -2` 和 `7 % -2` 各是？
== -3,1
---
商向 0 取整；餘數跟被除數同號，且滿足 $(a/b)\cdot b+a\%b=a$：$(-3)(-2)+1=7$。

@q ar3-15 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 比較
Compare Python integer expressions `-7 // 2` and `-7 % 2` with C11 int expressions `-7 / 2` and `-7 % 2`. All values fit the types and division is by nonzero two. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Python's quotient is minus four.
[x] Python's remainder is positive one.
[x] C11's quotient is minus three.
[ ] C11's remainder is positive one.
[ ] The identity dividend equals quotient times divisor plus remainder fails for one of these two language rules.
---
「Python 商負四」對：整數 // 向下取整。「Python 餘一」對：−7＝(−4)×2＋1。「有號整數商負三」對：題設的 C11 除法向零截斷。「其餘一」錯：C11 的餘數是 −1，−7＝(−3)×2−1。「恆等式有一方失效」錯：兩種都維持恆等式，不同的是商的取整方向與餘數符號。

@q ar3-16 | co-arith#3 | 2 | 計算

== 4,1,4
?? 商,剩下,輪數
---
每輪產生一位商，4 位元除法 4 輪。

@q ar3-17 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 觀念
Multiply two unsigned 32-bit operands exactly into a 64-bit result written as hi:lo, with 32 bits in each half. Consider operands 65536 and 65536. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The exact product is $2^{32}$.
[x] For this example hi is one and lo is zero.
[x] For any such unsigned product, fitting in 32 bits is equivalent to hi being zero.
[ ] Inspecting only the highest bit of lo always detects overflow beyond 32 bits.
[ ] A zero lo always proves that the exact product is zero.
---
「2³²」對：65536＝2¹⁶，相乘即 2³²。「高一低零」對：64 位元結果在第 32 位為一，低 32 位全零。「高半為零等價放得下」對：無號值就是 hi×2³²＋lo。「只看低半最高位」錯：本例低半全零仍超過 32 位元範圍。「低零代表乘積零」錯：非零的 2³² 倍數也會有零低半。

@q ar3-18 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 比較
Under a simplified model, a sequential multiplier takes 32 cycles per product and cannot overlap products. A parallel design completes a product in one cycle, using eight times the arithmetic area. Both have the same clock period. Ignore all other costs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The sequential design needs 320 cycles for ten products.
[x] The parallel design needs ten cycles for ten products.
[ ] The parallel design uses one eighth of the sequential design's arithmetic area.
[ ] The simplified timing assumptions prove that every physical parallel multiplier has the same clock period as every sequential multiplier.
[ ] Under the stated model, the sequential design completes ten products sooner.
---
「320 拍」對：10×32，題設不能重疊。「十拍」對：每拍一個。「面積八分之一」錯：題設是八倍，倒置比例。「能推到所有實體」錯：同週期是本題假設，實際還受最長組合路徑、設計與製程影響。「循序較早」錯：本模型 320 拍對十拍，平行快 32 倍；效能與面積必須在相同假設下比較。

@q ar3-19 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 計算
Recode an eight-bit signed multiplier with radix-four Booth, two original bits per digit and initial low extension bit zero. Include zero-valued rows when allocating partial-product rows. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Four recoded digit positions are required.
[x] An individual unshifted partial product can be twice the multiplicand or its negative.
[x] Adjacent digit positions have weights differing by a factor of four.
[ ] Eight recoded digit positions are required because triples overlap.
[ ] A digit equal to three is required for input triple 011.
---
「四位置」對：八位元每兩位處理一次。「正負兩倍」對：數字集合為 0、±1、±2，適當延伸寬度後兩倍可由左移產生。「權重四倍」對：每進一位置移兩個二進位位元。「八位置」錯：重疊一位是判斷轉換的輔助，不代表每次只處理一個原位元。「011 為三」錯：重新編碼不是直接讀三位元無號值，011 對應正二。

@q ar3-20 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 Booth 演算法對二補數（有號）乘數直接適用。
---
二補數 $Q=-q_{n-1}2^{n-1}+\sum_{i<n-1}q_i2^i$。Booth 第 $i$ 步做 $(q_{i-1}-q_i)\cdot M\cdot2^i$（$q_{-1}=0$），全部加總是望遠鏡和 $\sum_i(q_{i-1}-q_i)2^i=-q_{n-1}2^{n-1}+\sum_{i<n-1}q_i2^i=Q$，正好包含最高位的負權重，所以不需要先取絕對值。

@q ar4-08 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
0.375 的 IEEE 754 單精度十六進位表示？
== 3EC00000
== 0x3EC00000
---
$0.375=1.1_2\times2^{-2}$；指數欄 $-2+127=125$＝`01111101`；尾數 `1000…`。程式驗算。

@q ar4-09 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
$-13.25$ 的單精度十六進位？
== C1540000
== 0xC1540000
---
$13.25=1101.01_2=1.10101\times2^3$；符號 1、指數 130＝`10000010`、尾數 `10101000…`。

@q ar4-10 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
單精度 `BE200000` 是多少？
== -0.15625
---
符號 1；指數欄 `01111100`＝124，$124-127=-3$；尾數 `0100…`＝1.25。$-1.25\times2^{-3}$。

@q ar4-11 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 計算
單精度 `00000001`（最小的正數）是 2 的幾次方？
== -149
---
指數欄全 0 是非正規數：值＝$0.f\times2^{-126}$，最小的 $f$ 是 $2^{-23}$，所以 $2^{-149}$。程式驗算約 $1.4\times10^{-45}$。

@q ar4-12 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
單精度 `7F800000` 代表什麼？
== +∞
== 正無限大
---
指數欄全 1、尾數全 0 是無限大；尾數非 0 是 NaN。

@q ar4-13 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Round the exact real number one third to IEEE binary32 using nearest, ties to even. Two neighboring candidates have hexadecimal patterns `3EAAAAAA` and `3EAAAAAB`. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The rounded result has pattern `3EAAAAAB`.
[x] The exact value is closer to the upper candidate than to the lower candidate.
[ ] The exact value is halfway between the candidates, so a tie decides this result.
[ ] Truncating the infinite binary expansion produces the same pattern as rounding here.
[ ] One third has an exact finite binary32 representation.
---
「末位 AB」對：最接近的數是較高候選。「較接近上方」對：以兩候選間距為單位，真值位在從下方起的 2/3 處，上方誤差 1/3。「剛好一半」錯：不是平手，不必啟用取偶的分支。「截斷相同」錯：截斷會留在較低的 AA。「可精確」錯：三分之一的二進位小數無限循環，有限有效位只能近似。

@q ar4-14 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
單精度的「機器 epsilon」（1.0 和下一個可表示數的差）是 2 的幾次方？
== -23
---
1.0 的尾數最後一位代表 $2^{-23}\approx1.19\times10^{-7}$，大約 7 位十進位有效數字。

@q ar4-15 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 觀念
Use IEEE floating-point comparisons without transformations that assume NaNs cannot occur. Consider quiet NaN, positive infinity, and signed zeros. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For a quiet NaN x, `x != x` evaluates true.
[x] Positive infinity compares equal to itself.
[x] Positive zero and negative zero compare equal.
[ ] Positive infinity makes `x != x` true.
[ ] Distinct zero sign bits force positive zero to compare unequal to negative zero.
---
「NaN 自己不等」對：IEEE 不相等比較對 NaN 為真。「正無限等於自己」對：無限大不是 NaN。「正負零相等」對：數值比較不因符號位不同而判不等。「無限自不等」錯：把無限與非數混淆。「零符號不同就不等」錯：原始圖樣不同與數值相等是不同判斷。

@q ar4-16 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 追蹤
浮點加法 $1.5\times2^3+1.25\times2^1$：小的那個要先怎麼對齊？結果？
== 右移 2 位,14.5
== 右移2位,14.5
?? 對齊,結果
---
指數差 2，把 $1.25\times2^1$ 寫成 $0.3125\times2^3$，相加 $1.8125\times2^3=14.5$。對齊時被移出去的位元可能造成誤差。

@q ar4-17 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 情境
Starting from binary64 zero, add the binary64 value nearest decimal 0.1 ten times. Round each addition to binary64 using nearest, ties to even, with no reassociation or extra precision. For these finite values, use absolute tolerance $10^{-12}$ when comparing the final sum to 1. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Decimal 0.1 has no exact finite binary expansion.
[x] The final binary64 sum is slightly below one under the specified evaluation.
[x] The final sum passes the specified absolute-tolerance comparison with one.
[ ] Binary64 cannot represent the value one exactly.
[ ] Exact equality with one is guaranteed because ten times the real number 0.1 is one.
---
「0.1 無有限二進位」對：約分後分母為 10，含有因數 5，不能只靠 2 的冪表示。「略小於一」對：逐次捨入後為約 0.9999999999999999。「通過容忍比較」對：差約 1.11×10⁻¹⁶，小於 10⁻¹²。「一不精確」錯：一是可精確表示的 2⁰。「實數式保證逐次運算相等」錯：輸入表示與每一步捨入都要考慮。本題容忍值是針對此尺度明定，其他應用仍需依尺度與誤差要求選擇。

@q ar4-18 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 找錯
Encode positive six in IEEE binary32. Its normalized form is $1.1_2\times2^2$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The stored exponent is 129.
[x] The complete hexadecimal pattern is `40C00000`.
[x] The first stored fraction bit is one.
[ ] The exponent bits are `00000010` because the unbiased exponent is two.
[ ] The normal significand's leading one must be stored as an additional fraction bit.
---
「129」對：2＋127。「40C00000」對：符號零、指數 10000001、小數 1000…，合成此圖樣。「首小數位一」對：1.1 的小數部分是 .1。「直接存二」錯：漏加偏移；欄位二會被解讀為指數 −125。「多存首一」錯：正常數首一隱含，23 位小數只存小數點後。

@q ar4-19 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 計算
單精度能精確表示所有整數到 $2^{24}$。第一個**不能**精確表示的正整數？
== 16777217
---
$2^{24}+1$：尾數只有 24 位有效位（含隱藏的 1），這個數需要 25 位。它會被捨入成 $2^{24}$。

@q ar4-20 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 比較
Compare IEEE binary32 and binary64 normal-number formats. Distinguish stored fraction bits from significand precision including the implicit leading one. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Binary32 stores one sign bit, eight exponent bits, and 23 fraction bits.
[x] Binary64 normal values have 53 bits of significand precision.
[x] Binary64 stores 11 exponent bits with bias 1023.
[ ] Binary32 stores 24 fraction bits in addition to its sign and exponent.
[ ] Binary64 uses 52 exponent bits and 11 fraction bits.
---
「1、8、23」對：總和 32。「53 位精度」對：52 位儲存小數加隱含的一。「11 與 1023」對：倍精度的指數配置。「另存 24 位」錯：24 是正常值含隱含首位的精度，實際只存 23 位小數。「52 指數、11 小數」錯：把兩者對調，正確總配置為 1、11、52。

@q pf1-08 | co-perf#1 | 1 | 計算
時脈 2.5 GHz，一個週期多長（ns）？
== 0.4
---
$1/(2.5\times10^9)$ 秒。

@q pf1-09 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
程式有 $2\times10^9$ 條指令、CPI 1.2，要在 0.8 秒內跑完，時脈至少要幾 GHz？
== 3
---
$f=\frac{IC\times CPI}{T}=\frac{2.4\times10^9}{0.8}$。

@q pf1-10 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
程式跑了 $6\times10^9$ 個週期、執行 $4\times10^9$ 條指令。CPI？
== 1.5
---
CPI＝總週期數／指令數：平均每條指令花幾個週期。

@q pf1-11 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
時脈 2 GHz、平均 CPI 1.25。MIPS？
== 1600
---
$\frac{f}{CPI\times10^6}=\frac{2\times10^9}{1.25\times10^6}$。

@q pf1-12 | co-perf#1 | 3 | 情境
On a 1 GHz machine, the same source program is compiled by three different compilers. A product team must choose one of them, and the only thing that matters to the team is how long the program takes to run:
```
compiler  instructions   CPI
X         5 × 10^9       1.0
Y         2 × 10^9       2.0
Z         3 × 10^9       1.2
```
The MIPS rating of a run is the number of instructions executed per second, divided by $10^6$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The code from compiler Z runs fastest, in 3.6 s.
[x] The MIPS ratings are 1000 for X, 500 for Y, and about 833 for Z.
[ ] Since its MIPS rating is the highest, the code from X runs fastest.
[ ] The code from Z is 1.25 times as fast as the code from Y.
[ ] Since the CPI of the code from Y is twice that of X, its execution time is twice as long.
---
CPU 時間 $=$ 指令數 $\times$ CPI $\div$ 時脈：X 是 5 秒、Y 是 4 秒、Z 是 $3\times10^9\times1.2/10^9=3.6$ 秒。

「Z 最快、3.6 秒」對。

「MIPS 1000、500、約 833」對：MIPS $=$ 時脈 $\div$（CPI $\times10^6$），$10^9/(1.2\times10^6)\approx833$。

「MIPS 最高所以 X 最快」錯：MIPS 只看每秒幾條指令，不看指令做了多少事；X 用了最多指令，反而最慢。

「Z 比 Y 快 1.25 倍」錯：$4/3.6\approx1.11$ 倍；1.25 倍是 Y 對 X。

「CPI 兩倍所以時間兩倍」錯：還要乘指令數，Y 的指令只有 X 的 40%。

@q pf1-13 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 找錯
Let IC be the instruction count and CPI the average number of clock cycles per instruction. Three programs run on the same 3 GHz processor, so one clock cycle lasts $1/3$ ns. A student claims that $P_3$ finishes first because it executes the fewest instructions:
```
program  IC          CPI
P1       2 × 10^9    1.5
P2       6 × 10^9    1.0
P3       1 × 10^9    4.0
```
Which of the following statements about CPU time are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] CPU time = IC × CPI × clock cycle time.
[ ] CPU time = IC × clock rate ÷ CPI.
[x] P1 takes 1 second.
[ ] P3 takes less time than P1, because P3 executes only half as many instructions.
[ ] Raising the clock rate to 6 GHz necessarily halves the CPU time of all three programs.
---
「× 週期時間」對：指令數 × 每條幾拍 × 每拍幾秒。

「× 時脈 ÷ CPI」錯：單位對不上。週期時間＝1／頻率，所以是 IC × CPI ÷ 頻率。

「P1 是 1 秒」對：$2\times10^9\times1.5\div(3\times10^9)=1$。

「P3 指令少所以比 P1 快」錯：$1\times10^9\times4\div(3\times10^9)\approx1.33$ 秒，比 P1 慢；CPI 高抵銷了指令少。

「頻率加倍時間一定減半」錯：記憶體的存取時間不會跟著變快，時脈變快後每次 miss 要等的拍數變多，CPI 往往上升。

@q pf1-14 | co-perf#1 | 3 | 計算
指令比例：算術邏輯 40%（CPI 1）、load 30%（CPI 4）、store 15%（CPI 3）、branch 15%（CPI 2）。把 load 的 CPI 降到 2，整體快幾倍？
== 1.34
---
原 CPI $0.4+1.2+0.45+0.3=2.35$；新 $0.4+0.6+0.45+0.3=1.75$；$2.35/1.75\approx1.34$。

@q pf1-15 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
程式在 A 上跑 4 秒、B 上跑 5 秒。A 的效能是 B 的幾倍？
== 1.25
---
效能＝1／時間，$5/4$。說「A 比 B 快 25%」；不要說成「B 比 A 慢 25%」（B 是慢 20%）。

@q pf1-16 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 情境
A single-threaded text-processing program reads 2 GB from disk and sends its results over the network. The disk delivers about 125 MB/s, so reading the input alone takes about 16 s. During part of the run, another program was also using the same core. Running it under `time` prints:
```
real    20.0s
user     2.4s
sys      0.6s
```
Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] To compare two processors, the CPU time user+sys, here 3.0 s, is the more relevant measure.
[x] The real (elapsed) time also includes time spent on I/O and time when the other program was running.
[ ] Since real time is 20 s, the CPU was busy running this program for 20 s.
[x] About 17 s of the elapsed time were spent not running this program on the CPU.
[x] A processor twice as fast would cut the real time only to about 18.5 s.
---
「比較 CPU 看 user＋sys」對：CPU 時間才反映處理器本身；sys 是作業系統替它做的工作。

「real 包含等 I/O 和別的程式」對：使用者在意的是 real（回應時間）。

「CPU 忙了 20 秒」錯：真正在跑這個程式的只有 3 秒。

「約 17 秒沒在 CPU 上跑」對：$20-3$。

「CPU 快一倍 real 只到約 18.5 秒」對：只有 3 秒那部分變成 1.5 秒，$17+1.5=18.5$；這正是 Amdahl 定律的情況。

@q pf1-17 | co-perf#1 | 3 | 陷阱
An old processor and a new processor run the same program at the same clock rate. The new design splits many complex instructions into simple ones:
```
design  instructions   average CPI   clock
old     1.0 × 10^9     2.0           2 GHz
new     1.3 × 10^9     1.4           2 GHz
```
So the new design executes 30% more instructions while the average CPI drops by 30%. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The new design runs the program in 0.91 s, compared with 1.0 s for the old one.
[x] The new design is about 1.10 times as fast as the old one on this program.
[ ] The new design is slower, because it executes more instructions.
[ ] The new design is 30% faster, because the CPI dropped by 30%.
[ ] If the new design could run only at 1.8 GHz, it would still be faster than the old one.
---
時間 $=$ 指令數 × CPI ÷ 時脈。

「新的 0.91 秒、舊的 1.0 秒」對：$1.3\times10^9\times1.4\div(2\times10^9)=0.91$；$10^9\times2\div(2\times10^9)=1$。

「快約 1.10 倍」對：$1/0.91\approx1.099$。

「指令多所以變慢」錯：只看一個因素會誤判。

「CPI 降 30% 所以快 30%」錯：指令數同時多了 30%。

「1.8 GHz 也還比較快」錯：$1.3\times10^9\times1.4\div(1.8\times10^9)\approx1.011$ 秒，比原本慢約 1%。

@q pf1-18 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
CPI 1.5、時脈 3 GHz，執行 $1.2\times10^{10}$ 條指令要幾秒？
== 6
---
$1.2\times10^{10}\times1.5/(3\times10^9)$。

@q pf2-06 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
三個程式的執行時間 2、4、8 秒。算術平均和幾何平均？
== 4.67,4
?? 算術,幾何
---
$(2+4+8)/3$；$\sqrt[3]{64}=4$。

@q pf2-07 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
兩個程式在參考機器上 100、200 秒，在新機器上 10、50 秒。SPEC ratio 的幾何平均？
== 6.32
== √40
---
比值 10、4，$\sqrt{40}\approx6.32$。

@q pf2-08 | co-perf#2 | 3 | 比較
A lab compares two servers, $X$ and $Y$, on its two most important programs and records these execution times:

| program | time on $X$ (s) | time on $Y$ (s) |
|---|---|---|
| P1 | 1 | 10 |
| P2 | 1000 | 100 |

The lab's real workload runs P1 once and P2 once every night, and the manager wants a single number per server for a purchase report. Two summaries are proposed: the arithmetic mean of the execution times, and the geometric mean of the execution times. Both servers cost the same, and most people who see the report will never look at the raw times. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The arithmetic means of the execution times are 500.5 s for $X$ and 55 s for $Y$.
[x] The geometric means of the execution times of $X$ and $Y$ are both about 31.6 s.
[ ] Both kinds of mean say that $X$ is faster.
[x] Running both programs once takes 1001 s on $X$ and 110 s on $Y$.
[ ] The geometric mean tells which machine finishes the workload "run P1 once and P2 once" sooner.
---
「算術平均 500.5 對 55」對：$(1+1000)/2$、$(10+100)/2$。

「幾何平均都約 31.6」對：$\sqrt{1\cdot1000}=\sqrt{10\cdot100}=\sqrt{1000}$。

「兩種都說 $X$ 快」錯：算術平均說 $Y$ 快很多，幾何平均說一樣。

「各跑一次的總時間 1001 對 110」對：算術平均正比於這個總時間。

「幾何平均告訴你誰先跑完」錯：幾何平均說平手，實際上 $Y$ 快 9 倍。它反映的是「比值的平均」（P1 上 $X$ 快 10 倍、P2 上 $Y$ 快 10 倍，剛好抵消），不是任何實際工作負載的總時間。要選哪種平均，取決於你在意什麼。

@q pf2-09 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 觀念
SPEC reports, for each program, the ratio $t_R / t$ of a reference machine's time $t_R$ to the measured time $t$, and summarizes a machine by the geometric mean (GM) of these ratios. For example, if the reference machine takes 100 s on a program and machine $X$ takes 25 s, the ratio for $X$ on that program is 4. A reviewer worries that switching to a newer reference machine, which is 3 times as fast as the old one on some programs and 5 times as fast on others, could change the ranking of two machines $X$ and $Y$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For machines $X$ and $Y$, $\frac{\mathrm{GM}(t_R / t_X)}{\mathrm{GM}(t_R / t_Y)} = \mathrm{GM}(t_Y / t_X)$, so the reference times cancel.
[x] Therefore the ranking of machines by this geometric mean does not depend on which reference machine is used.
[ ] The arithmetic mean of the ratios has the same property, so it would order the machines the same way no matter which reference machine is chosen.
[x] The geometric mean of the ratios does not, in general, predict the total time of any particular workload.
[ ] SPEC uses the geometric mean because it is always the largest of the arithmetic, geometric, and harmonic means.
---
「參考時間約掉」對：每個程式的 $\frac{t_R/t_X}{t_R/t_Y}=\frac{t_Y}{t_X}$，幾何平均可以逐項相除。

「排名與參考機器無關」對：比值裡已經沒有 $t_R$。

「算術平均也一樣」錯：$\frac{\text{AM}(t_R/t_X)}{\text{AM}(t_R/t_Y)}$ 無法約掉 $t_R$，換參考機器可能翻盤。

「不代表任何工作負載的總時間」對：它是比值的平均，跟「這些程式各跑一次要多久」不同。

「因為它最大」錯：幾何平均介於調和平均和算術平均之間，不是最大的；選它是因為跟參考機器無關。

@q pf2-10 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
三個程式 MIPS 50、100、200，每個程式的**執行時間**相同（不是指令數相同）。整體平均 MIPS？
== 116.67
== 350/3
---
時間相同時，每個程式跑的指令數跟 MIPS 成正比，總指令／總時間＝算術平均 $(50+100+200)/3$。指令數相同時才用調和平均。

@q pf2-11 | co-perf#2 | 3 | 陷阱
「幾何平均比較高的機器，跑完整套測試的總時間一定比較短」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
上面 P1、P2 的例子：幾何平均相同，總時間差 10 倍。幾何平均不能預測總時間。

@q pf2-12 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
使用者 70% 的時間跑程式 X、30% 跑 Y。新機器讓 X 快 2 倍、Y 慢 1.5 倍（時間變 1.5 倍）。整體時間變成原來的幾倍？
== 0.8
---
加權：$0.7\times0.5+0.3\times1.5=0.35+0.45=0.8$。

@q pf2-13 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 找錯
程式 A、B 分別快了 2 倍和 8 倍（兩者原本時間一樣），學生說「平均快 5 倍」。用總時間算，整體真正快幾倍？
== 3.2
---
原本 $1+1=2$；新的 $0.5+0.125=0.625$；$2/0.625=3.2$。速率類的比值不能直接算術平均。

@q pf2-14 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 觀念
A benchmark consists of two programs, and each performs 100 tasks. Program 1 runs at 10 tasks per second and program 2 at 40 tasks per second, so they take 10 s and 2.5 s. A report must give one execution-time figure and one rate figure for the whole benchmark, where running the benchmark means running each program once. The author considers the arithmetic mean and the harmonic mean, and wants each summary to agree with the total time and with the overall rate of the benchmark, since it will be compared with other benchmarks. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] To summarize execution times, the arithmetic mean is appropriate, because it is proportional to the total time.
[x] To summarize rates when every program does the same amount of work, the harmonic mean is appropriate.
[x] The overall rate for running both programs is 16 tasks per second.
[ ] The overall rate for running both programs is the arithmetic mean of the rates, 25 tasks per second, since each program does the same 100 tasks.
[ ] To summarize execution times, the harmonic mean is appropriate.
---
「時間用算術平均」對：它乘上個數就是總時間。

「工作量相同時速率用調和平均」對。

「整體速率 16」對：時間分別是 10 秒和 2.5 秒，共 12.5 秒做 200 件，$200/12.5=16$；也就是 $\frac2{1/10+1/40}=16$。

「算術平均 25」錯：快的那個只跑了很短時間，不該占一半的權重。

「時間用調和平均」錯：方向反了。

@q pf2-15 | co-perf#2 | 3 | 證明
說明幾何平均為什麼滿足：A 對參考機器的比值的幾何平均 ÷ B 對參考機器的比值的幾何平均，與參考機器無關。
---
$\frac{\left(\prod_i\frac{R_i}{A_i}\right)^{1/n}}{\left(\prod_i\frac{R_i}{B_i}\right)^{1/n}}=\left(\prod_i\frac{B_i}{A_i}\right)^{1/n}$，參考時間 $R_i$ 完全約掉，結果只跟 A、B 自己的時間有關。

@q pf3-07 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 計算
程式 30% 的時間加速 3 倍、另外 20% 加速 2 倍，其餘不變。整體快幾倍？
== 1.43
---
新時間 $0.5+0.3/3+0.2/2=0.7$，$1/0.7\approx1.43$。

@q pf3-08 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 計算
Gustafson：序列部分佔 10%（以平行後的時間來量），64 核心的擴展加速？
== 57.7
---
$N-s(N-1)=64-0.1\times63$。問題規模跟著核心數變大時，序列部分的影響小很多。

@q pf3-09 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 情境
花一個月把只佔 5% 時間的函式優化到幾乎不花時間。整體最多快多少？
== 1.053
== 5.3%
---
$1/0.95\approx1.053$。先找出最花時間的部分（profiling）才值得優化。

@q pf3-10 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 計算
16 核心得到 10 倍加速。平行效率是多少？
== 62.5%
== 0.625
---
效率＝加速／核心數：理想時每個核心都貢獻 1 倍，效率 100%。

@q pf3-11 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 計算
某部分佔 60% 的時間。要讓整體快 2 倍，這部分至少要加速幾倍？
== 6
---
$0.4+0.6/s=0.5$ ⇒ $s=6$。

@q pf3-12 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 陷阱
A profiler shows that a program spends 40% of its original execution time in an image filter and 60% elsewhere, and only the filter can be sped up, for example by rewriting it in hand-tuned assembly. The team considers making the filter 4 times as fast with moderate effort, 5 times as fast with more effort, or, in the best case, almost infinitely fast. The project goal is to make the whole program twice as fast, and the manager asks whether any of these plans can reach that goal. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] No matter how much that part is sped up, the overall speedup stays below $1/0.6 \approx 1.67$.
[x] Speeding up that part by a factor of 4 gives an overall speedup of about 1.43.
[ ] If that part is sped up enough, for example by a factor of 1000, the overall speedup can reach 2, because the filter's time then almost vanishes.
[ ] Speeding up that part by a factor of 5 gives an overall speedup of 2.
[x] To make an overall speedup of 2 possible at all, the part that is sped up must take more than half of the original execution time.
---
Amdahl：整體加速 $=\dfrac{1}{(1-f)+f/s}$，$f=0.4$。

「上限 $1/0.6$」對：$s$ 再大，剩下的 60% 沒動，時間至少 0.6。

「加速 4 倍約 1.43」對：$1/(0.6+0.1)\approx1.43$。

「夠快就能到 2」錯：上限是 1.67。

「加速 5 倍就是 2」錯：$1/(0.6+0.08)\approx1.47$。

「要到 2，被加速的部分必須超過一半」對：上限是 $1/(1-f)$，要大於 2 就要 $f>0.5$（剛好 0.5 時要無限快才碰得到 2）。

@q pf3-13 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 比較
A weather simulation has a serial fraction of 10%: on the reference machine, 10% of its running time is spent in setup and output that cannot be parallelized, and the rest parallelizes perfectly. The team runs it on 10 cores in two ways. In strong scaling, the total problem size stays fixed, so the same forecast is computed faster. In weak scaling, the work per core stays fixed, so with 10 cores the team computes a forecast on a grid 10 times as fine in about the same time. A manager reads two reports, one giving a speedup of about 5.26 and the other a scaled speedup of 9.1, and asks which law each number comes from and why they differ so much, and which kind of scaling scientific simulations usually care about in practice, and why. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Strong scaling is described by Amdahl's law, which gives a speedup of about 5.26 here.
[x] Weak scaling is described by Gustafson's law, which gives a scaled speedup of $10 - 0.1 \times 9 = 9.1$ here.
[ ] In weak scaling, the total problem size stays fixed as cores are added.
[x] Scientific simulations often use weak scaling to compute finer results in about the same time.
[ ] Gustafson's law also gives a speedup of about 5.26 here, because both laws use the same serial fraction.
---
「強擴展是 Amdahl、約 5.26」對：$1/(0.1+0.9/10)=1/0.19\approx5.26$。

「弱擴展是 Gustafson、9.1」對：在 10 核心上的工作，若改用 1 核心做要 $0.1+0.9\times10=9.1$ 倍時間。

「弱擴展問題大小固定」錯：那是強擴展；弱擴展是每核心的工作量固定。

「科學計算常用弱擴展」對：有更多機器就算更大或更細的問題。

「Gustafson 也是 5.26」錯：兩個定律回答的是不同的問題，數字不同。

@q pf3-14 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 找錯
程式兩部分各佔 50%，A 部分加速 2 倍、B 部分加速 2 倍。學生說「整體加速 4 倍」。正確？
== 2
---
兩部分都快 2 倍，整體就是 2 倍。加速比不能相加或相乘。

@q pf3-15 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 計算
95% 可平行的程式，要達到 15 倍加速最少要幾個核心？
== 57
---
$\frac1{0.05+0.95/N}\ge15$ ⇒ $0.95/N\le\frac1{15}-0.05=\frac1{60}$ ⇒ $N\ge57$。核心數超過 57 後加速逐漸逼近上限 20。

@q pf3-16 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 證明
由 Amdahl 定律推出：可平行比例 $f$ 時，加速上限是 $\frac1{1-f}$。
---
$N$ 個核心時加速 $S(N)=\frac1{(1-f)+f/N}$。$N\to\infty$ 時 $f/N\to0$，$S\to\frac1{1-f}$。序列部分的時間不會因為加核心而減少，所以它決定上限。

@q pf4-05 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 計算
處理器 3 GHz、1.2 V 時功耗 100 W。降到 2 GHz、1.0 V，動態功耗約多少 W？
== 46.3
---
$100\times(1.0/1.2)^2\times(2/3)\approx46.3$。電壓的平方影響最大。

@q pf4-06 | co-perf#4 | 3 | 陷阱
A laptop processor's dynamic power is proportional to $C V^2 f$, where $C$ is the capacitive load, $V$ the supply voltage, and $f$ the clock rate, and a program needs a fixed number of clock cycles, say $3\times10^{9}$. To save battery, the operating system can halve the clock rate, with or without also lowering the voltage by 15%. The processor also leaks static power whenever it is on, at a roughly constant rate that does not depend on $f$. A student compares the dynamic power, the running time, and the dynamic energy needed to finish the program in each setting, and also asks what happens to the static energy when the program runs longer. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Halving the clock rate while keeping the voltage unchanged halves the dynamic power.
[x] Halving the clock rate while keeping the voltage unchanged leaves the dynamic energy needed to finish the program unchanged.
[x] Halving the clock rate while keeping the voltage unchanged doubles the time the program takes.
[x] If the clock rate is halved and the voltage is also lowered by 15%, the dynamic energy to finish the program becomes about 0.72 of the original.
[ ] Halving the clock rate also halves the static (leakage) energy used to finish the program, since the processor then switches less often and spends fewer cycles on the work.
---
「功耗減半」對：$P\propto CV^2f$，$f$ 減半。

「能量不變」對：能量＝功耗×時間，時間變兩倍；也可以直接看每次切換的能量 $\propto CV^2$，週期數固定，跟頻率無關。功耗和能量是不同的東西，這是最常見的混淆。

「時間變兩倍」對：週期數固定，每個週期變兩倍長。

「再降 15% 電壓，能量約 0.72」對：$0.85^2=0.7225$。要省能量必須降電壓，降頻只是讓降壓變得可行。

「靜態能量也減半」錯：漏電功耗大致跟頻率無關，時間變兩倍，靜態能量反而約變兩倍。

@q pf4-07 | co-perf#4 | 3 | 計算
一顆核心在 $V$、$f$ 下的功耗是 $P$。換成兩顆核心，各在 $0.8V$、$0.8f$。總功耗和總處理能力（工作可完美平行）各是原來的幾倍？
== 1.024,1.6
?? 功耗,處理能力
---
每顆 $0.8^3=0.512$，兩顆 1.024；處理能力 $2\times0.8$。差不多的功耗換到 1.6 倍的效能：這就是轉向多核的原因。

@q pf4-08 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 計算
良率模型 $\text{yield}=(1+\frac{D\cdot A}{2})^{-2}$，缺陷密度 $D=0.5$ 個／cm²。晶粒面積 1 cm² 和 2 cm² 的良率各是？
== 0.64,0.444
?? 1cm²,2cm²
---
$(1.25)^{-2}=0.64$；$(1.5)^{-2}\approx0.444$。面積加倍、良率下降，每顆好晶片的成本超過兩倍。

@q pf4-09 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 計算
300 mm 晶圓（半徑 150 mm）、晶粒 100 mm²，不考慮邊緣浪費，大約能切幾顆？
== 706
== 707
---
$\pi\times150^2/100\approx706.9$。實際上邊緣的不完整晶粒要扣掉。

@q pf4-10 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 觀念
Dennard scaling says that as transistors shrink, power density stays about the same. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Dennard scaling broke down around 2005, because supply voltage could no longer be reduced much and leakage grew.
[x] After that, raising the clock rate would have raised power density too much, so the industry turned to multicore processors.
[ ] Moore's law, the growth in the number of transistors per chip, ended at the same time as Dennard scaling.
[x] Today a chip may contain more transistors than can be powered at full speed at the same time.
[ ] Dennard scaling still holds today.
---
「約 2005 年失效」對：電壓降不下去（接近臨界電壓），漏電又變大。

「轉向多核」對：與其把一顆核心推到更高時脈，不如用多顆中等時脈的核心。

「摩爾定律同時結束」錯：電晶體數量還在增加，只是不能都同時全速運作。

「電晶體多到不能同時全速」對：這叫暗矽（dark silicon），也是專用加速器興起的原因。

「現在仍成立」錯。

@q pf4-11 | co-perf#4 | 3 | 情境
Which of the following statements about "dark silicon" are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It refers to a chip having more transistors than can be powered at full speed at the same time within its power budget.
[x] It is a consequence of the end of Dennard scaling.
[ ] It refers to transistors that are defective because of manufacturing errors.
[x] It encourages specialized accelerators that are powered on only when their kind of work is needed.
[ ] It means that chips are designed with large areas that contain no transistors at all.
---
「電晶體多到不能同時全速」對：功耗上限固定，晶片上能同時開著的只是一部分。

「Dennard scaling 結束的後果」對：電晶體變小後功率密度不再保持不變，同時開太多會過熱。

「製造缺陷」錯：跟良率無關。

「推動專用加速器」對：既然不能全部同時開，不如做很多「用到才開」的專用電路，每一種都很省電。

「刻意留空」錯：電晶體都在，只是不能同時全速運作。

@q pf4-12 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 找錯
學生用「功耗 $\propto Vf$」算電壓降 10%、頻率降 10% 後的功耗，得到 0.81 倍。正確？
== 0.729
---
$P\propto CV^2f$：$0.9^2\times0.9=0.729$。

@q pf4-13 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 情境
A phone lowers both the clock rate and the supply voltage of its cores when idle and raises them when busy (DVFS). Dynamic power is proportional to $C V^2 f$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Lowering $f$ allows $V$ to be lowered as well, and lowering both reduces dynamic power roughly with the cube of the scaling factor.
[x] Reducing both $V$ and $f$ by 20% reduces dynamic power to about $0.8^3 \approx 0.51$ of the original.
[ ] DVFS mainly reduces leakage power and has little effect on dynamic power.
[x] The energy saving per task comes from the lower voltage, since dynamic energy per operation is proportional to $C V^2$.
[ ] Lowering only $f$, without lowering $V$, reduces the dynamic energy needed for a task.
---
「降頻才能降壓，功率約隨三次方下降」對：$V^2\cdot f$ 都跟著縮小。

「各降 20%，約 0.51」對：$0.8^2\times0.8=0.512$。

「主要降漏電」錯：主要降的是動態功耗。

「省能量靠降電壓」對：每次切換的能量 $\propto CV^2$，跟頻率無關。

「只降頻也省能量」錯：功率降低但時間拉長，動態能量不變。

@q pf4-14 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 觀念
Compare static (leakage) power with dynamic power in a processor. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Dynamic power is roughly proportional to $C V^2 f$ and comes from transistors switching.
[x] Static power comes from leakage current, which flows even when transistors are not switching.
[ ] Stopping the clock reduces static power to zero.
[x] Cutting off the power supply to idle blocks (power gating) removes their leakage.
[ ] Halving the clock rate halves the static power.
---
「動態 $\propto CV^2f$，來自切換」對。

「靜態來自漏電，不切換也有」對：只要有電壓就會漏。

「停時脈就沒有靜態功耗」錯：停時脈只讓動態功耗降到 0，漏電還在。

「切斷電源可以消除漏電」對：這就是 power gating，代價是喚醒要時間、內容會消失。

「降頻減半靜態功耗」錯：漏電大致跟頻率無關。

@q cd1-07 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算
Encode the RV32I instruction `add x8, x17, x18`. It is R-type with opcode `0110011`, funct3 `000`, and funct7 `0000000`; the layout from bit 31 down to bit 0 is funct7 | rs2 | rs1 | funct3 | rd | opcode. Give the 32-bit machine code in hexadecimal.
== 0x01288433
== 01288433
---
funct7 0000000｜rs2 10010（18）｜rs1 10001（17）｜funct3 000｜rd 01000（8）｜opcode 0110011。
連起來 0000 0001 0010 1000 1000 0100 0011 0011 = `0x01288433`。注意 rs2 在 rs1 的左邊（高位），跟組語裡寫的順序相反。

@q cd1-08 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算
Encode the RV32I instruction `lw x8, 8(x17)`. It is I-type with opcode `0000011` and funct3 `010`; the layout from bit 31 down to bit 0 is imm[11:0] | rs1 | funct3 | rd | opcode. Give the 32-bit machine code in hexadecimal.
== 0x0088A403
== 0088A403
---
imm 000000001000（8）｜rs1 10001（17，基底）｜funct3 010｜rd 01000（8，目的地）｜opcode 0000011。
連起來 0000 0000 1000 1000 1010 0100 0000 0011 = `0x0088A403`。

@q cd1-09 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算
Encode the RV32I instruction `addi x8, x8, -1`. It is I-type with opcode `0010011` and funct3 `000`; the layout from bit 31 down to bit 0 is imm[11:0] | rs1 | funct3 | rd | opcode. Give the 32-bit machine code in hexadecimal.
== 0xFFF40413
== FFF40413
---
$-1$ 的 12 位元二補數是 111111111111，所以最高 12 位元全是 1。rs1 01000｜funct3 000｜rd 01000｜opcode 0010011。
連起來 1111 1111 1111 0100 0000 0100 0001 0011 = `0xFFF40413`。執行時立即值會符號延伸成 32（或 64）位元的 $-1$。

@q cd1-10 | co-datapath#1 | 3 | 計算
The 32-bit RV32I machine code `0x00442503` has opcode `0000011` and funct3 `010`, which is `lw`. Write the instruction in assembly form, such as `lw x1, 0(x2)`.
== lw x10, 4(x8)
== lw x10,4(x8)
---
`0x00442503` = 0000 0000 0100 0100 0010 0101 0000 0011。
I 型拆欄位：imm = 000000000100（4）｜rs1 = 01000（8）｜funct3 = 010｜rd = 01010（10）｜opcode = 0000011。
`lw rd, imm(rs1)` → `lw x10, 4(x8)`。

@q cd1-11 | co-datapath#1 | 3 | 計算
The 32-bit RV32I machine code `0x40940533` has opcode `0110011` (R-type). For this opcode, funct3 `000` with funct7 `0000000` means `add`, and funct3 `000` with funct7 `0100000` means `sub`. Write the instruction in assembly form, such as `add x1, x2, x3`.
== sub x10, x8, x9
== sub x10,x8,x9
---
`0x40940533` = 0100 0000 1001 0100 0000 0101 0011 0011。
funct7 = 0100000（sub）｜rs2 = 01001（9）｜rs1 = 01000（8）｜funct3 = 000｜rd = 01010（10）｜opcode = 0110011。
組語寫成 `sub rd, rs1, rs2` → `sub x10, x8, x9`。add 和 sub 只差 funct7 的第 30 位元。

@q cd1-12 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算
`beq` 在 0x00400020，立即值是 $-3$。跳到哪？
== 0x00400018
== 00400018
---
目標＝PC＋4＋imm×4＝0x00400024 − 12。負的立即值往回跳，迴圈常用。

@q cd1-13 | co-datapath#1 | 3 | 計算
`j` 在 0x10000008，26 位元欄位是 0x0000200。目標位址？
== 0x10000800
== 10000800
---
(PC＋4) 的最高 4 位元 0001，接上欄位左移 2 位：0x0000200×4＝0x800。`j` 只能跳到同一個 256 MB 區塊內。

@q cd1-14 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 比較
MIPS 為什麼把所有指令都固定成 32 位元、而且格式這麼少？
[x] 解碼簡單、欄位位置固定，方便硬體同時處理好幾條指令的不同步驟；代價是程式碼比較長、立即值範圍受限
[ ] 為了讓程式比較短
[ ] 因為記憶體很便宜
[ ] 為了相容 x86
---
x86 指令長度 1～15 位元組，解碼複雜，現代 x86 內部會先轉成類似 RISC 的微指令。

@q cd1-15 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 情境
A RISC-V program must load 32-bit constants into registers. On RV32I, `lui rd, imm` puts its 20-bit immediate into bits 31 to 12 of `rd` and clears the lower 12 bits, while `addi rd, rs1, imm` adds a 12-bit immediate that is sign-extended, so it can only represent values from $-2048$ to 2047. A student first loads `0x12345678` with the pair below and gets the right result:
```
lui  x5, 0x12345
addi x5, x5, 0x678
```
The student then tries to load `0x12345FFF` the same way, writing `addi x5, x5, 0xFFF` after the same `lui`, and finds that x5 ends up holding `0x12344FFF` instead. The student also wonders whether a single `addi` from `x0` could load a full 32-bit constant, and what the assembler does with the pseudo-instruction `li`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] `lui x5, 0x12345` followed by `addi x5, x5, 0x678` leaves `0x12345678` in x5.
[x] To leave `0x12345FFF` in x5, one can use `lui x5, 0x12346` followed by `addi x5, x5, -1`.
[ ] `lui x5, 0x12345` followed by `addi x5, x5, 0xFFF` leaves `0x12345FFF` in x5.
[ ] `addi x5, x0, 0x12345678` is a single real machine instruction that loads the constant, since the assembler widens the immediate.
[x] `li x5, 0x12345678` is a pseudo-instruction that the assembler expands.
---
「`lui 0x12345`＋`addi 0x678`」對：`lui` 後 x5＝`0x12345000`，`0x678` 最高位是 0，符號延伸後還是正的，相加得 `0x12345678`。

「`lui 0x12346`＋`addi -1`」對：`0x12346000`$-1$＝`0x12345FFF`。

「`lui 0x12345`＋`addi 0xFFF`」錯：12 位元的 `0xFFF` 會被符號延伸成 $-1$，結果是 `0x12345000`$-1$＝`0x12344FFF`。所以低 12 位元的最高位是 1（≥ `0x800`）時，`lui` 的值要先加 1。這是 MIPS 用 `ori`（零延伸）不會遇到的陷阱。

「`addi` 載入 32 位元常數」錯：`addi` 的立即值只有 12 位元。

「`li` 是虛擬指令」對：組譯器會依常數大小展開成 `addi` 一條，或 `lui`＋`addi` 兩條。

@q cd1-16 | co-datapath#1 | 3 | 陷阱
用 `ori` 放低 16 位時，為什麼不用 `addi`？
[x] `addi` 會把立即值符號延伸；低 16 位最高位是 1 時（例如 0x8000）會把高半部減 1
[ ] `addi` 不能用立即值
[ ] `ori` 比較快
[ ] 兩者完全一樣
---
`ori` 的立即值是補 0（無號延伸），正好不影響高 16 位。

@q cd1-17 | co-datapath#1 | 1 | 計算
MIPS 有幾個通用暫存器？暫存器欄位要幾位元？
== 32,5
?? 暫存器數,欄位位元
---
$2^5=32$。`$zero` 永遠是 0，寫入無效。

@q cd1-18 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 找錯
A student encodes the RV32I instruction `sw x8, 4(x17)` (S-type, opcode `0100011`, funct3 `010`) by putting 17 in the rs2 bits and 8 in the rs1 bits. The S-type layout from bit 31 down to bit 0 is imm[11:5] | rs2 | rs1 | funct3 | imm[4:0] | opcode. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The rs1 bits should hold 17, because x17 is the base address register.
[x] The imm[4:0] bits should be `00100` and the imm[11:5] bits should be `0000000`.
[x] The correct machine code is `0x0088A223`.
[ ] Like `lw`, `sw` keeps its 12-bit immediate contiguously in bits 31 to 20, so the correct machine code is `0x0048A423`.
[ ] Since `sw` writes to memory, its bits 11 to 7 are rd and hold 8.
---
「rs1 應該是 17」對：位址＝rs1＋imm，基底 x17 放 rs1；要存的資料 x8 放 rs2。學生剛好填反。

「imm[4:0]＝00100、imm[11:5]＝0000000」對：4 的 12 位元是 000000000100，拆成高 7、低 5。

「`0x0088A223`」對：0000000｜01000（8）｜10001（17）｜010｜00100｜0100011 = 0000 0000 1000 1000 1010 0010 0010 0011。

「跟 lw 一樣連續放、`0x0048A423`」錯：`sw` 沒有目的暫存器，S 型把立即值拆開，讓 rs2 能放在跟 R 型一樣的位置；照 I 型排會把 8 塞進 rd 的位置，硬體會讀錯資料暫存器。

「第 11～7 位元是 rd、放 8」錯：S 型那 5 個位元是 imm[4:0]，`sw` 不寫任何暫存器。

@q cd1-19 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算
Encode the RV32I instruction `slli x10, x9, 2`. It uses the I-type layout with opcode `0010011` and funct3 `001`; the upper 7 bits of the 12-bit immediate are `0000000`, and the lower 5 bits hold the shift amount. Give the 32-bit machine code in hexadecimal.
== 0x00249513
== 00249513
---
imm[11:5] 0000000｜shamt 00010｜rs1 01001（9）｜funct3 001｜rd 01010（10）｜opcode 0010011。
連起來 0000 0000 0010 0100 1001 0101 0001 0011 = `0x00249513`。MIPS 的 `sll` 是 R 型、用 shamt 欄；RISC-V 的 `slli` 是 I 型，位移量放在立即值的低 5 位元。

@q cd2-07 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
單週期資料路徑中，R 型指令的 RegDst、ALUSrc、MemtoReg、RegWrite、MemWrite 各是？
== 1,0,0,1,0
---
寫入的暫存器是 rd（RegDst=1）；第二個運算元是暫存器（ALUSrc=0）；寫回 ALU 結果（MemtoReg=0）；要寫暫存器、不寫記憶體。

@q cd2-08 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
`beq` 的 RegWrite、MemWrite、Branch、ALUSrc 各是？
== 0,0,1,0
---
不寫任何東西；比較兩個暫存器（ALU 做減法看是否為 0），由 Branch 和 Zero 一起決定要不要跳。

@q cd2-09 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 陷阱
In the single-cycle MIPS datapath, the multiplexer controlled by MemtoReg chooses the value written back to the register file: the ALU result when MemtoReg is 0, and the data memory output when it is 1. A manufacturing defect leaves MemtoReg stuck at 1, so the value written back is always the data memory output. A test program runs `add`, `addi`, `lw`, `sw`, and `beq`, 20 instructions of each kind, and a technician must predict which kinds of instructions still produce correct results, before the chip is sent back to the factory. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] R-type instructions write the memory output instead of the ALU result.
[x] addi writes the wrong value as well.
[x] lw still works correctly.
[ ] sw and beq stop working.
[ ] Every instruction stops working.
---
逐類指令看「它本來要這個訊號是多少、會不會用到」。

「R 型寫回記憶體的值」對：它本來要 MemtoReg＝0 選 ALU 結果。

「addi 也壞」對：同樣要寫回 ALU 結果。

「lw 正常」對：它本來就是 1。

「sw、beq 壞掉」錯：它們不寫暫存器（RegWrite＝0），寫回選哪一路都沒差。

「全部都壞」錯：見上。

@q cd2-10 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 陷阱
In the single-cycle MIPS datapath, the multiplexer controlled by RegDst chooses the destination register number: the rt bits (20–16) of the instruction when RegDst is 0, and the rd bits (15–11) when it is 1. A defect leaves RegDst stuck at 0, so the destination is always taken from rt. A test program runs `add`, `lw`, `sw`, `beq`, and `addi`, 20 instructions of each kind, and a technician must predict which kinds of instructions still produce correct results, before the chip is sent back to the factory. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] R-type instructions write their result into rt instead of rd.
[ ] lw stops working.
[x] sw and beq are unaffected, since they do not write any register.
[x] addi is unaffected.
[ ] Every instruction stops working.
---
「R 型寫進 rt」對：R 型的目的暫存器在 rd。

「lw 壞」錯：lw 本來就寫 rt。

「sw、beq 不受影響」對：RegWrite＝0，RegDst 是什麼都沒差（don't care）。

「addi 不受影響」對：I 型也寫 rt。

「全部都壞」錯。

@q cd2-11 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
單週期：取指 250 ps、讀暫存器 150、ALU 200、記憶體 300、寫回 100。只有 R 型和 lw 時，時脈週期最小是多少？
== 1000
---
最長的是 lw：$250+150+200+300+100=1000$ ps。R 型只要 700，但單週期每條都要等最慢的。

@q cd2-12 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 觀念
單週期資料路徑為什麼要分開的指令記憶體和資料記憶體？
[x] lw／sw 在同一個週期內既要取指令又要存取資料，一個記憶體不能同時做兩件事
[ ] 為了安全
[ ] 為了省錢
[ ] 不需要分開
---
多週期可以共用一個記憶體，因為取指和存取資料在不同週期。

@q cd2-13 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 找錯
A student fills in the control table of the single-cycle MIPS datapath for the 6 instructions `add`, `sub`, `lw`, `sw`, `beq`, and `addi`, writing 0 or 1 for each of RegDst, ALUSrc, MemtoReg, RegWrite, MemRead, MemWrite, and Branch. For `sw`, the student sets RegDst = 1 and MemtoReg = 0, and a classmate marks both entries as wrong. The teaching assistant asks which entries in the row of `sw` actually matter, and why a hardware designer would prefer to mark some entries as don't-cares when building the control logic from the table. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For sw, RegWrite = 0, so RegDst is a don't-care.
[x] Setting RegDst = 1 for sw is therefore not wrong.
[ ] sw writes its result into rd.
[x] Treating such entries as don't-cares lets the control logic be simplified.
[ ] For sw, MemtoReg must be 1, because sw is the instruction that accesses the data memory.
---
「sw 的 RegDst 是 don't care」對：根本不寫暫存器，常寫 X。

「設 1 不算錯」對：只是沒意義。

「sw 寫 rd」錯：sw 寫的是記憶體，不寫暫存器。

「don't care 讓邏輯化簡」對：可以挑最方便的值。

「MemtoReg 一定要 1」錯：同樣是 don't care。

@q cd2-14 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 情境
We want to add `jal rd, imm` to a single-cycle RISC-V datapath that already supports R-type instructions, `lw`, `sw`, and `beq`. The new instruction writes PC + 4 into `rd` and then jumps to PC + imm, where the 20-bit immediate is scattered over the instruction bits in the J-type layout and is shifted left by 1. In the existing datapath, a multiplexer chooses whether the register file receives the ALU result or the data memory output, and a separate adder computes PC + imm for taken branches. A student lists the changes that `jal` requires and estimates which existing units can be reused, for example in a test where `jal x1, 0x40` sits at address `0x100`, so that it must write `0x104` into x1 and jump to `0x140` in a single cycle. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The multiplexer feeding the register file's write data needs an extra input carrying PC + 4.
[x] The jump target PC + imm can be computed by the same adder that already computes branch targets.
[x] The unit that builds the immediate from the instruction bits must also handle the J-type bit layout.
[ ] `jal` must read rs1 from the register file to compute its target.
[ ] `jal` needs to access the data memory, because the return address must be saved there before jumping.
---
加新指令的思路：它要讀什麼、算什麼、寫到哪。

「寫回多工器多一個 PC＋4」對：原本寫回的資料只有 ALU 結果或記憶體讀出值，`jal` 要寫的是 $PC+4$。（RISC-V 的 rd 欄位置固定，不像 MIPS 的 `jal` 要多一個固定寫入 31 號暫存器的輸入。）

「目標用分支的加法器算」對：分支目標也是 PC＋立即值，同一個加法器，只是 PC 來源多工器的選擇條件要加上「是 `jal`」。

「立即值產生器要處理 J 型」對：J 型的 20 位元立即值在指令裡的排法跟分支用的格式不同，要另外接線。

「要讀 rs1」錯：那是 `jalr`；`jal` 的目標只看 PC 和立即值。

「要存取資料記憶體」錯：`jal` 不讀也不寫記憶體。

@q cd2-15 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
A teaching processor implements the following RISC-V RV64I instructions with a single-cycle datapath. Its ALUSrc selector chooses either the second register value, when zero, or a correctly decoded signed immediate, when one. The immediate is twelve bits for these arithmetic, load and store instructions; an S-type store immediate is assembled from its two encoded parts before sign extension. Memory and register write controls are separate from ALUSrc.

Start each experiment from the same state: register x6 contains 4096, x7 contains 24, and the eight-byte memory value at address 4120 is 99. Execute one of `addi x8,x6,-16`, `ld x5,24(x6)`, `sd x7,24(x6)`, or `add x9,x6,x7`. Assume naturally aligned addresses and no faults. The arithmetic instruction does not implicitly read data memory, and the store does not write a destination register. Use the instruction semantics and selector definition to distinguish an effective address from the data subsequently read or written. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The add-immediate result written to x8 is 4080.
[x] The load computes effective address 4120 before retrieving its data.
[x] The store uses ALUSrc equal to one with the assembled S-type immediate.
[x] The register-register add uses ALUSrc equal to zero.
[ ] Setting ALUSrc to one by itself selects the value read from data memory as the register writeback value, regardless of the instruction.
---
「加立即值4080」對：4096−16＝4080，十二位負立即值須符號延伸。「載入位址4120」對：4096＋24，不可把讀出的99當成位址。「儲存選立即值」對：S型先組合立即值，再與基底相加。「暫存器加選零」對：第二輸入是x7的24。「ALUSrc決定記憶體寫回」錯：它只選ALU來源，資料記憶體與寫回選擇另有控制，不因選一就自動載入。

@q cd2-16 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 計算
單週期：指令 30% lw、10% sw、50% R 型、10% beq。各類延遲 lw 800、sw 700、R 600、beq 500 ps。若能讓每類用自己的時間（理想的可變時脈），平均每條快幾倍？
== 1.21
---
可變：$0.3\times800+0.1\times700+0.5\times600+0.1\times500=660$ ps；固定 800。$800/660\approx1.21$。實務上做不到可變時脈，這是多週期的動機。

@q cd2-17 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 觀念
A simplified RISC-V RV32I processor separates main decoding from ALU decoding. The main decoder sends ALUOp zero for effective-address addition, one for branch comparison by subtraction, and two for register arithmetic whose operation is selected using funct3 and funct7. This is an explicitly defined teaching convention; the ALUOp numbers are not additional bits required by the ISA.

| Instruction | opcode | funct3 | funct7 |
|---|---|---|---|
| add x5,x6,x7 | 0x33 | 0 | 0x00 |
| sub x5,x6,x7 | 0x33 | 0 | 0x20 |

Registers x6 and x7 contain nine and four for the arithmetic experiments. In a separate load experiment, the base register contains 1000 and the signed displacement is positive twelve. Register identifiers choose values to read, whereas operation bits tell the ALU what calculation to perform. The Zero output is a result of that calculation, not a replacement for decoding its operation. Evaluate the stated control organization and numerical load address. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With the displayed common opcode and funct3, funct7 distinguishes addition from subtraction.
[x] The source-register identifiers alone do not determine whether the ALU adds or subtracts.
[ ] The stated branch-comparison convention selects addition instead of subtraction.
[ ] The load experiment produces address 988 because a positive displacement must be subtracted from the base.
[ ] The ALU cannot choose its operation until its own Zero result has first been used as the sole operation-decoding input.
---
「funct7區分加減」對：表中opcode與funct3相同，funct7分別零與0x20。「來源編號不決定運算」對：讀誰與對讀到的值做何運算是不同控制。「分支選加法」錯：本模型明定ALUOp一做減法比較。「載入988」錯：1000＋12＝1012，正位移不是減十二。「先靠自身Zero才決定運算」錯：先解碼ALU操作，Zero才是計算出的結果；不能用它取代操作解碼。

@q cd3-07 | co-datapath#3 | 3 | 陷阱
A multicycle teaching implementation of RISC-V has five kinds of step. Their combinational delays are 200, 150, 250, 300 and 100 ps. Every clock period must also include twenty ps of register overhead, and all steps share one clock. A load uses five cycles and a branch uses three. These counts describe this implementation, rather than fixed timing requirements of the RISC-V instruction set.

An engineer divides the 300-ps memory step into two successive 150-ps steps. A load must now visit both memory steps and therefore uses six cycles; the branch uses neither memory step and still takes three cycles. The other step delays and register overhead remain unchanged. Each instruction runs by itself, so no instruction overlap or queueing is being counted. Compare the minimum safe periods and the complete load latencies before and after the change. A shorter clock period does not by itself specify how many periods an instruction needs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The original minimum safe clock period is 320 ps.
[x] The original load latency is 1600 ps.
[x] The redesigned minimum safe clock period is 270 ps.
[x] The redesigned six-cycle load takes 1620 ps.
[ ] Dividing the slowest step necessarily reduces every instruction's latency even when that instruction must execute an additional cycle.
---
「原週期320」對：最大邏輯300加暫存器20。「原載入1600」對：五拍乘320。「新週期270」對：拆完仍有250的其他步，故250＋20，不是150＋20。「新載入1620」對：六乘270，反而比1600多20。「短週期保證每指令更快」錯：還要乘指令拍數；分支三拍變810 ps較快，但載入多一拍稍慢，不能只看時脈。

@q cd3-08 | co-datapath#3 | 2 | 觀念
A multicycle RISC-V teaching machine retains the address of the current instruction in a saved-PC register. For the branch in this experiment that address is 0x100. Fetch reads the instruction and advances the next sequential PC to 0x104. During decode, the single shared ALU can form a target by adding the saved instruction address to a decoded signed byte displacement, and the result is retained for the following cycle.

The instruction is `beq x5,x6,16`, where sixteen denotes the actual byte displacement rather than an encoded value that still needs another scale factor. Both source registers contain seven. The machine uses cycle one for fetch, cycle two for decode and target preparation, and cycle three for register comparison and branch completion. A register arithmetic instruction has a fourth writeback cycle; a load additionally visits data memory and uses five cycles. There are no compressed instructions or architectural delay slots in this experiment. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The specified branch completes after three cycles.
[x] The taken target is address 0x110.
[ ] The branch must write its comparison result into a destination register during a fourth writeback cycle.
[ ] The taken target is 0x114 because the already advanced sequential PC must be used as the RISC-V branch base.
[ ] Completing one branch in three cycles means this machine fetches three new instructions in each single cycle.
---
「三拍」對：依題設fetch、decode、比較後完成。「目標0x110」對：分支自身0x100加十六bytes。「需第四拍寫目的暫存器」錯：條件分支只更新控制流程，不需要這種寫回。「目標0x114」錯：混用已前進的PC；RISC-V分支以原分支位址為基底。「三拍完成代表每拍取三條」錯：延遲與吞吐不同，此處也沒有流水重疊。

@q cd3-12 | co-datapath#3 | 2 | 找錯
A multicycle RISC-V implementation executes exactly one billion retired instructions with a 200-ps clock period. Loads use five cycles, stores use four, register arithmetic uses four, and conditional branches use three. There are no additional misses, exceptions or stalls, and no instructions overlap. Treat the following percentages as exact dynamic instruction counts, not as probabilities requiring an extra variance estimate.

| Class | Original mix | Revised mix |
|---|---|---|
| lw | 25 percent | 25 percent |
| sw | 10 percent | 10 percent |
| register arithmetic | 45 percent | 25 percent |
| beq | 20 percent | 40 percent |

The revised program still retires one billion instructions. Only the mix changes; neither the clock nor any class's cycle count changes. A timing report incorrectly charges every instruction the load's five cycles. Another report uses a weighted average over the four classes. Determine the original average and elapsed time, then the revised average and the difference in elapsed time. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The original average CPI is 4.05.
[x] The original elapsed time is 0.81 seconds.
[x] The revised average CPI is 3.85.
[x] The revised mix saves 0.04 seconds at the stated instruction count and clock.
[ ] The original elapsed time must be one second because every instruction is charged the five-cycle load latency.
---
「原CPI4.05」對：0.25×5＋0.10×4＋0.45×4＋0.20×3＝4.05。「原0.81秒」對：10⁹×4.05×200 ps。「新CPI3.85」對：1.25＋0.4＋1＋1.2＝3.85。「省0.04秒」對：新時間0.77秒，0.81−0.77＝0.04。「全部乘五得到一秒才對」錯：多週期各類拍數不同，不能把最慢類別套到每條指令。

@q cd3-13 | co-datapath#3 | 1 | 觀念
A RISC-V multicycle processor uses a control state number in addition to the current instruction's opcode. Each state occupies one clock cycle. The next fetch belongs to the following instruction and is not counted again as part of the instruction that has just completed. The following state paths are part of the implementation specification.

| Instruction | States before returning to fetch state zero |
|---|---|
| load | 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 |
| store | 0, 1, 2, 5 |
| register add | 0, 1, 6, 7 |
| conditional branch | 0, 1, 8 |

State zero fetches the instruction, state one decodes it, and state two forms a data address. Register writeback is enabled only in states four and seven. A memory write is enabled only in state five. Memory reads occur during instruction fetch and the load's data-read state three. All other unspecified write controls are disabled. Evaluate whether the opcode alone can replace this stored sequencing state while preserving these control actions. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The load path occupies five cycles before the next instruction begins.
[x] Loads and stores share address-generation state two.
[x] The stated register-write control is enabled only in states four and seven.
[x] Instruction fetch and load data retrieval use distinct states even though both read memory.
[ ] A state-free function of the unchanged load opcode must automatically produce different read and write controls on its successive cycles without retaining any sequencing information.
---
「載入五拍」對：零至四共五個狀態，返回零已是下一條。「共用狀態二算位址」對：load、store兩路皆經它。「僅四與七寫暫存器」對：依指定控制表。「取指與取資料分不同狀態」對：分別零與三，支援同一硬體在不同拍重用。「只看不變opcode自動逐拍改控制」錯：相同輸入的無狀態組合函數不會自行記住已走到哪一拍，需保留狀態或微程式位置。

@q pl1-17 | co-pipeline#1 | 3 | 計算
某設計每條指令平均要 4 個週期（一次只處理一條）、時脈 200 ps；改成管線後 CPI 約 1.2、時脈 220 ps。管線快幾倍？
== 3.03
---
多週期 $4\times200=800$ ps／指令；管線 $1.2\times220=264$。$800/264\approx3.03$。管線讓不同指令的不同階段同時進行。

@q pl1-07 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 計算
6 級管線、沒有危障，跑 50 條指令要幾個週期？
== 55
---
第一條要 6 拍，之後每拍完成一條：$6+(50-1)$。

@q pl1-08 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 計算
不做管線時每條指令 1000 ps；切成 5 級，每級 200 ps 加 20 ps 暫存器。很多指令時加速約幾倍？
== 4.55
---
管線每拍 220 ps，穩定後每 220 ps 完成一條：$1000/220\approx4.55$。暫存器延遲讓加速達不到 5。

$5\times220=1100>1000$。管線提高的是產出量，單一指令反而稍微變慢。

@q pl1-10 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 觀念

[ ] 資料危障

@q pl1-11 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 計算
5 級管線，CPI 1.4（平均每條停 0.4 拍），時脈 250 ps。$10^9$ 條指令要多久（秒）？
== 0.35
---
