@q pl1-12 | co-pipeline#1 | 3 | 計算
把 5 級管線改成 10 級，每級時間減半，但每級暫存器延遲固定 20 ps、停頓拍數變成 2 倍（CPI 從 1.2 變 1.4）。原本每級 200 ps。新舊每條指令平均時間？
== 264,168
?? 舊,新
---
舊：$1.2\times(200+20)=264$ ps；新：$1.4\times(100+20)=168$ ps。管線越深，暫存器延遲和停頓的比例越大，到某個深度後就不划算。

@q pl1-13 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「$k$ 級管線一定快 $k$ 倍。」漏了什麼？
[x] 各級延遲不平均（以最慢的為準）、級間暫存器的額外延遲、危障造成的停頓、管線填滿的時間
[ ] 沒漏
[ ] 只漏了危障
[ ] 其實會快 $2k$ 倍
---
$k$ 倍是理想上限：要各級完全平均、沒有暫存器延遲、沒有任何停頓，而且指令數遠大於 $k$。

@q pl1-14 | co-pipeline#1 | 1 | 觀念
管線中的「暫存器」（例如 IF/ID）用途？
[x] 保存每一級的結果和控制訊號，讓下一級在下一拍使用，各級互不干擾
[ ] 存放程式的變數
[ ] 加快 ALU
[ ] 存放 cache 資料
---
沒有它們的話，前一級一換指令，後一級的輸入就跟著變了。

@q pl1-15 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 情境
洗衣店有洗衣機（30 分）、烘衣機（40 分）、摺衣服（20 分）各一台。洗 4 批衣服，用管線方式最少要多久？
== 210
---
瓶頸是烘衣 40 分：第一批 $30+40+20=90$，之後每 40 分出一批：$90+3\times40=210$。

@q pl1-16 | co-pipeline#1 | 3 | 證明
說明 $k$ 級管線跑 $n$ 條指令（無危障、每級 1 拍）需要 $k+n-1$ 拍。
---
第 1 條指令要經過 $k$ 級，在第 $k$ 拍結束時完成。之後每拍都有一條新指令進入、一條完成，第 $i$ 條在第 $k+i-1$ 拍完成。所以第 $n$ 條在第 $k+n-1$ 拍完成。

@q pl2-07 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 追蹤
有 forwarding。`lw $t0,0($a0)`、`lw $t1,4($a0)`、`add $t2,$t0,$t1`。要停幾拍？
== 1
---
add 用到 `$t1`，它是上一條 lw 的結果：load-use 停 1 拍。`$t0` 隔了一條，可以從 MEM/WB 轉送，不用停。

@q pl2-08 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 計算
有 forwarding。程式：`lw $t0,0($s0)`、`add $t1,$t0,$t2`、`lw $t3,4($s0)`、`sub $t4,$t3,$t1`、`sw $t4,8($s0)`。總共停幾拍？調換順序最少能變成幾拍？
== 2,0
?? 原本,調換後
---
兩個 load-use（lw→add、lw→sub）各停 1 拍。把第二個 lw 移到 add 前面：lw t0、lw t3、add、sub、sw，兩個 lw 都跟使用者隔了一條，不用停。

@q pl2-09 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 觀念
EX/MEM 的轉送條件（轉給下一條指令的 rs）是？
[x] EX/MEM.RegWrite 而且 EX/MEM.Rd ≠ 0 而且 EX/MEM.Rd ＝ ID/EX.Rs
[ ] 只要 Rd ＝ Rs
[ ] EX/MEM.MemRead
[ ] Rs ＝ 0
---
Rd ≠ 0：`$zero` 不能被「轉送」成非 0 的值。

@q pl2-10 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 陷阱
連續三條：`add $t0,…`、`sub $t0,…`、`or $t1,$t0,$t2`。or 要的 `$t0` 從哪裡轉送？
[x] 從 EX/MEM（sub 的結果），比 MEM/WB 的 add 結果優先
[ ] 從 MEM/WB（add 的結果）
[ ] 兩個都要
[ ] 從暫存器檔
---
最近的那一筆才是正確的值。轉送單元要先檢查 EX/MEM，沒有才看 MEM/WB。

@q pl2-11 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
沒有 forwarding，但暫存器檔「前半拍寫、後半拍讀」。`add $t0,…` 緊接著 `sub $t1,$t0,…`，要停幾拍？
== 2
---
add 在第 5 拍 WB 寫入，sub 的 ID 要在第 5 拍（同一拍後半）讀：sub 本來第 3 拍 ID，要延後 2 拍。

@q pl2-12 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
有 forwarding。`lw $t0,0($s0)` 隔一條無關指令後才 `add $t1,$t0,$t2`。要停幾拍？
== 0
---
lw 在 MEM 結束時有資料，add 的 EX 晚兩拍，可以從 MEM/WB 轉送。

@q pl2-13 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 情境
A compiler translates the two C statements below for a five-stage pipeline with full forwarding. On this machine, a `lw` followed immediately by an instruction that uses its result costs exactly one stall cycle, and every other dependence is covered by forwarding.
```
a = b + c;
d = e + f;
```
In program order, each statement becomes `lw`, `lw`, `add`, `sw`: the two loads bring the operands into registers, `add` computes the sum, and `sw` stores it, so eight instructions run in total. Assume there are no cache misses and no branches, and that the compiler may reorder instructions only if every value is still produced before it is used. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The original order causes 2 load-use stalls.
[x] Loading all four values first, then doing add, sw, add, sw, causes no stall.
[ ] Inserting a nop after every lw instead gives the same running time as the reordered code.
[x] The reordered code needs more registers holding values at the same time.
[ ] Moving both sw instructions to the very beginning is a legal reordering.
---
「原本 2 次」對：每個 add 都緊接在它要用的第二個 lw 後面。

「四個 lw 先做」對：每個 lw 和用到它的指令中間至少隔一條。

「加 nop 一樣快」錯：nop 也能避免危障，但每個 nop 浪費一拍，共多 4 拍。

「需要更多暫存器」對：四個載入的值同時活著。

「sw 移到最前面」錯：sw 要存的是 add 的結果，不能比 add 早。

@q pl2-14 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 找錯
In the textbook five-stage MIPS pipeline, a `lw` writes its result into register Rt, while an R-type instruction reads its two source registers Rs and Rt; in both formats, Rs and Rt are the same bit positions of the instruction. A student's hazard detection unit, which sits in ID, uses this condition:
```
if (ID/EX.MemRead and ID/EX.Rt == IF/ID.Rs)
    stall one cycle
```
When it stalls, the unit freezes PC and IF/ID and sets the control signals entering ID/EX to 0; forwarding from MEM/WB then supplies the loaded value one cycle later. The student tests the unit only on `lw $t0,0($s0)` followed by `add $t1,$t0,$t2`, sees the correct result, and concludes that the unit is complete. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The unit must also stall when ID/EX.MemRead is true and ID/EX.Rt equals IF/ID.Rt.
[ ] The unit should compare ID/EX.Rd instead of ID/EX.Rt.
[x] With only the student's condition, `add $t1,$t2,$t0` right after `lw $t0,0($s0)` would use a wrong value of `$t0`.
[ ] The unit should check MemWrite instead of MemRead.
[x] When the full condition holds, the pipeline stalls one cycle, and forwarding then supplies the loaded value.
---
「也要比 Rt」對：下一條的兩個來源暫存器都可能用到剛 load 的值。

「改比 Rd」錯：lw 的目的暫存器在 Rt 欄位。

「`add $t1,$t2,$t0` 會拿到錯的值」對：這條的第二個來源才是 `$t0`，學生的條件抓不到，不停的話資料來不及轉送。

「改看 MemWrite」錯：要找的是「正在讀記憶體」的 lw。

「停一拍後靠轉送」對：停一拍後 lw 在 WB，可以從 MEM/WB 轉送給 EX。

@q pl2-15 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 陷阱
In the five-stage MIPS pipeline with the standard forwarding paths into EX, `lw $t0,0($s1)` is immediately followed by `sw $t0,0($s2)`, which copies the loaded word to another address. Without any stall, the timing would be:

| cycle | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `lw` | IF | ID | EX | MEM | WB | |
| `sw` | | IF | ID | EX | MEM | WB |

The loaded word is available only at the end of cycle 4, so with the standard paths the pair needs one stall. A designer considers adding one more forwarding path, from the MEM/WB register to the store-data input of the data memory, and asks which instruction pairs it would help. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Adding a forwarding path from the MEM/WB register to the store-data input of the following sw's MEM stage removes the stall.
[x] The sw needs the value it stores only in its MEM stage.
[ ] Even with the extra path, two stall cycles are needed.
[x] If the instruction after the lw were `add $t1,$t0,$t2`, one stall cycle would still be needed even with every forwarding path.
[ ] If the sw instead used the loaded register as its base address, `sw $t3,0($t0)`, the new path would also remove the stall.
---
「加一條 MEM 轉送就不用停」對：lw 的資料在它的 MEM 結束時有，下一拍 sw 正好在 MEM 要寫入。只有標準的 EX 前轉送路徑時要停 1 拍。

「sw 在 MEM 才需要資料」對：要存的資料在 MEM 才寫進記憶體。

「還要停 2 拍」錯。

「換成 add 還是要停 1 拍」對：add 在 EX 就要用 `$t0`，比 lw 拿到資料早一拍，這是 load-use 危障。

「拿來當位址也能省」錯：位址在 EX 就要算，新的路徑來不及。

@q pl2-16 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 觀念
A five-stage pipeline runs the code below, with `lw` entering IF in cycle 1.
```
lw   $t0, 0($s1)
add  $t2, $t0, $t3
sub  $t4, $t5, $t6
```
In cycle 3, `lw` is in EX and `add` is in ID; `add` needs `$t0` in EX in cycle 4, but the loaded word exists only at the end of cycle 4, so the hazard detection unit in ID stalls for one cycle. During the stall, `lw` must keep going, `add` and `sub` must be held back without losing either of them, and nothing the stalled `add` might do may change the register file or memory. Which of the following statements about what the hardware does are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The PC is not updated.
[x] The IF/ID register is not updated, so the same instruction stays in ID.
[x] The control signals written into ID/EX are set to 0, inserting a bubble.
[ ] The whole pipeline is flushed.
[ ] The next instruction is skipped.
---
「PC 不更新」對：取指令的那條也留在原地。

「IF/ID 不更新」對：等著用 load 結果的那條留在 ID 再解碼一次。

「ID/EX 控制訊號清成 0」對：bubble 往後流，就像一條什麼都不做的指令。

「整條管線清空」錯：只停一拍，前面已經在 EX、MEM 的指令照常往前走。

「跳過下一條」錯：沒有任何指令被丟掉，只是晚一拍。

@q pl2-17 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
程式有 $10^6$ 條指令，其中 25% 是 lw，lw 中 40% 後面緊接著就用到它的結果。有 forwarding 時平均 CPI？
== 1.1
---
$1+0.25\times0.4\times1$。

@q pl2-18 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼有 forwarding 時，lw 後面緊接著使用它還是要停 1 拍，但 add 後面緊接著使用不用停。
---
add 的結果在 EX 階段結束時就有了，下一條在下一拍的 EX 開始時需要，時間上剛好接得上（EX/MEM → EX）。lw 的資料要到 MEM 階段結束才從記憶體讀出來，下一條的 EX 跟 lw 的 MEM 在同一拍，要在那一拍開始就用到還不存在的資料，時間上不可能，只能晚一拍再從 MEM/WB 轉送。

@q pl3-07 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 計算
迴圈每次跑 5 圈（前 4 次分支成立、第 5 次不成立），整個迴圈被執行 4 次。1-bit 預測器一開始預測「不成立」，總共錯幾次？
== 8
---
第一次進迴圈：第 1 次分支錯（預測不成立）；離開時錯；之後每次重新進入時第 1 次又錯（上次記住不成立），離開又錯。共 $1+1+3\times2=8$。程式模擬驗算。

@q pl3-08 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 計算
同上，換成 2-bit 飽和計數器，一開始「強不成立」，錯幾次？一開始「弱成立」呢？
== 6,4
?? 強不成立,弱成立
---
程式模擬。2-bit 在離開迴圈時只錯一次、不會因為一次不成立就翻轉預測，所以重新進入時不再錯。從強不成立出發要先錯兩次才轉成「成立」。

@q pl3-09 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 計算
分支模式「成立、成立、不成立」重複 4 次（共 12 次）。1-bit（初始不成立）和 2-bit（初始弱不成立）各錯幾次？
== 8,5
?? 1-bit,2-bit
---
程式模擬。這種固定模式用「看最近幾次結果」的相關預測器可以做得更好。

@q pl3-10 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 計算
分支 20%、預測正確率 90%、預測錯誤懲罰 3 拍。CPI（理想 1）？
== 1.06
---
$1+0.2\times0.1\times3$。

@q pl3-11 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 比較
In design 1, branches are resolved in EX with a 2-cycle misprediction penalty; in design 2, they are resolved in ID with a 1-cycle penalty, but the clock is 5% slower. Both predict correctly 85% of the time, branches are 20% of instructions, and the base CPI is 1. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The CPI of design 1 is 1.06.
[x] The CPI of design 2 is 1.03.
[x] Design 1 is faster: its time per instruction is proportional to 1.06, versus 1.0815 for design 2.
[ ] Design 2 is faster, because its CPI is lower.
[ ] If the prediction accuracy dropped to 50% in both designs, design 1 would still be faster.
---
「設計一 CPI 1.06」對：$1+0.2\times0.15\times2$。

「設計二 CPI 1.03」對：$1+0.2\times0.15\times1$。

「設計一比較快」對：設計二 $1.03\times1.05=1.0815>1.06$。提早決定要付出時脈的代價，不一定划算。

「CPI 低就比較快」錯：要乘上時脈週期。

「正確率 50% 時設計一還是比較快」錯：設計一 $1+0.2\times0.5\times2=1.2$，設計二 $(1+0.1)\times1.05=1.155$，換成設計二快。猜錯越多，懲罰少的設計越划算。

@q pl3-12 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 觀念
A processor fetches one instruction per cycle and resolves branches in its third stage. To avoid wasting cycles, it adds a branch target buffer (BTB) with 512 entries that is looked up with the PC during IF, in parallel with the instruction cache. In a loop, the branch at address 0x400120 jumps back to 0x400100 on every iteration except the last, and a jump at 0x400200 always goes to 0x400380. The designers want to know what the buffer stores, when it helps, and what it does not do. Which of the following statements about a branch target buffer (BTB) are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It maps the address of a branch instruction to the target address that branch went to last time.
[x] It lets the IF stage fetch from the predicted target in the next cycle, before the branch is decoded.
[ ] It stores the register values that the branch condition compares.
[x] Predicting whether a branch is taken and predicting where it goes are separate problems.
[ ] The buffer is useful only for unconditional jumps.
---
「分支位址 → 上次的目標」對。

「IF 就知道從哪取」對：不用等解碼算出目標。

「存比較用的暫存器值」錯：BTB 只存位址，不存資料。

「方向和目標是兩件事」對：方向靠 1-bit、2-bit 預測器，目標靠 BTB。

「只對無條件跳躍有用」錯：條件分支預測要跳時一樣需要目標。

@q pl3-13 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 找錯
Two predictors are compared on a single branch: a 1-bit predictor that predicts the outcome seen last time, and a 2-bit saturating counter with states 0 and 1 (predict not taken) and 2 and 3 (predict taken), which moves up by 1 after a taken branch and down by 1 after a not-taken branch, staying within 0 to 3. The states 1 and 2 are called weak, and the states 0 and 3 strong. The test uses two outcome patterns, where T means taken and N means not taken:

| pattern | outcomes |
|---|---|
| alternating | T N T N T N … |
| loop | T T T T T T T T T N, repeated |

For the alternating pattern the counter starts at 1; for the loop pattern it starts at 3, and both predictors are run for many passes of each pattern. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For the alternating pattern, the counter mispredicts every time.
[x] For the alternating pattern, the 1-bit predictor mispredicts every time after the first.
[ ] On every possible branch pattern, a 2-bit predictor mispredicts no more often than a 1-bit predictor does, since it changes its mind more slowly.
[x] For the loop pattern, the counter mispredicts about once per pass and the 1-bit predictor about twice.
[ ] When in a strong state, the 2-bit predictor changes its prediction after a single misprediction.
---
「交替時 2-bit 從弱狀態開始每次都錯」對：猜錯一次就從弱狀態跳到另一邊的弱狀態，每次都跟實際相反（程式模擬確認）。

「1-bit 每次都錯」對：它總是猜「跟上次一樣」。

「2-bit 永遠不會比較差」錯：交替的例子裡兩者一樣差；也有特別構造的模式讓 2-bit 更差。

「迴圈：2-bit 每輪錯 1 次、1-bit 錯 2 次」對：1-bit 在離開迴圈時錯一次、下一輪進入時又錯一次；2-bit 離開時錯一次，但還停在「傾向跳」。這正是 2-bit 的優勢：大多數時候同方向、偶爾例外。

「強狀態錯一次就換」錯：強狀態錯一次只會變弱，還要再錯一次才換方向。

@q pl3-14 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 情境
The loop below sums the elements of a list `data` of $n = 32768$ random integers between $-128$ and 127, adding only the positive values:
```
for (i = 0; i < n; i++)
    if (data[i] > 0)
        sum += data[i];
```
On a processor with a 15-stage pipeline, the loop runs about 5 times as fast after `data` is sorted, even though sorting changes neither the set of values nor the number of iterations. The processor uses a 2-bit predictor for each branch, and a misprediction costs about 14 cycles. A student wants to explain the difference. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] After sorting, the predictor is almost always right.
[x] With random signs, the predictor is wrong about half of the time.
[ ] Sorting helps mainly because the loop then processes fewer elements, skipping the negative ones.
[x] Each misprediction flushes instructions, which costs many cycles in a deep pipeline.
[ ] Branch prediction has no effect on the running time.
---
「排序後幾乎都猜中」對：只在正負交界錯一兩次。

「隨機時約一半錯」對：沒有規律可學。

「排序讓元素變少」錯：元素一樣多。

「每次猜錯要清掉很多拍」對：現代處理器管線很深，預測錯一次要十幾拍。

「預測不影響時間」錯：這正是差很多的原因。

@q pl3-15 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 觀念
A classic MIPS pipeline resolves branches in ID and has one branch delay slot: the instruction right after a branch always executes. A compiler must schedule this code:
```
add  $t1, $t2, $t3     # independent of the branch
beq  $t4, $zero, L
nop                    # delay slot, not yet filled
```
On a newer design without delay slots, the same branch would instead cost a 1-cycle penalty whenever it is taken, and the designers compare the two approaches for deeper pipelines that resolve branches several stages later. Which of the following statements about delayed branches are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The instruction in the branch delay slot is executed whether or not the branch is taken.
[x] Here the compiler can move the `add` into the delay slot.
[ ] With a delayed branch, the processor postpones deciding the branch by one cycle.
[x] If no useful instruction can be found, the compiler puts a nop in the delay slot.
[ ] Like MIPS, RISC-V has a branch delay slot.
---
「delay slot 不管跳不跳都執行」對：這就是延遲分支的定義。

「編譯器從分支前面搬一條不相關的指令進去」對：這樣那一拍不浪費。

「分支延後一拍再決定」錯：決定的時間沒變，變的是「分支後面那一條一定會執行」的規則。

「找不到就放 nop」對：那一拍等於浪費掉，但程式還是對的。

「RISC-V 也有延遲槽」錯：RISC-V 沒有延遲槽。管線變深、多發射後一個延遲槽不夠用，現代 ISA 大多不用了。

@q pl3-16 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 計算
分支 25%，其中 70% 會跳。「預測會跳」需要目標位址，在 ID 才算得出來（預測對也要等 1 拍），預測錯懲罰 2 拍。CPI？
== 1.325
---
會跳（預測對）：1 拍；不會跳（預測錯）：2 拍。$1+0.25\times(0.7\times1+0.3\times2)=1+0.25\times1.3=1.325$。這也是為什麼需要 BTB。

@q pl4-05 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 觀念
Using MIPS terminology, an interrupt is caused outside the processor, while an exception is caused by the instruction that is running. A student watches a system for 1 second and logs 5 kinds of events: a key press on the keyboard, the timer expiring after 10 ms, an undefined instruction in a buggy program, an `add` that overflows, and a program executing `syscall` to ask for 4 KiB of input, and must sort them into the 2 categories. Which of the following events are interrupts (caused outside the processor) rather than exceptions caused by the running instruction? Select all that apply.
[x] A key is pressed on the keyboard.
[x] A timer expires.
[ ] An undefined instruction is executed.
[ ] An add instruction overflows.
[ ] The program executes syscall.
---
「鍵盤」、「計時器」是中斷：來自外部裝置，和正在執行的指令無關。

「未定義指令」、「溢位」、「syscall」是例外：都是目前這條指令造成的。

課本用「例外」泛指兩者時，會把來自外部的特別稱為中斷。

@q pl4-06 | co-pipeline#4 | 3 | 計算
A 5-stage pipeline (IF, ID, EX, MEM, WB) supports precise exceptions. In some clock cycle, instruction $I_k$ is in MEM and raises a memory access fault. At that moment $I_{k-1}$ is in WB, $I_{k+1}$ is in EX, $I_{k+2}$ is in ID, and $I_{k+3}$ is in IF. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Instruction $I_{k-1}$ is allowed to complete its WB stage.
[x] Instructions $I_k$, $I_{k+1}$, $I_{k+2}$, and $I_{k+3}$ are flushed so that none of them changes the architectural state.
[ ] Only $I_k$ is flushed; the instructions behind it continue, because they did not cause the fault.
[ ] If $I_{k+1}$ also overflows in EX in the same cycle, its exception should be handled first, because it was detected in an earlier pipeline stage.
[x] The address of $I_k$ must be recorded so that execution can restart from $I_k$ after the handler finishes.
---
精確例外的意思：之前的指令全部完成、出事的那條和之後的都像沒執行過。

「$I_{k-1}$ 讓它寫回」對：它在 $I_k$ 之前，必須完成。

「$I_k$ 到 $I_{k+3}$ 全部 flush」對：$I_k$ 自己不能留下效果（記憶體存取失敗），後面三條也不行，否則處理完回來會重複執行。

「只 flush $I_k$」錯：後面的指令在程式順序上晚於 $I_k$，讓它們改變狀態就不精確了。

「$I_{k+1}$ 在 EX 也出例外時先處理它」錯：同一拍有兩個例外時，要先處理**程式順序較早**的 $I_k$；$I_{k+1}$ 會被 flush，回來重跑時若還溢位，再處理它。管線階段較前面不代表指令較早。

「要記下 $I_k$ 的位址」對：記憶體錯誤（例如分頁錯誤）處理完要從 $I_k$ 重新執行。

@q pl4-07 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 計算
發生例外的指令位址是 0x00400040。MIPS 的 EPC 存的是？
== 0x00400040
== 00400040
---
存造成例外的那一條（有些實作存 PC+4，處理程式再減 4；題目要依課本慣例）。處理完要能重新執行它或跳過它。

@q pl4-08 | co-pipeline#4 | 3 | 陷阱
In the same cycle of a 5-stage pipeline, instruction $i$ raises an overflow in EX, and instruction $i+1$ is found to be undefined in ID. The processor provides precise exceptions and has a single EPC register. A student must decide which exception is handled first and what happens to instruction $i+1$, and a classmate suggests handling both exceptions together, saving 2 values in EPC, or handling the one detected in the earlier stage first, and the student also asks whether the undefined instruction will ever be reported. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The exception of instruction $i$ is handled first.
[x] Instruction $i+1$ is flushed; if it is executed again later, its exception will be raised then.
[ ] The exception of instruction $i+1$ is handled first, because it was detected in an earlier stage.
[ ] Both exceptions are handled together, with two values saved in EPC.
[x] Handling exceptions in program order is what precise exceptions require.
---
「先處理第 $i$ 條」對：程式順序在前的先處理。

「第 $i+1$ 條被 flush，之後重做時才再遇到」對：處理完第 $i$ 條的例外後，如果程式繼續執行到它，才會再處理它的例外。

「先處理第 $i+1$ 條」錯：偵測的級數早，不代表程式順序在前。

「同時處理、存兩個 EPC」錯：EPC 只有一個，也不需要同時處理。

「照程式順序是精確例外的要求」對：前面的做完、後面的都還沒改狀態。

@q pl4-09 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 情境
作業系統處理完「要用的頁不在記憶體」這種例外後，要讓程式繼續。為什麼精確例外對這件事很重要？
[x] 要能從造成例外的那一條重新執行，而且之前的指令都完成、之後的都沒做，狀態才一致
[ ] 為了速度
[ ] 不重要
[ ] 為了找出 bug
---
不精確的話，可能有後面的指令已經改了暫存器，重新執行會出錯。

@q pl4-10 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 找錯
A MIPS pipeline handles an exception raised by an instruction in a fixed way: the processor saves the address of the faulting instruction in EPC, records the reason in the Cause register, and transfers control to a handler at a fixed address, such as `0x80000180`. A student lists the steps and wonders what happens to the instructions before and after the faulting one in the 5-stage pipeline, and whether exceptions could simply be postponed until the program finishes. Which of the following statements about how the MIPS pipeline handles an exception raised by an instruction are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The instructions after the faulting instruction are flushed.
[ ] The instructions before the faulting instruction are flushed as well.
[x] The reason for the exception is recorded in the Cause register.
[x] Control is transferred to a fixed handler address in the operating system.
[ ] Exceptions are handled only after the whole program finishes.
---
「後面的清掉」對：它們還沒改狀態，之後可以重新執行。

「前面的也清掉」錯：flush 早的指令會讓它們的效果消失，之後又不會重新執行，程式就錯了。

「Cause 記原因」對：處理程式看它決定怎麼處理。

「跳到固定的處理程式位址」對。

「整個程式跑完才處理」錯：要立刻處理。

@q pl4-11 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 觀念
MIPS 的 Cause 暫存器記錄什麼？
[x] 例外的原因（例如溢位、未定義指令、syscall），讓處理程式知道該怎麼處理
[ ] 造成例外的指令位址
[ ] 下一條要執行的指令
[ ] 例外發生的時間
---
另一種設計是向量式例外：不同原因跳到不同的處理位址。

@q pl4-12 | co-pipeline#4 | 3 | 比較
An out-of-order teaching processor keeps unfinished instructions in a hardware list ordered by the original program. Execution may finish in a different order, but architectural register updates occur only when an instruction reaches the front and commits. Before the trace begins, register r7 contains 2. An older arithmetic instruction writes 6 to r5 and commits. The next instruction is a load that reports a memory fault.

A younger instruction has already computed 9 for r7, but its result is still held in the list and has not committed. The faulting load now reaches the front. The processor provides precise exceptions, so the handler must observe a state corresponding to a clean program-order boundary. Cache changes or other internal timing effects are outside the architectural state requested here. Determine which completed work can be kept and which speculative result must be discarded; completing arithmetic early is not the same as committing it. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Instructions may execute out of order while committing in program order.
[x] The fault is handled when the load reaches commit, with younger instructions discarded.
[x] The processor may discard a younger arithmetic result that was already computed.
[x] The uncommitted younger result has not yet changed architectural r7.
[ ] The handler must observe r7 equal to nine merely because the younger arithmetic instruction finished executing.
---
「亂序執行仍循序提交」對：兩種順序分開。「load到提交處理、清年輕者」對：保留先前已完成的程式前綴。「已算結果仍可丟」對：九尚未提交。「r7未變」對：仍是初值二；r5已由較早指令變六。「處理程式必見r7九」錯：把執行完成當作提交，會破壞精確狀態。這裡不宣稱快取等內部副作用全部復原。

@q pl4-13 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 觀念
A legacy 32-bit MIPS user program deliberately executes a syscall instruction at address 0x1000 to request an operating-system service. The instruction is not in a branch delay slot. The next sequential instruction is at 0x1004. For this model, the hardware records the syscall address in EPC, records its cause, enters privileged mode, and transfers control to the configured handler.

The requested service is performed once by the handler. Its normal return policy advances past the completed syscall rather than executing the request again. Younger instructions may already have entered the pipeline, but they must not modify architectural state before the handler has processed the request. A student confuses permission to invoke the syscall with permission to run arbitrary privileged instructions; another identifies every handler entry as an external-device interrupt. Separate the intentional synchronous request from the handler's privilege and from the next address used when this completed service returns normally. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The syscall causes a synchronous exception that saves its address and transfers control to the privileged handler.
[x] The specified normal return continues at 0x1004.
[x] A user program may invoke this service entry without being allowed to execute arbitrary privileged instructions.
[x] Younger pipeline instructions are flushed before they can commit state ahead of the syscall handling.
[ ] The completed service must normally return to 0x1000 and reissue the same syscall as its next program instruction.
---
「同步例外進特權處理」對：由正在執行的syscall本身觸發，不是外部裝置。「回0x1004」對：已完成的四位元組指令往後加四。「使用者可呼叫但不等於任意特權」對：這正是受控的服務入口。「清較年輕指令」對：避免先改架構狀態。「必回0x1000再呼叫」錯：題設正常返回已跳過完成的請求，回原處會重新執行它。

@q pl4-14 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 計算
管線偵測到例外的那一拍，第 $i$ 條在 EX。處理程式的第一條指令最早在第 $i$ 條的 EX 之後幾拍進入 IF？（flush 後下一拍從處理程式位址取指）
== 1
---
偵測到後把 PC 換成處理程式位址、flush 後面的指令，下一拍就開始取處理程式的第一條。

@q ca1-09 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算
32 位元位址、64 KB 的 2-way 組關聯 cache、區塊 32 bytes。offset、index、tag 各幾位元？
== 5,10,17
?? offset,index,tag
---
set 數 $64\text{K}/(32\times2)=1024$，index 10；offset $\lg32=5$；tag $32-15=17$。

@q ca1-10 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算
8 KB 直接映射、區塊 16 bytes、32 位元位址。位址 0xABCD1234 的 offset、index、tag（十六進位）？
== 0x4,0x123,0x55E68
== 4,123,55E68
?? offset,index,tag
---
offset 4 位元（最低一個 hex 數字 4）；index 9 位元＝$(\text{addr}\gg4)\bmod512$＝0x123；tag 是剩下的 19 位元＝0x55E68。程式驗算。

@q ca1-11 | co-cache#1 | 3 | 計算
4 個區塊的 cache，存取區塊位址 0、4、8、0、4、1、8、0。直接映射、2-way、全關聯（都換掉最久沒用的）各命中幾次？
== 0,0,4
?? 直接映射,2-way,全關聯
---
0、4、8 在直接映射都對到第 0 格、在 2-way 都對到第 0 組（組內只放得下 2 個），三個輪流把彼此踢掉。全關聯放得下全部，後面四次都命中。程式模擬驗算。

@q ca1-12 | co-cache#1 | 3 | 陷阱
A tiny cache holds 4 blocks and starts empty; a block whose number is $b$ maps to set $b \bmod s$, where $s$ is the number of sets. A program repeatedly touches three arrays whose blocks happen to be numbered 0, 4, and 8, and the trace of block references is
```
0, 4, 8, 0, 4, 8
```
Two designs are compared: a direct-mapped cache, with 4 sets of 1 block each, and a two-way set-associative cache, with 2 sets of 2 blocks each and least-recently-used replacement within a set. A student first checks which set each block maps to in both designs, and then counts misses and hits step by step. A classmate argues that the two-way cache must do better, because each set can now hold two of the three blocks, and predicts only 3 misses in total for the six references. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The direct-mapped cache has six misses.
[ ] The two-way cache has only three misses, because each set can hold two of the three blocks.
[ ] The two-way cache has three hits in the second half of the sequence.
[x] Three blocks competing for two places can keep replacing one another.
[ ] This sequence proves that associativity can never improve the hit rate.
---
「直接映射六次失誤」對：0、4、8 全映到同一列，每次替換。「兩路只有三次失誤」錯：0、4、8 除以 2 都餘 0，全進同一組；兩路放不下三塊，依最近最少使用策略每次都踢掉下一次要用的那塊，也是六次失誤。「後半三次命中」錯：後半仍全失誤。「兩個位置互踢」對。「永遠無效」錯：這只是一條特定序列，不能推廣到所有存取。

@q ca1-13 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算
16 KB、4-way、區塊 32 bytes、32 位元位址，每行另有 2 個狀態位元（valid 和「改過沒」）。每行共幾個位元（資料＋tag＋2）？全部共幾個位元？
== 278,142336
?? 每行,全部
---
128 組，index 7、offset 5、tag 20。每行 $256+20+2=278$，共 512 行。不含 LRU 位元。

@q ca1-14 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算
64 bytes 的直接映射 cache、區塊 16 bytes。依序存取位元組位址 0x00、0x04、0x40、0x10、0x44、0x80、0x08、0x48，命中幾次？
== 2
---
4 行。0x00（行 0 miss）、0x04（同區塊 hit）、0x40（行 0 miss，踢掉 0x00 區塊）、0x10（行 1 miss）、0x44（hit）、0x80（行 0 miss）、0x08（行 0 miss）、0x48（行 0 miss）。程式驗算。

@q ca1-15 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 觀念
`for (i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += a[i];` 主要利用了哪一種區域性？`sum` 呢？
[x] `a[i]` 是空間區域性（相鄰元素在同一區塊）；`sum` 是時間區域性（一直重複用）
[ ] 兩個都是時間區域性
[ ] 兩個都是空間區域性
[ ] 都沒有區域性
---
迴圈的指令本身也有時間區域性（同一段程式一直執行）。

@q ca1-16 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 找錯

[ ] 沒錯

@q ca1-17 | co-cache#1 | 1 | 計算

== 232
---
$1000\bmod256=232$。

@q ca1-18 | co-cache#1 | 3 | 比較
同樣 32 KB、64 B 區塊，直接映射和 8-way 的 tag 位元數差多少？
== 3
---
8-way 的 set 數少 8 倍，index 少 3 位元，這 3 位元變成 tag。關聯度越高，tag 越長、比較器越多。

@q ca1-19 | co-cache#1 | 3 | 情境
A physics simulation stores 64 particles, and each particle record occupies 32 bytes, with its 4-byte position first, followed by velocity, mass, and other members. One loop reads only the positions of all 64 particles. A developer compares this layout of contiguous records with a second layout that keeps all positions together in a separate contiguous array of 64 entries. Both layouts start at a 64-byte boundary, the positions are read in particle order, and the cache is initially empty, large enough to hold everything, and fetches 64-byte blocks. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] The record layout needs 64 distinct blocks for the requested positions.
[x] The separate position array needs four distinct blocks.
[ ] Every fetched byte in the record layout is a position used by this loop.
[ ] The record layout uses more of each fetched block for this loop.
[ ] The separate layout must also be better for every loop that reads every record member.
---
「64 個區塊」錯：每塊含兩個 32 位元組的紀錄，64÷2＝32 塊。「四個區塊」對：位置共 64×4＝256 位元組，256÷64＝4。「每個位元組都用到」錯：紀錄內還有未讀的成員。「紀錄布局利用率較高」錯：每塊 64 位元組只用到 8 位元組；分開的位置陣列整塊都用到。「任何迴圈都較好」錯：若各成員總是一起讀取，原布局也可能有優勢。

@q ca2-07 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 計算
write-through、no-write-allocate。程式對同一個（不在 cache 裡的）位址連續寫 5 次，再讀 1 次。記憶體被寫幾次？讀的那次命中嗎？
== 5,不命中
?? 寫記憶體次數,讀命中
---
每次寫都直接寫記憶體；no-write-allocate 寫入沒中時不把區塊搬進來，所以之後讀還是 miss。

@q ca2-08 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 計算
write-back、write-allocate。同樣對同一個（不在 cache 裡的）位址寫 5 次再讀 1 次，最後區塊被換出。記憶體被寫幾次（以區塊計）？讀命中嗎？
== 1,命中
?? 寫回次數,讀命中
---
第一次寫 miss，把區塊搬進來，之後寫都在 cache；讀命中；換出時 dirty 才寫回一次。

@q ca2-09 | co-cache#2 | 3 | 陷阱
A single-processor system uses a write-through cache: every 4-byte store is sent to memory, and the cache never merges several stores into one memory write. A program performs 8 stores to different words of one cached 64-byte block, and all of these writes reach memory before the block is evicted. A student asks which of the usual per-block bits are still needed in this design, how much data the stores send to memory, and whether a write buffer between the cache and memory could still help the processor. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] These stores send 32 data bytes to memory.
[x] A dirty bit is unnecessary solely for deciding whether this line needs write-back on eviction.
[ ] A valid bit becomes unnecessary because stores reach memory.
[x] Eviction need not write the block back, even though it was written eight times.
[x] A write buffer can still be useful for handling memory-write latency.
---
「32 位元組」對：8×4＝32，題設禁止合併。「無須用髒位元決定換出寫回」對：這些寫入已完成，記憶體已有最新值。「不需有效位元」錯：仍要分辨列內資料是否有效。「換出不用寫回」對：write-through 每次寫入都已送到記憶體。「寫入緩衝有用」對：可吸收寫入等待，是否需要髒位元與是否有等待是兩回事。

@q ca2-10 | co-cache#2 | 3 | 觀念
A write-through cache sends stores to memory through a write buffer with 4 entries, which is initially empty. Each store occupies one entry until memory completes it, and memory needs about 100 cycles per store. Three separate stores arrive within 10 cycles, before memory has completed any of them. A student wants to know what the buffer achieves, what it does not guarantee about memory, and what happens if the program keeps storing at that rate for 1000 cycles without a pause. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] One buffer entry remains unused after the three stores.
[x] The buffer can let the processor continue before the memory writes complete.
[x] Enqueuing a store does not mean that memory has already completed it.
[x] A full buffer may prevent a later store from proceeding until space is freed.
[x] If stores keep arriving faster than memory completes them, the buffer eventually fills.
---
「剩一格」對：4−3＝1。「可先繼續」對：先入緩衝，再由記憶體慢慢完成。「入列不等於完成」對：只是暫存未完成的工作。「滿載會停」對：無處存新寫入時就要等。「寫入比記憶體快就會滿」對：緩衝只能吸收短時間的尖峰，不會讓記憶體本身變快。五句都對。

@q ca2-11 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 計算
write-back cache，每 1000 條指令有 100 次換出，其中 40% 是 dirty，每次寫回 80 拍（不能重疊）。每條指令平均多花幾拍在寫回？
== 3.2
---
$100\times0.4\times80/1000$。

@q ca2-12 | co-cache#2 | 3 | 情境
一個區塊被寫入 1000 次才被換出。write-through 和 write-back 寫到記憶體的流量（以字計，每次寫一個字；區塊 8 字）差多少倍？
== 125
---
write-through：1000 個字；write-back：換出時寫一整個區塊 8 個字。$1000/8$。寫入次數多、集中時 write-back 省很多頻寬。

@q ca2-13 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 比較
write-allocate 通常搭 write-back、no-write-allocate 搭 write-through。理由？
[x] write-back 希望之後的寫入都在 cache 裡完成，所以要先搬進來；write-through 本來就會寫記憶體，搬進來的好處小
[ ] 硬體規定
[ ] 沒有理由
[ ] 為了省 dirty 位元
---
不過也有系統用其他組合，例如寫入很少再讀的串流資料。

@q ca2-14 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「write-back cache 換出區塊時，一律都要寫回記憶體。」錯在哪？
[x] 只有 dirty（被改過）的區塊要寫回；乾淨的直接丟掉
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 一律不用寫回
[ ] 只有 valid 的要寫回
---
這正是 dirty 位元存在的理由。

@q ca2-15 | co-cache#2 | 3 | 計算
write-back、write-allocate，區塊 4 字。一次寫入 miss 時，要先把 dirty 的舊區塊寫回（4 字）、再把新區塊讀進來（4 字）。每字傳輸 2 拍、啟動 10 拍（讀和寫各一次）。這次 miss 共幾拍？
== 36
---
寫回 $10+4\times2=18$、讀入 18。如果有 write buffer 讓寫回跟讀入重疊，可以縮短到 18 左右。

@q ca2-16 | co-cache#2 | 1 | 觀念
Memory stores value 7 at a location. A write-back cache has a clean copy, then a store changes only the cached value to 9. No eviction or explicit write-back has occurred. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Memory can still contain 7.
[x] The cached block must be marked dirty after the store.
[ ] Memory must contain 9 immediately after this store.
[ ] A cache read must return 7 solely because memory still contains 7.
[ ] The cached value differs from memory by 3.
---
「記憶體仍是 7」對：write-back 允許延後寫回。「標 dirty」對：快取的值已修改。「立刻 9」錯：那是本題未執行的寫回。「讀必回 7」錯：有效的快取命中應回傳最新的 9。「相差 3」錯：9−7＝2。

@q ca3-06 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 計算
4 個區塊的直接映射 cache，區塊位址序列 0、4、8、0、4、1、8、0。8 次中 compulsory、capacity、conflict miss 各幾次？
== 4,0,4
?? compulsory,capacity,conflict
---
第一次碰到 0、4、8、1 是 compulsory（4 次）。同樣大小的全關聯 cache 後面都會命中，所以剩下的 4 次（0、4、8、0）都是 conflict；沒有 capacity miss。

@q ca3-07 | co-cache#3 | 2 | 觀念
怎麼判斷一次 miss 是 capacity 還是 conflict？
[x] 用同樣容量的全關聯（LRU）cache 跑同一串：那邊也 miss 的是 capacity；那邊會中、這邊沒中的是 conflict
[ ] 看是不是第一次
[ ] 看位址的大小
[ ] 看區塊大小
---
compulsory 則是不管 cache 多大都一定會發生的第一次存取。

@q ca3-08 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 陷阱
An empty direct-mapped cache stores 128 data bytes. Compare 32-byte blocks with 64-byte blocks. Use byte addresses. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] With 32-byte blocks, a sequential read of bytes 0 through 127 touches eight distinct blocks.
[ ] With 64-byte blocks, that sequential read touches four distinct blocks.
[ ] Larger blocks increase the number of cache lines in this fixed-size cache.
[x] For sequence 0,160,0,160, the larger-block cache has more misses.
[ ] Larger blocks always reduce conflict misses.
---
「32 位元組塊碰到八塊」錯：128÷32＝4 塊。「64 位元組塊碰到四塊」錯：128÷64＝2 塊。「增加列數」錯：列數從四減成二。「0、160 序列較差」對：小塊時區塊編號 0、5 映到不同列，兩次失誤；大塊時區塊編號 0、2 都映到同一列，四次失誤。「衝突必少」錯：上一個例子正好顯示相反結果。

@q ca3-09 | co-cache#3 | 2 | 計算
8-way 組關聯，每組用「記住完整使用順序」的精確 LRU，至少要幾個位元？
== 16
---
8 個區塊的排列有 $8!=40320$ 種，$\lceil\lg40320\rceil=16$。所以高關聯度常用近似 LRU（例如樹狀 pseudo-LRU 只要 7 位元）。

@q ca3-10 | co-cache#3 | 2 | 計算
樹狀 pseudo-LRU 給 8-way 用，每組要幾個位元？
== 7
---
8 個葉子的完整二元樹有 7 個內部節點，每個節點 1 位元指向「比較久沒用的那一半」。

@q ca3-11 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 找錯
An empty fully associative cache holds two blocks and uses least-recently-used replacement. Read block sequence 0,1,2,0. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] Only three of the four accesses miss.
[ ] Only the first two accesses are compulsory misses.
[x] The final miss remains possible even without mapping conflicts.
[ ] Full associativity means that the cache can hold any number of distinct blocks simultaneously.
[ ] The final access must hit because block 0 was fetched earlier.
---
「只有三次失誤」錯：0、1 先裝入，讀 2 時換出最久未用的 0，最後讀 0 再失誤，四次全失誤。「只有前兩次強制」錯：0、1、2 各是首次存取，前三次都是強制失誤。「沒衝突仍會失誤」對：只有兩格，放不下三個重用的塊，這是容量失誤。「無限多塊」錯：全關聯只放寬位置限制，不增加容量。「曾載入必命中」錯：資料可能已被換出。

@q ca3-12 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 情境
程式交替存取兩個大陣列 A、B，每次存取 A[i] 後存取 B[i]。在直接映射 cache 中兩個陣列的起始位址剛好相差 cache 大小的整數倍。會發生什麼？怎麼解？
[x] A[i] 和 B[i] 一直對到同一行、互相踢掉（conflict miss）；改成組關聯，或在陣列之間加一點間隔（padding）
[ ] 都會命中
[ ] 只有 compulsory miss
[ ] 加大區塊就好
---
這種「剛好對齊」的衝突在矩陣運算中很常見。

@q ca3-13 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 比較
An eight-block cache changes from direct mapping to four-way associativity. Consider the number of sets and the information required to represent every possible least-recently-used order within one set. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The four-way design has two sets.
[ ] Four ways have only four possible recency orders.
[ ] Increasing associativity reduces the work needed to select the data on a hit.
[ ] Increasing cache size guarantees less time to deliver data on a hit.
[x] Encoding all 24 orders of four distinct ways requires at least five bits per set.
---
「兩組」對：八塊除以四路。「只有四種順序」錯：四個不同位置有 4!＝24 種先後次序。「選資料成本減少」錯：多路要同時比較更多標籤、再從多路中選出資料，工作反而增加。「容量大必更快」錯：較大的儲存陣列可能增加延遲。「至少五位」對：四位只能表示 16 種，五位能表示 32 種，才足以編碼 24 種狀態；實作也可能用更多位元。

@q ca3-14 | co-cache#3 | 1 | 計算
4-way 組關聯用精確 LRU，每組至少幾個位元？
== 5
---
$4!=24$ 種順序，$\lceil\lg24\rceil=5$。

@q ca4-06 | co-cache#4 | 2 | 計算
命中 2 拍、miss rate 4%、miss penalty 150 拍。AMAT？
== 8
---
$2+0.04\times150$。

@q ca4-07 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 計算
L1 命中 1 拍、local miss rate 4%；L2 命中 12 拍、local miss rate 25%；記憶體 200 拍。AMAT？L2 的 global miss rate？
== 3.48,1%
== 3.48,0.01
?? AMAT,L2 global
---
$1+0.04\times(12+0.25\times200)=1+0.04\times62=3.48$。global＝$0.04\times0.25$。

@q ca4-08 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Out of 2000 processor references, 100 miss in L1 and reach L2; 40 of those miss in L2. There is no other path to memory. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The L2 local miss rate is 40%.
[x] The global fraction of references reaching memory is 2%.
[ ] Forty percent of all processor references reach memory.
[x] The average access time also depends on the L1 miss rate and on the access time of each level.
[x] L2 services only the references not satisfied by L1 in this example.
---
「局部 40%」對：40÷100。「全域 2%」對：40÷2000。「全部 40% 到記憶體」錯：混用分母。「平均時間還要看 L1 比例與各層時間」對：只看 L2 局部比例算不出平均存取時間。「只接 L1 未處理者」對：這是題設的存取路徑，不能直接拿局部數字代表全部存取。

@q ca4-09 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 計算
基本 CPI 1.2；指令 cache miss rate 1%；每條指令平均 0.4 次資料存取、資料 cache miss rate 6%；miss penalty 都是 100 拍。實際 CPI？
== 4.6
---
$1.2+0.01\times100+0.4\times0.06\times100=1.2+1+2.4$。

@q ca4-10 | co-cache#4 | 2 | 計算
接上題，把時脈加倍（基本 CPI 不變，但 miss penalty 變成 200 拍）。新的 CPI？整體快幾倍？
== 8,1.15
?? CPI,快幾倍
---
$1.2+2+4.8=8$。時間比：舊 $4.6\times1$、新 $8\times0.5=4$，快 $4.6/4=1.15$ 倍。時脈加倍只換到 15%：記憶體不跟著變快。

@q ca4-11 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 找錯
An L1 lookup takes 1 ns and misses on 10% of references. An L2 lookup takes an additional 5 ns and misses on 20% of L1 misses. A memory access adds 80 ns after an L2 miss. Lookups are sequential. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] The fraction of all references that access memory is 0.2.
[x] The average access time is 3.1 ns.
[ ] The average access time is 17.5 ns.
[ ] A reference that misses in both caches takes 80 ns.
[ ] The L2 local miss rate is 2%.
---
「0.2 到記憶體」錯：0.2 是 L2 的局部失誤率；到記憶體的比例是 0.1×0.2＝0.02。「3.1 ns」對：1＋0.1×(5＋0.2×80)＝3.1。「17.5 ns」錯：它把 L2 的局部失誤率直接乘記憶體時間，漏乘到達 L2 的 0.1。「兩層都失誤 80 ns」錯：查找是依序的，要 1＋5＋80＝86 ns。「局部 2%」錯：2% 是相對所有存取的全域失誤率，局部仍為 20%。

@q ca4-12 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 情境
A processor has a 1 ns L1 cache with a 10% miss rate. After an L1 miss, it pays 8 ns to access L2; 25% of those accesses then require another 100 ns for memory. An alternative single large cache takes 5 ns and misses on 1% of references, each adding 100 ns. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The L1/L2 design has an average access time of 4.3 ns.
[x] The single large cache has an average access time of 6 ns.
[x] Despite its smaller miss rate, the single large cache is slower on average.
[x] In this example, the L1/L2 design is faster on average.
[x] The L1/L2 design accesses memory on 2.5% of all references.
---
「4.3 ns」對：1＋0.1×(8＋0.25×100)＝4.3。「6 ns」對：5＋0.01×100＝6。「失誤率較低卻較慢」對：命中時間 5 ns 每次都要付，失誤率低省下的不夠抵。「兩層較快」對：4.3＜6。「2.5%」對：到記憶體的比例為 10%×25%。五句都對。

@q ca4-13 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 計算

== 9%,5%
== 0.09,0.05
?? 原本,目標
---
$1+m\times100=10$ ⇒ $m=9\%$；$=6$ ⇒ $5\%$。

@q ca4-14 | co-cache#4 | 1 | 計算
命中 1 拍、miss rate 10%、miss penalty 50 拍。AMAT？
== 6

$1+0.1\times50$。

@q ca5-05 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算
送位址 1 拍、DRAM 存取 20 拍、傳一個字 1 拍；區塊 8 個字。記憶體一個字寬時 miss penalty？
== 176
---
每個字 $1+20+1$，8 個字：$8\times22$。

@q ca5-06 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算

== 29
---
送位址 1 拍、8 個 bank 同時存取 20 拍、再逐字傳 8 拍：$1+20+8$。

@q ca5-07 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算
同上，改成記憶體和匯流排都 8 個字寬。miss penalty？
== 22
---
$1+20+1$。最快，但匯流排和記憶體都要加寬，很貴。

@q ca5-08 | co-cache#5 | 3 | 比較
In a simplified eight-word fetch, a design with an eight-word-wide bus uses one address cycle, 20 access cycles, and one data cycle. A banked design accesses all eight words concurrently but returns them on a one-word-wide bus in eight data cycles. Both use one address cycle and have no phase overlap. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The wide-bus design takes 22 cycles.
[x] The banked design takes 29 cycles.
[ ] The banked design is faster in this model.
[ ] The banked design still requires an eight-word-wide data bus.
[ ] Banking removes the 20-cycle bank-access phase entirely.
---
「22 拍」對：1＋20＋1＝22。「29 拍」對：1＋20＋8＝29。「分 bank 較快」錯：本模型快七拍的是寬匯流排。「仍要八字寬匯流排」錯：分 bank 的好處正是只用一字寬的匯流排，代價是串行回傳。「移除 20 拍」錯：它將多個 bank 的存取重疊，並非使存取延遲消失；實際成本優劣仍需看硬體設計。

@q ca5-09 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 觀念
現代 DRAM 的「突發傳輸」（burst）在做什麼？
[x] 一次送出列位址後，連續送出同一列中相鄰的好幾個字，不用每個字都重新送位址
[ ] 一次傳整個記憶體
[ ] 讓 DRAM 不用刷新
[ ] 讓 cache 變大
---
跟交錯的目的一樣：把固定的啟動延遲攤到很多字上。

@q ca5-10 | co-cache#5 | 3 | 找錯
A four-word block request sends one address in one cycle. Four banks then access their assigned words simultaneously for 15 cycles. A one-word-wide bus finally returns the four words serially, one cycle each; these three phases do not overlap. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The block transfer takes 20 cycles.
[x] The bus-return phase takes four cycles.
[ ] Each bank must wait for all other banks' 15-cycle access phases to finish before starting.
[x] The parallel bank-access phase lasts 15 cycles in total.
[x] If every word instead needed its own address, access, and transfer cycles in series, the request would take 68 cycles.
---
「20 拍」對：1＋15＋4＝20。「匯流排四拍」對：一次只能傳一個字。「各 bank 輪流等 15 拍」錯：題設四個 bank 同時存取。「存取共 15 拍」對：可重疊的是 bank 存取階段。「全部串行 68 拍」對：4×(1＋15＋1)＝68，這就是沒有分 bank 的情形。

@q ca5-11 | co-cache#5 | 3 | 計算
區塊 4 字。一字寬記憶體 miss penalty 是 4×(1+15+1)=68；改成 4-bank 交錯是 20。miss rate 3%、命中 1 拍，AMAT 各是？
== 3.04,1.6
?? 一字寬,交錯
---
$1+0.03\times68=3.04$；$1+0.03\times20=1.6$。

@q ca5-12 | co-cache#5 | 1 | 觀念
miss penalty 的三個主要部分？
[x] 送位址、等記憶體存取、把資料傳回來
[ ] 解碼、執行、寫回
[ ] 讀、寫、刷新
[ ] tag、index、offset
---
改善 miss penalty 就是讓這三部分更短或互相重疊。

@q ca6-06 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 計算
`int A[64][64]`（4 bytes、列優先），cache 512 bytes 直接映射、區塊 16 bytes。逐列走訪和逐行走訪各 miss 幾次？cache 改成 16 KB 呢？
== 1024,4096,1024,1024
?? 列序512B,行序512B,列序16KB,行序16KB
---
列序：每 4 個 int 一個區塊，$4096/4=1024$。行序 512 B 時每次都換不同列、區塊早被踢掉，4096 次全 miss。16 KB 放得下整個陣列，兩種都只有 1024 次。程式模擬驗算。

@q ca6-07 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 觀念
A software prefetch starts a 40 ns fetch. Assume no contention and that the block remains cached until use. The processor can perform independent work during the fetch. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] Starting the fetch 20 ns before use leaves no stall at use.
[ ] Starting it 60 ns before use still leaves a 20 ns stall.
[ ] A prefetch instruction increases the physical size of the cache.
[ ] A prefetch issued at the instant of use hides all 40 ns under these assumptions.
[x] Extra prefetch instructions can add execution overhead.
---
「提早 20 ns 不停等」錯：還差 40−20＝20 ns。「提早 60 ns 仍停 20 ns」錯：40 ns 內已完成，題設保證資料未被換出，可完全遮蔽。「增加容量」錯：預取只是提早載入。「用到才發動也遮蔽」錯：沒有任何提前時間。「增加指令成本」對：軟體要執行額外的預取指令。

@q ca6-08 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 情境
Blocked multiplication keeps three square tiles in a fully associative 32 KiB cache. Each tile has $b^2$ eight-byte entries. Ignore all other cache use. Restrict b to positive powers of two. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[ ] Three tiles with b equal to 32 require 8 KiB.
[x] Choosing b equal to 64 exceeds the cache budget.
[x] The largest permitted power-of-two tile side is 32.
[ ] Choosing the largest possible tile always guarantees the fastest execution on every real cache.
[ ] Only one of the three tiles needs to fit because the arithmetic uses one entry at a time.
---
「$b=32$ 三塊 8 KiB」錯：8 KiB 是一塊的大小（$32^2\times8=8192$）；三塊是 24 KiB。「64 超額」對：3×64²×8＝98304 位元組。「最大二次方邊長 32」對：24 KiB 放得下，下一個 64 已超額。「真實一定最快」錯：組映射、替換與其他資料仍會影響實際效能。「只放一塊」錯：題設要同時重用三個小塊。

@q ca6-09 | co-cache#6 | 2 | 計算
victim cache 有 4 個區塊。直接映射 cache 中兩個區塊 X、Y 一直互相衝突，交替存取 X、Y、X、Y…共 100 次。有 victim cache 時大約幾次要到記憶體？
== 2
---
第一次 X、第一次 Y 是 compulsory；之後被踢掉的那個放在 victim cache，下一次從 victim cache 換回來，不用到記憶體。

@q ca6-10 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 比較
A block has words numbered 0 through 3. After startup, one word arrives per cycle. The processor needs word 2. A fixed-order transfer sends 0,1,2,3; a critical-word-first transfer sends 2,3,0,1. Execution may resume when the requested word arrives. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With early restart and fixed order, the requested word arrives in transfer cycle 3.
[x] With critical-word-first, the requested word arrives in transfer cycle 1.
[x] Early restart by itself does not change the transfer order.
[x] Both transfers still send four words.
[x] With critical-word-first, execution can resume before word 1 arrives.
---
「第三拍」對：固定順序先到 0、1，才到 2。「第一拍」對：關鍵字優先直接先送 2。「提早開始不改順序」對：它只規定需要的字到達即可繼續；改順序的是關鍵字優先。「仍四字」對：只是調順序，整塊仍補齊。「字 1 到之前就能繼續」對：需要的是字 2，它第一拍就到，字 1 最後才到。五句都對。

@q ca6-11 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 找錯
Two loops read exactly the same elements of one array, each element once. The array layout and cache block size are unchanged. A block's first access is classified as a compulsory miss. Only traversal order changes. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both loops touch the same set of distinct blocks.
[ ] Both loops must have the same total number of misses.
[ ] A traversal with fewer total misses must touch fewer distinct blocks.
[x] Traversal order can change misses caused by replacing a block before its next use.
[ ] A block loaded once can never miss again during that traversal.
---
「相同區塊集合」對：元素、布局與區塊大小未變，所以強制性失誤也一樣多。「總失誤一定相同」錯：強制性失誤相同，但一個順序可能在同塊資料用完前就把它換出、之後再載入，另一個不會。「較少總失誤必較少區塊」錯：可由減少重新載入達成。「替換後重用」對：較佳順序可在換出前用完同塊資料。「載入後不再失誤」錯：被換出後仍可能再次失誤。

@q ca6-12 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 情境
A processor can issue independent loads while an older load is incomplete. A non-blocking cache starts one 100 ns miss at time 0 and another independent 100 ns miss at time 10. The memory system can handle both concurrently without extra delay. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The second miss can complete at 110 ns.
[x] The two misses overlap for 90 ns.
[ ] The second miss must wait until 100 ns merely because the first miss is outstanding.
[x] Having independent work helps exploit support for multiple outstanding misses.
[x] The second load's value cannot be used before 110 ns.
---
「110 ns」對：10＋100＝110。「重疊 90 ns」對：同時未完成的區間為 10 到 100。「一定等前筆完成」錯：題設支援同時未完成的失誤。「獨立工作有幫助」對：不能提前做的相依工作仍受限制。「110 ns 前不能用」對：資料要等失誤完成才回來。

@q ca6-13 | co-cache#6 | 1 | 觀念
Eight four-byte integers in a row are stored consecutively in a row-major array. The row begins at a 32-byte boundary. A loop is changed so that its inner index walks across this row. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The eight integers fit in one 32-byte block.
[x] Walking across the row can exploit spatial locality.
[ ] Consecutive elements in this row are 32 bytes apart.
[ ] Interchanging loops changes the values stored in the array by itself.
[ ] This layout stores only one integer per 32-byte block.
---
「一塊放八個」對：8×4＝32。「利用空間區域性」對：取來一塊就含接著要用的鄰近資料。「相距 32」錯：同列相鄰整數相距四位元組。「交換迴圈自行改值」錯：改的是走訪次序，並非資料內容。「每塊一個」錯：依題設每塊可容納八個。

@q ca6-14 | co-cache#6 | 2 | 計算
陣列 `double x[1024]`（8 bytes），區塊 64 bytes。循序加總一次，至少幾次 miss？若加了「提前 8 個元素預取」而且時間夠，實際要停下來等的 miss 大約幾次？
== 128,1
?? 至少miss,要等的
---
每區塊 8 個 double，$1024/8=128$。預取讓後面的區塊在用到之前就到了，只有第一個要等。

@q il1-07 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 計算
I1: R1 = R2 + R3；I2: R4 = R1 × R5；I3: R2 = R6 − R1；I4: R4 = R2 + R7。RAW、WAR、WAW 各有幾個？
== 3,1,1
?? RAW,WAR,WAW
---
RAW：I1→I2（R1）、I1→I3（R1）、I3→I4（R2）。WAR：I1 讀 R2、I3 寫 R2。WAW：I2、I4 都寫 R4。

@q il1-08 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 計算
接上題，用暫存器換名後，還剩幾個相依？
== 3
---
WAR、WAW 是名稱相依（只是剛好用到同一個暫存器名字），換名可以消除；3 個 RAW 是真的資料流，消不掉。

@q il1-09 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 反例
A compiler considers moving a load above a store to hide memory latency. The original code is
```
li  r1, 7
sw  r1, 0(r2)      # store 7 through base register r2
lw  r3, 0(r4)      # load through base register r4
```
At run time, both r2 and r4 happen to contain the address 40, and memory location 40 initially holds 5; ignore faults and all other memory accesses. The compiler cannot see the run-time values of r2 and r4, only their names, and a junior developer argues that the two instructions are independent because they use different base registers. Another developer suggests renaming r4 to a fresh register that keeps the same value, to make the reordering safe, and a third points out that the load would then return a different value. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] In the original order the load obtains seven.
[x] Moving the load before the store can make it obtain five.
[x] Different register names do not prove different effective memory addresses.
[ ] The load and store are independent solely because r2 and r4 have different names.
[ ] Renaming r4 while preserving its value makes address 40 different from the store address.
---
「原順序讀七」對：先寫同位置再讀。「提前讀五」對：會讀到寫入前的舊內容。「不同名仍可同址」對：兩個基底值都是四十。「名不同就獨立」錯：還有透過記憶體的真相依。「只改名改位址」錯：值不變，有效位址仍四十，不能靠暫存器名字解除此資料關係。

@q il1-10 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 找錯
A scheduler examines this pair of instructions, in program order:
```
add r1, r2, r3     # reads r2 and r3, writes r1
sub r2, r4, r5     # reads r4 and r5, writes r2
```
The second instruction does not read r1, and all four source values r2, r3, r4, and r5 are available at the start. The scheduler wants to know which kind of dependence, if any, links the two instructions, whether `sub` could be executed first, and what register renaming would change. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The first read of r2 followed by the second write to r2 is WAR.
[x] Giving the second destination a fresh name can remove this name dependence if all later uses are updated consistently.
[ ] The second instruction needs the first instruction's produced r1 as a source.
[ ] Both instructions write r2, so their dependence is WAW.
[ ] Renaming makes the first instruction's arithmetic unnecessary for every program that later reads r1.
---
「r2 上 WAR」對：第一條讀，第二條寫。「新名並一致更新可解」對：老讀值與新結果不再共用同一儲存版本。「第二需 r1」錯：其來源是 r4、r5，沒有此真相依。「兩者都寫 r2」錯：第一條寫的是 r1。「改名使加法沒用」錯：後續仍可能需要 r1，消除名稱限制不等於刪除資料計算。

@q il1-11 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 計算
迴圈每次迭代用 3 個暫存器存中間值。展開 8 次、為了排程讓每份都用不同暫存器，至少要幾個？
== 24
---
展開越多、需要的暫存器越多；暫存器不夠時就得把值存回記憶體（spill），反而變慢。

@q il1-12 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 情境
A compiler unrolls the loop below by a factor of 4, for an iteration count $n$ that is known only at run time and may be any nonnegative integer:
```
for (k = 0; k < n; k++) x[k] = x[k] + s;
```
The transformed code has a main loop whose body handles 4 original iterations at a time, followed by a cleanup loop that handles the remaining iterations, the remainder of $n$ divided by 4, one at a time. A tester runs the transformed code with $n = 11$, recording how many times the main loop body and the cleanup body run, and also with $n = 2$ and $n = 0$, to check that no element outside `x[0..n-1]` is touched. A colleague proposes dropping the cleanup loop whenever $n$ is unknown at compile time, to make the code shorter and slightly faster. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The main loop executes two blocks.
[x] The cleanup executes three original iterations.
[x] The total number of original iteration bodies executed remains eleven.
[ ] The compiler may silently omit cleanup whenever n is not known at compile time.
[ ] For n equal to two, one full four-body block must execute even if it accesses elements outside the original range.
---
「主迴圈兩塊」對：floor(11/4)＝2。「收尾三次」對：11 mod 4＝3。「總共十一」對：2×4＋3。「不知 n 就省收尾」錯：恰好更需要執行時處理餘數，省略會漏做三次。「小於四仍硬做」錯：n＝2 應只有收尾兩次，不能越界補成四次。

@q il1-13 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 觀念
分支後面的指令依賴「分支走哪邊」（控制相依）。處理器想提早執行它們，要靠什麼？
[x] 分支預測：先照猜的方向執行後面的指令，猜錯再把那些結果丟掉
[ ] 換名
[ ] 迴圈展開
[ ] 不能提早
---
推測執行讓結果不能直接寫進架構狀態，要等確定（commit）。

@q il1-14 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 比較
限制一段程式能平行到什麼程度的根本因素是？
[x] 真資料相依（RAW）串成的最長鏈；名稱相依可以換名、控制相依可以預測
[ ] 暫存器數量
[ ] 指令長度
[ ] 記憶體大小
---
就算硬體無限大，RAW 鏈的長度也決定了最短執行時間。

@q il1-15 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 計算
I1: R1 = R2 × R3（4 拍）；I2: R4 = R1 + R5（1 拍）；I3: R6 = R7 + R8（1 拍）；I4: R9 = R6 + R4（1 拍）。無限多執行單元、換名完美，最少幾拍做完？
== 6
---
關鍵路徑 I1→I2→I4：$4+1+1=6$；I3 可以跟 I1 同時做。

@q il1-16 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 觀念
A loop runs 128 iterations, and each iteration ends with one loop-control branch that jumps back to the top. A compiler unrolls the loop by 4, so that one loop-control branch is executed per 4 original iterations, and no remainder loop is needed because 128 is a multiple of 4. The processor uses a 2-bit branch predictor, and other branches can be ignored. An engineer wants to know how many loop-control branches the new loop executes, how much that saves, and what it means for prediction errors, which cost about 15 cycles each. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The transformed loop executes 32 loop-control branches.
[x] This transformation reduces the number of those branch executions by 75 percent.
[ ] The number of prediction errors must decrease by exactly 75 percent for every predictor and input.
[ ] Unrolling guarantees that every remaining branch prediction is correct.
[ ] The transformed loop executes 512 loop-control branches.
---
「三十二次」對：128÷4。「少百分之七十五」對：減少 96 次，96/128＝0.75。「錯誤數也必同比例」錯：錯誤取決於預測器與分支歷史，次數比例不是錯誤率保證。「剩下全猜對」錯：展開不會消除最後退出等預測問題。「五百一十二次」錯：把展開倍率用成增加分支次數，控制分支實際更少。

@q il2-04 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 觀念
A classic scoreboard separates issue, read operands, execute, and write result. It does not rename registers. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Issue checks functional-unit availability and pending instructions that will write the destination.
[x] Read operands waits for RAW requirements to be satisfied.
[x] Write result may wait because an older instruction has not read an old source value.
[ ] WAW is handled only after the younger instruction has overwritten the destination.
[ ] An instruction missing one source must perform its arithmetic with zero at execute and repair the value later.
---
「發出查單元與目的寫者」對：結構與 WAW 條件。「讀等 RAW」對：必須取得真正的來源。「寫等老讀者」對：避免 WAR。「覆寫後才處理 WAW」錯：會破壞程式寫入順序，應在發出擋住。「缺來源先用零」錯：本模型等待資料，不會任意替換數值計算。

@q il2-05 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
A scoreboard has issued `DIV f0,f2,f4` and then `ADD f10,f0,f8`. The add unit is free, f8 is available, and the DIV has not written f0. This scoreboard has no forwarding. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The ADD must wait at the read-operands stage for f0.
[x] The dependence through f0 is RAW.
[ ] The ADD is blocked by WAW because its destination is f10.
[ ] Availability of f8 alone permits the ADD to compute its correct result.
[ ] The ADD must necessarily wait at issue solely because one source is unavailable under this model.
---
「卡讀運算元」對：發出可以先完成，真正資料須等 DIV 寫回。「f0 是 RAW」對：前一條寫、後一條讀。「目的 f10 構成 WAW」錯：DIV 寫 f0，兩目的不同。「只靠 f8 可算」錯：加法另一來源仍缺。「必卡發出」錯：題設已發出，典型記分板在 read operands 等真正來源。

@q il2-06 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
In a scoreboard without renaming, a MUL has issued with destination f2. The next ADD also targets f2 and otherwise has no resource or input blockage. The MUL finishes execution in cycle eight but writes its result in cycle ten. Issue checks see released destinations beginning in cycle eleven. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The ADD is blocked by WAW before the MUL writes back.
[x] Cycle eleven is the first permitted ADD issue cycle under the specified timing.
[ ] The ADD may issue in cycle nine solely because the MUL has finished execution.
[ ] This destination hazard is removed by the scoreboard assigning the ADD a new physical register in the stated design.
[ ] The ADD must wait until cycle twelve solely because writeback occurred in cycle ten.
---
「寫回前 WAW」對：較老寫者尚未釋放目的地。「第十一拍」對：題設明定第十拍寫回後下一拍檢查可用。「第九拍即可」錯：執行完成與寫結果不同，後者才釋放此衝突。「自動改實體名」錯：本記分板沒有換名。「還需第十二拍」錯：沒有明定額外空拍，不能自行多加一拍。

@q il2-07 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
In a classic scoreboard, `SUB f8,f6,f2` cannot yet read both operands because f2 is unavailable; f6 still contains old value 20. A younger `ADD f6,f10,f4` has executed and computed 30. Sources are read together only in the read-operands stage, without early capture. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The ADD must delay writing f6 until the older SUB has read its old f6 source.
[x] This delayed write prevents a WAR hazard.
[x] After the SUB reads both operands, this WAR hazard alone does not require the SUB's execution to finish.
[ ] Writing 30 immediately preserves the SUB's intended old operand under this model.
[ ] The ADD's completed result must be discarded because a younger instruction may never execute first.
---
「等較老讀完」對：SUB 尚未保存二十，提早寫會使它讀成三十。「防 WAR」對：前讀後寫的反相依。「不必再等執行結束」對：讀者取走舊值後，這個名稱限制已解除。「立刻寫安全」錯：與沒有提前捕捉值的題設矛盾。「較年輕不能先執行」錯：記分板允許先算完再卡寫回，並非把已完成工作全部丟掉。

@q il2-08 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 觀念

[x] The table distinguishes source availability from the numeric source value.
[x] After operand reading, $R_j$ becomes false even though the instruction has already obtained its source.
[ ] $Q_j$ is the numeric source value.
[ ] $R_j$ must stay true forever once its source first becomes available.
[ ] If $Q_j$ names unit number two, the source value must equal two.
---
「可用狀態不同於值」對：此表追蹤控制，數值從暫存器讀取。「讀後變假」對：標誌的意思含未讀這個條件。「Q 就數值」錯：它是供應結果的單元識別。「真後永真」錯：完成讀取便不再是可讀未讀。「二號單元就值二」錯：識別編號不決定運算所得的數值。

@q il3-15 | co-ilp#3 | 3 | 比較
Compare a classic scoreboard without renaming with Tomasulo scheduling using reservation-station tags. An older instruction has captured an available old source value under Tomasulo; a younger instruction writes the same architectural register. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both mechanisms can permit out-of-order execution when dependencies allow it.
[x] Tomasulo's saved source version can remove the need to delay the younger write for this WAR hazard.
[x] A scoreboard can delay a write until older instructions have read the old value.
[ ] Tomasulo renaming eliminates every RAW data dependence.
[ ] A classic scoreboard automatically assigns a new register version to every destination at issue.
---
「都能亂序」對：前面的等待不一定阻止已發出且無相依的工作執行。「保存版本解 WAR」對：舊讀者不再依賴同名暫存器的目前內容。「記分板延寫」對：沒有換名，必須等較老讀者取走舊值。「消掉 RAW」錯：需要生產者的真正資料仍得等。「記分板自動新版本」錯：題設的典型記分板沒有換名，名稱衝突以等待處理。

@q il2-13 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
In a classic scoreboard, an issued ADD needs f2 from an active load unit. Its second-source status uses $Q_k$ for the unit that will supply the value and $R_k$ for available-but-not-yet-read. The load has not written back. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $Q_k$ identifies the load unit before writeback.
[x] $R_k$ is false before the load's result becomes available.
[x] Once the value is available and the ADD reads its operands, its available-but-unread flag becomes false again.
[ ] $Q_k$ contains the loaded numeric value before memory returns it.
[ ] A false $R_k$ always proves that the source will never become available.
---
「記載入單元」對：狀態記的是來源單元識別。「尚未可讀為假」對：RAW 尚未滿足。「讀走後再變假」對：R 表示已可讀且未讀，不是永久的結果有效位。「先有數值」錯：Q 是識別，不是資料。「假就永不會好」錯：可能還沒到，也可能已讀走，必須搭配其他狀態解讀。

@q il2-14 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 Scoreboard 在 Issue 階段擋住 WAW 就能保證正確，不需要在後面再檢查。
---
若後一條要寫的暫存器已經是某個未完成指令的目的地，就不讓它發出，所以同一個暫存器任何時刻最多只有一個「待寫者」。之後的寫入一定照發出順序發生，不可能後發先至地被前一條蓋掉。

@q il3-10 | co-ilp#3 | 3 | 情境

@q il3-14 | co-ilp#3 | 3 | 觀念

@q il4-05 | co-ilp#4 | 2 | 觀念
ROB 的每一項通常記錄哪些欄位？
[x] 指令類型、目的地（暫存器或記憶體位址）、結果值、是否已完成
[ ] 只有指令位址
[ ] 保留站的編號
[ ] 分支歷史
---
commit 時把結果寫進目的地，然後移除這一項。

@q il4-06 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 陷阱
有 ROB 的處理器，store 指令什麼時候真的寫進記憶體？
[x] commit 時：之前都只放在 ROB／store 緩衝區，因為還可能被推測錯而丟掉
[ ] Execute 時
[ ] Issue 時
[ ] Write result 時
---
記憶體的修改一旦發生就收不回來，所以一定要等確定。

@q il4-07 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 計算
ROB 有 64 項，每拍發出 4 條。某個 load 在 ROB 最前面卡住（cache miss 200 拍），後面指令都不相依、都很快。大約幾拍後 ROB 滿了、處理器停止發出？
== 16
---
$64/4$。之後要等那個 load 完成、commit，ROB 才有空位。所以 ROB 大小限制了處理器能「蓋過」多長的延遲。

@q il4-08 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 情境
In a ROB-value design, committed r1 is five. An uncommitted ADD has written nine into its ROB entry, and a younger dependent instruction can obtain that result. Before the ADD commits, an older fault causes its speculative work to be discarded. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Committed r1 can remain five throughout this sequence.
[x] A younger instruction can have used nine speculatively without making nine committed.
[x] Discarding the ADD's uncommitted result prevents it from becoming an architectural update.
[ ] Write-result necessarily changed committed r1 to nine before the fault.
[ ] Correct recovery must preserve the younger result solely because it already consumed nine.
---
「正式值仍五」對：本設計在提交前只存重排項目。「年輕可推測用九」對：轉送不等於提交。「丟未交結果」對：較老故障使此推測版本不得生效。「寫結果已改正式 r1」錯：混淆兩個階段。「用了就必留」錯：相依於被撤銷工作的年輕結果也必須避免提交。

@q il4-09 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 情境
A branch at ROB position three resolves to target address 800, but fetch had followed predicted address 400. Positions one and two are valid older instructions; four and five are uncommitted wrong-path work. Use recovery that keeps valid older work. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Fetch should be redirected to address 800.
[x] Entries four and five must not commit their wrong-path results.
[ ] Entries one and two must be discarded solely because their position precedes the branch.
[ ] Recovery should fetch from 1200 by adding the predicted and actual target addresses.
[ ] Entry five can commit if its execution completed before the branch resolved.
---
「取八百」對：實際目標決定正確路徑。「四五不得提交」對：兩者屬錯路徑。「較老反而必丟」錯：本政策保留有效老工作。「目標相加一千二」錯：重新導向不是加總兩個位址。「先完成就可交」錯：結果可用不代表程式應執行它，完成與提交要分開。

@q il4-10 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 情境
A speculative wrong-path load leaves a cache line resident but never commits. After architectural recovery, that line takes ten cycles to access; an absent line takes 100 cycles. A secret-dependent choice selects which of two lines is touched speculatively. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The cache timing difference can reveal information despite discarded architectural results.
[x] In this model the resident-line access is 90 cycles faster than the absent-line access.
[ ] Precise architectural recovery necessarily restores every cache entry to its earlier state.
[ ] Information leakage requires the wrong-path load to commit its register result.
[ ] Both lines must have identical measured access times after recovery under the stated model.
---
「可從時間洩漏」對：錯路徑結果撤銷，留下的快取狀態仍可被觀察。「差九十拍」對：100−10。「架構恢復必復原所有快取」錯：精確狀態保證不包含此項，題設正保留一條快取線。「一定要提交才洩漏」錯：推測造成的微架構效果就可能形成通道。「時間必相同」錯：直接違反兩種命中狀態的已知時間。這是 Spectre 類問題的重要區別。

@q il4-11 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 比較
Compare two specified renaming designs. In one, result values reside in ROB entries and are copied into committed architectural registers at commit. In the other, values reside in a physical register file, and commit updates the committed mapping. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The second design need not copy every result value into a separate committed register file at commit.
[x] Both designs can preserve in-order architectural commitment.
[x] A physical destination may contain an uncommitted speculative value before its instruction commits.
[ ] Merely placing a value in a physical register makes it architecturally committed.
[ ] The first design cannot assign different speculative versions to the same architectural destination.
---
「不必逐值搬到另一檔」對：值已存在實體暫存器，提交可更新已提交的對應。「兩者可循序提交」對：資料存放位置不決定架構提交順序。「實體先有推測值」對：寫實體值與更新已提交狀態分開。「有值就已提交」錯：還可能因更老例外或錯路徑而丟棄。「重排設計不能換名」錯：不同項目編號可以代表同一架構目的地的不同版本。

@q il4-12 | co-ilp#4 | 2 | 計算
ROB 最前面那條是分支，它已確定預測錯誤；ROB 裡它後面還有 23 條指令。要丟掉幾條？
== 23
---
分支本身是對的指令（照常 commit），後面的都是走錯路徑取來的。

@q il4-13 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 追蹤
Three ROB entries, oldest first, have states: instruction one unfinished, instruction two result available, instruction three result available. The machine commits at most one instruction per cycle in order. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] No instruction can commit while instruction one remains unfinished.
[x] Instructions two and three may already have finished execution without being committed.
[x] Once all are eligible, committing all three requires at least three commit cycles.
[ ] Instruction three can commit first because its result is available.
[ ] A single commit cycle can retire all three under the stated one-per-cycle limit.
---
「頭未好不能提交」對：不能跳過最老項目。「後兩可先算完」對：亂序執行與循序提交分開。「至少三提交拍」對：頻寬每拍一條。「第三先提交」錯：違反順序。「一拍全交」錯：超過明定頻寬，不應把結果同時可用當作可無限提交。

@q il4-14 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 證明
說明 ROB 為什麼能同時支援推測執行和精確例外。
---
所有指令依程式順序放進 ROB，可以亂序完成，但只有在最前面而且確定（分支已解決、沒有例外）時才 commit、改變暫存器或記憶體。遇到例外或預測錯誤時，只要把該指令（例外）或分支之後的項目清掉，架構狀態就恰好等於「前面的都做完、後面的都沒做」，這正是精確例外的定義，也讓推測錯誤能完全還原。

@q il5-05 | co-ilp#5 | 2 | 計算
4 發射的 SMT 處理器，兩個執行緒單獨跑時平均每拍各用 1.5 和 1.2 個發射槽。一起跑（不互相干擾的理想情況）時發射槽使用率？
== 67.5%
== 0.675
---
$(1.5+1.2)/4$。單獨跑時最多只用 37.5%，SMT 讓空著的槽給另一個執行緒用。

@q il5-06 | co-ilp#5 | 2 | 觀念
VLIW 的缺點，下列哪個正確？

[ ] 硬體很複雜
[ ] 不能平行
[ ] 只能跑一條執行緒
---
硬體反而簡單：排程工作交給編譯器。

@q il5-07 | co-ilp#5 | 3 | 情境
A GPU scheduler can choose a ready warp while another warp is waiting for a memory result. In this teaching model, issuing a common arithmetic instruction takes one cycle and performs useful work for the warp's active threads. Changing the selected warp has no additional cost because the required execution state is already resident. These assumptions describe the experiment, not a promise that every GPU request has identical latency.

Warp R issues a memory request at time zero and cannot continue until cycle 100. Warp T has exactly 80 ready arithmetic instructions, and no instruction in T depends on R. With only these two warps, execute T during R's wait and then consider how much of that interval remains without ready work. In a separate variant, a third warp has thirty additional ready instructions. Keep the same single common-instruction issue position; having 32 threads per warp does not multiply its cycle budget. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] All eighty instructions of T can fit inside R's hundred-cycle wait.
[x] With no third warp, twenty cycles of the wait remain after T finishes.
[ ] The wait must become 3200 cycles solely because warp R contains 32 threads.
[ ] Running T supplies the missing memory value to R without the memory request returning.
[ ] All eighty instructions of T plus thirty instructions of the third warp fit into the same hundred cycles on the stated issue unit.
---
「八十道放得下」對：一拍一道，八十不超過一百。「剩二十」對：100−80＝20，沒有其他就緒工作便仍會空著。「32緒把等待乘32」錯：題設是一個已定一百拍的群組記憶體事件。「跑T就補R資料」錯：兩者工作獨立，不會無中生有。「八十加三十全藏進一百」錯：總110道需110個發射拍，前一百拍最多容納其中一百道。

@q il5-08 | co-ilp#5 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「開了 SMT（兩個硬體執行緒），效能就加倍。」錯在哪？
[x] 兩個執行緒共用同一套執行單元和 cache，只是把空閒的資源用起來；通常只多 10%～30%，搶資源時甚至變慢
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 一定會變慢
[ ] 效能會變四倍
---
效能增加多少取決於單一執行緒原本浪費多少資源。

@q il5-09 | co-ilp#5 | 2 | 觀念
A teaching description of a GPU distinguishes two observation levels before applying Flynn's classification. At the smaller level, one selected warp receives a single common instruction and applies it to 32 separate data elements. Every active execution position performs that operation on its own element. The description intentionally uses a SIMD-like model for this one instruction step; it does not claim that the entire software programming model is identical to a traditional SIMD instruction set.

At the larger level, four such warps have independent instruction-control states and may execute different instruction sequences. Each arithmetic data value occupies 32 bits. An engineer proposes changing the arithmetic values to 64 bits without changing the number of warps or the control organization. Classify the specified streams, keeping the number of bits in a value separate from the number of instruction streams or data streams. State explicitly which observation level each conclusion refers to. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The smaller-level common instruction acting on separate elements is SIMD-like.
[x] Changing a value from 32 bits to 64 bits does not by itself add an instruction stream.
[ ] Having multiple data elements makes the smaller-level description MISD rather than SIMD-like.
[ ] Four independently controlled warp necessarily share exactly one instruction stream at the larger level.
[ ] The number 32 in the data word width proves that every warp has 32 independently selected opcodes during the same common instruction step.
---
「小層次像SIMD」對：一道指令作用於多筆資料，題設明定此觀察範圍。「字寬不增指令流」對：32改64只改每筆資料位數。「多資料便MISD」錯：MISD是多指令單資料，方向顛倒。「四個獨立控制仍必單指令流」錯：觀察整體時有多個獨立序列。「字寬32代表32個opcode」錯：資料位數、lane數與指令控制流是三種不同數量。

影像每個像素都做同樣的亮度調整。最適合哪種平行？
[x] SIMD（一條指令同時處理很多像素，例如 AVX 指令或 GPU）
[ ] MISD
[ ] coarse-grained 多執行緒
[ ] 單執行緒
---

@q il5-11 | co-ilp#5 | 3 | 比較
A simplified GPU selects one ready warp for one common instruction in each cycle. Warp R issues a load at cycle 0. Its next instruction adds one to that load's result, which becomes available only at the beginning of cycle ten. The scheduler may not manufacture an input or execute this dependent addition using another warp's register value. Each warp retains its own program and register state.

Warp T has three ready common additions with all 32 lanes active. It can issue those additions at cycles 1, 2 and 3 while R remains unable to issue its dependent instruction. Assume no memory interference or other resource restriction blocks T. The scheduler's choice concerns which ready warp to run, rather than moving R's dependent instruction before its load. Do not infer a synchronization operation between warps merely from the fact that execution resources were reassigned. Track the distinction between hiding an unavailable warp's delay and changing its required data dependence. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] R's dependent addition cannot issue before its input becomes available at cycle ten.
[x] Scheduling T permits useful common additions during cycles one through three.
[ ] Selecting T retroactively cancels R's outstanding memory request and removes its need for a result.
[ ] A change of selected warp automatically completes a GPU-wide synchronization for all threads.
[ ] During one common instruction step, every lane is required to choose a different opcode independently of the selected instruction.
---
「R需等第十拍」對：真正輸入尚未可用，排程不能消除它。「一至三拍可做T工作」對：換成其他就緒組來利用空檔。「自動取消R請求」錯：排程選別組不會改寫原程式語意或刪掉必要讀取。「換組等於全GPU同步」錯：資源重選不是同步原語。「共同一步每lane必不同opcode」錯：本模型一步是一道共同指令，對各lane的資料分別運算。

@q il5-14 | co-ilp#5 | 3 | 計算
A simplified GPU warp contains 32 threads with lane identifiers zero through thirty-one. The program tests whether the identifier is less than 8. The first eight threads follow a path containing 12 common arithmetic instructions, while the remaining twenty-four follow a path containing 4. Every instruction on a path performs one useful scalar operation for each thread that chose that path. Both paths must produce their specified results.

For this exercise, the warp executes the two paths serially, with inactive lanes disabled while the other path runs. A common instruction step occupies all 32 possible lane positions even when some positions are inactive. Ignore the test, reconvergence, memory, and scheduling overheads. Instructions are neither removed nor combined by a compiler. Compute common instruction steps, useful lane operations, total possible lane positions, and utilization over the two paths. This is the stated timing model, not an assumption that all current GPU implementations have identical scheduling behavior. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The two paths require sixteen common instruction steps.
[x] The useful lane-operation count is 192.
[x] Useful utilization over the two paths is 37.5 percent.
[x] There are 320 inactive lane positions over those steps.
[ ] The path taken by fewer threads may be discarded entirely because only the majority path is required to produce correct results.
---
「十六共同步」對：12＋4＝16。「有用192」對：8×12＋24×4＝96＋96＝192。「37.5%」對：總位置32×16＝512，192/512＝0.375。「停用320格」對：512−192＝320。「可丟少數路徑」錯：八條執行緒也有自己的正確結果需求，遮罩只控制哪一路在當下工作，不是多數表決刪掉程式語意。

@q vc1-05 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 2 | 計算
