@q vc1-17 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 3 | 情境
A VIPT cache has 8 KiB ways, and pages are 4 KiB, so its set index uses bit 0 of the virtual page number as well as page-offset bits. The hardware does not handle aliases. A process maps the virtual pages 0x3 and 0x6 to the same physical page 0x51; bit 0 of 0x3 is 1 and bit 0 of 0x6 is 0. The operating system considers a rule: when it maps a virtual page to a physical page, bit 0 of the physical page number must equal bit 0 of the virtual page number. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Without the rule, the two virtual pages above can place copies of the same data in different sets.
[x] The rule forbids mapping virtual page 0x6 to physical page 0x51.
[x] With the rule, all virtual addresses of one physical page share one set index.
[x] With the rule, the hardware needs no extra logic for aliases.
[ ] Flushing the TLB at every context switch would also remove the aliases from the cache.
---
「沒有規則時會落在不同的 set」對：index 用到虛擬頁號的位元 0，0x3 是 1、0x6 是 0。

「規則不允許 0x6 對到 0x51」對：0x6 的位元 0 是 0，0x51 的位元 0 是 1。

「同一個實體頁只有一個 index」對：所有對到它的虛擬頁，位元 0 都等於實體頁號的位元 0，索引的每一位都一樣。這種做法叫 page coloring。

「硬體不必另外處理」對。

「清 TLB 也能消除」錯：清 TLB 不會碰到 cache 裡的資料，和別名無關。

@q vc1-18 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 3 | 計算
沒有資料 cache。TLB 1 ns、命中率 98%；記憶體 100 ns；頁表 4 層（TLB miss 要讀 4 次記憶體）。平均每次存取多少 ns？
== 109
---
命中：$1+100=101$；miss：$1+4\times100+100=501$。$0.98\times101+0.02\times501=109$。多層頁表讓 TLB miss 很貴。

@q vc1-19 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 2 | 找錯
On a 2 GHz processor, a TLB miss whose translation is found by a hardware page-table walk costs about 30 cycles, while a page fault that must read the page from a disk takes about 5 ms. A student, seeing many TLB misses in a profile, says: "A TLB miss means that a page fault has occurred, so this program is reading the disk all the time." Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] On a TLB miss, a page-table walk usually finds the translation.
[x] A page fault means the page is not in memory, so the operating system must load it from the disk.
[ ] Every TLB miss is handled by the operating system loading the page from the disk into memory.
[ ] A page fault costs about as much as a TLB miss, since both involve one table lookup.
[ ] For a given page, TLB misses can happen only right after a page fault on that page.
---
「TLB miss 查頁表通常就找到」對：轉換只是不在 TLB，約 30 拍。

「page fault 要從磁碟載入」對：頁不在記憶體裡。

「每次 TLB miss 都讀磁碟」錯：大多數 TLB miss 不用碰磁碟，學生說錯了。

「兩者代價差不多」錯：5 ms 在 2 GHz 下是 $10^7$ 拍，比 30 拍多了好幾個數量級。

「只在 page fault 之後才 miss」錯：頁一直在記憶體裡，也會因為 TLB 項目有限、被其他轉換擠掉而 miss。

@q vc1-20 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 2 | 陷阱
A processor has a 64-entry TLB and a 32 KiB first-level data cache that is physically indexed and physically tagged. During a profiling run, a load instruction finds its virtual page number in the TLB, so the physical address is available after 1 cycle, and the cache is then accessed with that physical address. The profiler reports a TLB hit rate of 99% and a cache hit rate of 95% for the program, and a student tries to deduce what one TLB hit says about the cache access that follows. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] The data may or may not be in the cache.
[ ] The data must be in the cache, since the TLB holds only recently used translations.
[ ] The data cannot be in the cache, since a TLB hit and a cache hit never happen together.
[ ] Whenever the cache is physically tagged, a TLB hit guarantees a cache hit.
[ ] After a TLB hit, the page table must still be read to confirm the translation.
---
TLB 只負責位址轉換，資料是否在 cache 是另一回事，「可能在、也可能不在」。轉換最近用過不代表資料還在 cache；兩者也可以同時 hit；cache 用實體 tag 也一樣。TLB hit 就直接拿到實體頁號，不必再查頁表。

@q vc2-17 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 3 | 追蹤
MESI，兩顆 CPU 一開始都沒有 X。依序：P1 寫 X、P2 讀 X、P2 寫 X、P1 寫 X。最後 P1、P2 的狀態？區塊在兩個 cache 之間直接傳了幾次？
== M,I,2
?? P1,P2,傳送次數
---
P1 寫：M。P2 讀：P1 提供資料，兩者 S。P2 寫：invalidate P1，P2 M（自己已有資料，不用傳）。P1 寫：P2 提供資料後變 I，P1 M。傳送發生在第 2、4 步。

@q vc2-18 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 2 | 情境
兩個執行緒各自不停加自己的 int 計數器，兩個計數器宣告在相鄰位置，區塊 64 bytes。要消除 false sharing，兩個計數器的起點至少要相隔幾 bytes？
== 64
---
不在同一個區塊才不會互相 invalidate；常見寫法是在中間補空白或讓每個計數器對齊到 64 bytes。

@q vc2-19 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 2 | 觀念
directory 協定中，每個記憶體區塊的狀態通常是哪三種？
[x] 沒有 cache 有（Uncached）、一或多個 cache 有乾淨的副本（Shared）、恰好一個 cache 有而且可能改過（Exclusive／Modified）
[ ] M、E、S、I 四種
[ ] 讀、寫、執行
[ ] 只有有效和無效
---
目錄另外記錄誰有副本，才能只送訊息給相關的處理器。

@q vc2-20 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼一致性協定必須保證「對同一位址的寫入有唯一的全域順序」（write serialization）。
---
若兩顆 CPU 先後寫入 1 和 2，P3 看到「1 再 2」而 P4 看到「2 再 1」，兩者最後對這個位址的值意見不同，程式就無法推理共享資料的狀態。invalidate 協定讓寫入前必須取得獨佔權，同一時間只有一個寫者，寫入自然排成一個順序，所有讀者都照這個順序看到結果。

@q vc3-14 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 3 | 計算
load buffering 測試：P1 `r1=y; x=1;`、P2 `r2=x; y=1;`（初始 0）。SC 下 $(r1,r2)$ 有幾種可能的結果？$(1,1)$ 可能嗎？
== 3,不可能
?? 結果數,(1,1)
---
可能 (0,0)、(0,1)、(1,0)。$(1,1)$ 需要兩邊的讀都看到對方「之後」才寫的值，形成環。TSO 也不允許；ARM 這類弱模型理論上允許。

@q vc3-15 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 2 | 計算
三個執行緒各有 2 個操作（各自順序固定），SC 下總共有幾種交錯？
== 90
---
$\frac{6!}{2!\,2!\,2!}=90$。執行緒或操作稍多，可能性就爆炸，所以要靠模型規則推理，不能一一列舉。

@q vc3-16 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 3 | 陷阱
C 語言把共享變數宣告成 `volatile`，就能保證不同執行緒之間的讀寫順序嗎？
[x] 不能：volatile 只阻止編譯器把存取省略或留在暫存器，不阻止硬體重排，也不保證讀改寫不被打斷；要用 C11 提供的同步型別或鎖
[ ] 能
[ ] 只在 x86 上能
[ ] volatile 會讓程式變成單執行緒
---
這是很常見的誤解；Java 的 volatile 語意不同，有提供順序保證。

@q vc3-17 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 2 | 觀念
Thread 1 and thread 2 share the variables `x`, `y`, `z`, and `flag`, all initially 0:
```
thread 1:  x = 1;  y = 2;  release-store(flag, 1);  z = 3;
thread 2:  r0 = acquire-load(flag);  r1 = x;  r2 = z;
```
A release store keeps every earlier memory access of its thread from moving after it, and an acquire load keeps every later memory access of its thread from moving before it. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The write `x = 1` cannot be moved after the release store.
[x] The read `r1 = x` cannot be moved before the acquire load.
[ ] The write `z = 3` cannot be moved before the release store.
[ ] If `r0` is 1, then `r2` must be 3.
[ ] Acquire and release forbid every reordering of any two memory accesses.
---
「`x = 1` 不能移到 release 之後」對：release 擋住「前面的往後跑」。

「`r1 = x` 不能移到 acquire 之前」對：acquire 擋住「後面的往前跑」。所以 `r0` 讀到 1 時，`r1` 一定讀到 1：取得鎖用 acquire、放開鎖用 release，中間的存取就跑不出去。

「`z = 3` 不能移到 release 之前」錯：release 不管它後面的存取往前移。

「`r0` 是 1 時 `r2` 一定是 3」錯：`z = 3` 寫在 release 之後，release 不保證它已經被看到。

「禁止所有重排」錯：兩者都只擋一個方向。

@q vc3-18 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「在 TSO 機器上，用 pthread 的鎖保護的程式還要自己加 mfence 才正確。」錯在哪？
[x] 鎖的實作本身已經包含必要的特殊指令和順序保證；正確加鎖的程式不用另外處理
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] TSO 不需要鎖
[ ] 鎖只在單核心有效
---
自己加 fence 主要出現在不用鎖的寫法（例如旗子同步、無鎖資料結構）。

@q vc3-19 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 3 | 情境
A server creates a configuration object lazily with double-checked locking, so that the lock is taken only the first time:
```
if (p == NULL) {
    lock(m);
    if (p == NULL) p = new Config();   // constructor fills 20 fields
    unlock(m);
}
use(p->timeout);
```
On a machine with a weak memory model, which may reorder ordinary writes that go to different addresses, a thread occasionally reads `p` as non-null but sees `p->timeout` still 0, its value before initialization. The developers suspect that the writes to the object's fields and the write to `p` can become visible to other cores in a different order than they were issued. A reviewer suggests removing the second check inside the lock to simplify the code, and another suggests writing `p` with release semantics and reading it with acquire semantics. Which of the following statements about running it on a weak memory model are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Another thread may see `p` as non-null but see the object's fields not yet initialized.
[x] This happens because the writes to the fields and the write to `p` may be reordered.
[x] Writing `p` with release semantics and reading it with acquire semantics fixes the problem.
[ ] The second check inside the lock is unnecessary, since the first check already tests `p`.
[ ] The problem appears only on machines with sequential consistency, not on weak models.
---
「寫物件內容和寫 `p` 可能被重排」，另一個執行緒在沒加鎖的第一次檢查時看到 `p` 非空，卻「看到物件還沒初始化完」；所以 `p` 要「release 寫、acquire 讀」。第二次檢查不能省：兩條執行緒可能都通過第一次檢查，靠它才不會建兩個物件。SC 下不會重排，問題正是出在弱記憶體模型。

@q vc3-20 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 2 | 比較
A processor company must choose a memory model for its next 8-core design. Under sequential consistency (SC), a simple implementation must make each write visible to all cores before the core's later accesses proceed, which can cost about 100 cycles per write, and about 30% of all instructions are writes in typical programs. Under total store order (TSO), each write waits in a store buffer while later reads continue, which hides most of that latency. The company's compiler team notes that most programs, which use locks from a standard library, would run unchanged, and that only a few places in the library need ordering instructions. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] TSO lets writes wait in a store buffer while reads continue, hiding write latency.
[x] A simple SC implementation must make each write visible before later accesses proceed.
[x] The cost is that programmers must add ordering instructions in a few places.
[x] x86 processors provide TSO.
[ ] SC cannot be implemented in hardware at all.
---
TSO「讓寫入在 store buffer 等、讀繼續做」，隱藏寫入延遲；「簡單的 SC 實作每次寫都要先對所有人可見」。代價是「少數地方要自己加排序指令」。「x86 就是 TSO」。SC 做得出來，只是慢。

@q vc4-13 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 計算
NUMA：本地 100 ns、遠端 300 ns。要讓平均存取時間不超過 150 ns，本地存取至少要佔多少？
== 75%
== 0.75
---
$100p+300(1-p)\le150\Rightarrow p\ge0.75$。

@q vc4-14 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 計算
ticket lock：`next_ticket`＝5、`now_serving`＝3（3 號正拿著鎖）。新來的執行緒拿到幾號？前面還有幾個要先拿到鎖？
== 5,2
?? 號碼,前面幾個
---
它拿 5 號（之後 next_ticket 變 6）；3 號正在用，4 號在等，輪到它之前有 3、4 兩個。

@q vc4-15 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 比較
ticket lock 和單純 test-and-set 自旋鎖相比？
[x] ticket lock 照拿號順序給鎖，公平、不會有人一直搶不到；test-and-set 誰搶到算誰的，可能有人一直等
[ ] test-and-set 比較公平
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] ticket lock 不需要原子指令
---
拿號時要用 fetch-and-add 這類原子操作。

@q vc4-16 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 3 | 情境
A lock-free stack uses a compare-and-swap operation on its top pointer. Three allocated nodes have addresses 0x100, 0x200 and 0x300. Initially the top is 0x100, whose saved next node is 0x200. A paused thread remembers these two addresses and intends to replace the top by the saved next node. Meanwhile, another thread changes the top from 0x100 to 0x200, then to 0x300, and finally back to 0x100. The nodes remain allocated throughout this trace; returning to the same address does not restore the old logical history.

Consider two alternative protections. A tagged top stores a pointer together with a version that starts at 41 and increases on every successful top change. No counter wrap occurs. A second design uses an LL/SC reservation that is invalidated by every intervening write to the monitored top. Evaluate the paused thread's stale observation under these specified models. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A pointer-only CAS can miss the intervening top changes when the address bits return to 0x100.
[x] A version stored with the pointer can distinguish the two observations.
[x] The version after the three intervening changes is 44 rather than 41.
[x] The specified LL/SC reservation logic invalidates the paused thread's reservation during the intervening writes.
[x] The trace demonstrates that equal pointer values do not establish equal logical histories.
---
「只比指標可漏掉變動」對：舊值與新值的位址同為 0x100，指標 CAS 看不出中間三次變化。「版本可區分」對：必須連版本一同比較，題設也排除了繞回。「版本四十四」對：41＋3＝44，與暫停前不同。「LL/SC 記錄失效」對：指定模型監看中間寫入，即使值回原樣也已破壞保留。「同值不等於同歷史」對：這是 ABA 的核心，本例甚至不用依賴釋放後位址再利用。

@q vc4-17 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 3 | 觀念
MCS 鎖讓每個等待者在「自己的」節點上自旋。好處？
[x] 每個等待者只讀自己 cache 裡的旗子，放鎖時只通知下一個，一致性流量不會隨等待者數量變大
[ ] 讓鎖不需要原子指令
[ ] 讓等待者不用等
[ ] 只對單核心有用
---
大家在同一個變數上自旋時，放鎖那一刻所有人一起 miss、一起搶。

@q vc4-18 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「自旋鎖在單核心系統上也很有效率。」錯在哪？
[x] 單核心時拿著鎖的執行緒不在跑，自旋的那個只是把時間片浪費掉；應該直接讓出處理器（睡眠）
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 單核心不需要鎖
[ ] 自旋鎖只能用在單核心
---
自旋只在「鎖很快會被別的核心放開」時才划算。

@q vc4-19 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 計算
LL/SC 迴圈每次嘗試有 20% 機率失敗（彼此獨立）。平均要試幾次才成功？
== 1.25
---
成功機率 0.8，幾何分布的期望次數 $1/0.8$。

@q vc4-20 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 1 | 觀念
Two x86-64 cores update a shared eight-byte counter in ordinary cacheable write-back memory. The counter is naturally aligned, initially contains nine, and lies entirely inside one cache line. Each core executes one locked read-modify-write addition that adds one to this memory operand. A third core accesses an unrelated variable in a different cache line. Assume the two additions complete successfully and that there are no faults or other writes to the counter.

The code uses the LOCK prefix on the memory addition, rather than attempting to attach it to an ordinary register-to-register computation. Analyze both the atomic modification of the counter and the memory-ordering effect of the locked operation. Do not confuse ordering of memory operations with stopping the entire machine, and do not infer that atomic operations bypass all cache structures. The separate variable has no dependence on either increment. Which statements are correct under the ordinary x86-64 locked-operation rules? Select all that apply.
[x] The locked addition makes its memory read-modify-write atomic.
[x] The locked operation supplies a full memory-ordering barrier for ordinary loads and stores.
[ ] The LOCK prefix speeds the addition up by making it bypass every cache.
[ ] The third core must stop accessing every unrelated memory location until the addition finishes.
[ ] After both additions complete, the counter must be ten because one increment is necessarily lost.
---
「讀改寫原子」對：兩個增量不會被切成會遺失更新的普通讀與寫。「普通讀寫的完整排序屏障」對：這裡限定正常可快取記憶體的 locked 操作，不把它說成所有種類的指令序列化。「跳過所有快取必加速」錯：現代實作一般使用快取一致性保護相關區塊，並非如此繞過快取。「無關位址全停」錯：不會把所有其他核心的所有記憶體操作凍結。「最後十」錯：九加兩次原子一，最後十一。

@q pf2-16 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
三個程式執行時間 10、20、40 秒，使用頻率權重 0.5、0.3、0.2。加權算術平均時間？
== 19
---
$5+6+8$。權重要反映真實的使用情況，否則平均沒意義。

@q pf2-17 | co-perf#2 | 1 | 計算
三個程式的 SPEC ratio 是 2、8、4。幾何平均？
== 4
---
$(2\times8\times4)^{1/3}=64^{1/3}=4$。

@q pf2-18 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 陷阱
Two machines, $X$ and $Y$, have different instruction sets, and a vendor advertises $X$ as faster because its MIPS rating is higher. For the same program, compiled separately for each machine, the measurements are:

| machine | instructions executed | MIPS on this program |
|---|---|---|
| $X$ | $6 \times 10^9$ | 2000 |
| $Y$ | $2 \times 10^9$ | 1000 |

Here MIPS is the number of instructions executed per second, divided by $10^6$. A reviewer must explain to customers which machine actually finishes the program sooner, and why the vendor's comparison misleads. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The program takes 3 s on $X$ and 2 s on $Y$.
[x] $Y$ is 1.5 times as fast as $X$ on this program, even though its MIPS rating is lower.
[ ] A machine with a higher MIPS rating is always faster, even when the instruction sets differ, because it completes more instructions per second.
[x] Comparing MIPS ratings is meaningful only when both machines execute the same instructions for the program.
[ ] The MIPS rating of a machine is the same for every program it runs.
---
「$X$ 3 秒、$Y$ 2 秒」對：時間 $=$ 指令數 $\div$（MIPS $\times10^6$）：$6\times10^9/(2\times10^9)=3$、$2\times10^9/10^9=2$。

「$Y$ 快 1.5 倍」對：$3/2$。$X$ 的指令比較簡單，要多做三倍的指令，MIPS 高只是因為每條指令做得少。

「MIPS 高一定快」錯：上面就是反例。

「只在同樣的指令時有意義」對：同指令集、同程式時，指令數相同，MIPS 才跟時間成反比。

「每個程式的 MIPS 都一樣」錯：MIPS $=$ 時脈 $\div$（CPI $\times10^6$），不同程式的指令組成不同，CPI 不同，MIPS 也不同。

@q pf2-19 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 情境
A vendor reports that its new processor runs a 20-line loop that sums 1000 integers 3 times as fast as its old processor, and claims that real programs will also run 3 times as fast. Reviewers instead use a suite of real applications such as SPEC, which includes compilers, compression programs, and scientific simulations, each running for minutes and touching hundreds of megabytes of memory. The reviewers also want results that other labs can reproduce. Why do performance evaluations use suites of real applications instead of a single small loop? Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A small kernel can be specially optimized by compilers, or even computed away entirely.
[x] A small loop may fit entirely in the cache, so it does not reflect the memory behavior of real programs.
[ ] Real applications are used because they finish faster than small loops.
[x] Even with a standard suite, vendors may tune their compilers for the suite, so the reported conditions matter.
[ ] A single small loop is enough as long as it is run many times, for example a million times, to reduce measurement noise.
---
「小程式容易被特別最佳化或整個算掉」對：編譯器可能在編譯時就算出結果，或針對這段程式加特殊處理，量到的不是處理器本身。

「小迴圈整個塞進 cache」對：真實程式的 cache 失誤、分支行為都量不到。

「真實程式跑得比較快」錯：真實程式通常比較久；用它是為了代表性，不是速度。

「廠商仍可能針對測試集調編譯器」對：所以 SPEC 要求公布編譯選項等條件。

「跑很多次就夠」錯：重複只能降低量測雜訊，不能讓一個不具代表性的程式變得有代表性。

@q pf2-20 | co-perf#2 | 3 | 找錯
Two programs take 5 s and 200 s on machine $X$, and 20 s and 50 s on machine $Y$. A student normalizes each time by a reference machine (time divided by the reference time) and compares the arithmetic means of the normalized times. Reference $R'$ takes 20 s on each program, and reference $R''$ takes 1 s and 1000 s. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With $R'$, the means are 5.125 for $X$ and 1.75 for $Y$, so $Y$ looks faster.
[x] With $R''$, the means are 2.6 for $X$ and about 10.0 for $Y$, so $X$ looks faster.
[x] The geometric means of the normalized times of $X$ and $Y$ are equal to each other for every choice of reference machine.
[ ] Using the harmonic mean of the normalized times instead makes the ranking independent of the reference machine.
[ ] Comparing the total execution times of the two programs also gives a ranking that depends on the reference machine.
---
「$R'$ 下 5.125 對 1.75」對：$X$：$(5/20+200/20)/2=5.125$；$Y$：$(20/20+50/20)/2=1.75$。

「$R''$ 下 2.6 對約 10.0」對：$X$：$(5+0.2)/2=2.6$；$Y$：$(20+0.05)/2\approx10.0$。換一台參考機器，排名整個反過來，這就是學生的問題。

「幾何平均永遠相等」對：$X$ 是 $\sqrt{5\cdot200}/\sqrt{r_1r_2}$、$Y$ 是 $\sqrt{20\cdot50}/\sqrt{r_1r_2}$，分子都是 $\sqrt{1000}$；參考機器只出現在共同的分母，比值時約掉。

「改用調和平均就不受影響」錯：$R'$ 下調和平均 $X$ 約 0.49、$Y$ 約 1.43（$X$ 看起來快）；$R''$ 下 $X$ 約 0.38、$Y$ 約 0.10（$Y$ 看起來快），一樣會翻。

「總時間也受參考機器影響」錯：總時間 $X$ 205 秒、$Y$ 70 秒，根本沒用到參考機器。要代表實際工作負載時，總時間（或加權總時間）最直接。

@q pf3-17 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 計算
90% 可平行。用 $n$ 核心時每個核心額外有 0.01 的協調開銷（以原始時間為 1），時間＝$0.1+\frac{0.9}{n}+0.01n$。$n=10$ 的加速多少？
== 3.45
---
$1/(0.1+0.09+0.1)\approx3.45$。開銷隨核心數成長，核心再多反而變慢；這個例子最好約在 $n=\sqrt{90}\approx9.5$。

@q pf3-18 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 計算
Gustafson：序列部分佔 5%（以平行後的時間量），32 核心的擴展加速？
== 30.45
---
$32-0.05\times31=30.45$。問題規模跟著核心數變大時，加速可以接近核心數。

@q pf3-19 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 陷阱
A research team parallelizes a data-processing program and reports a speedup of 9 on 8 cores over the single-core version, measured on a data set of 64 MB. Each core of the machine has a private 8 MB cache, so on one core most of the data set misses in the cache, while on 8 cores each core's share fits almost entirely in its own cache. A reviewer of the paper is skeptical, because Amdahl's law predicts a speedup of at most 8 on 8 cores, and suspects either a measurement error or a weak baseline. The authors must answer the review before the paper can be accepted. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This can happen when each core's share of the data fits in its own cache.
[ ] This is impossible, so the measurement must be wrong.
[x] Amdahl's law alone cannot explain a speedup larger than the number of cores.
[x] A poorly optimized single-core baseline can also make the reported speedup look larger.
[ ] A speedup above the number of cores proves that the single-core version contains a bug.
---
「每顆的資料塞進自己的 cache」對：這叫超線性加速，通常來自記憶體階層：總 cache 容量隨核心數變大，miss 大幅減少。

「不可能」錯：如上，有真實的原因。

「Amdahl 解釋不了」對：Amdahl 假設每部分的速度不變，最多只能到核心數倍；超過的部分來自「每單位工作變快了」。

「基準版本沒最佳化好也會灌水」對：比較加速比時要用最好的循序版本當基準。

「證明單核版有 bug」錯：cache 效應就是合理的解釋，不代表有 bug。

@q pf3-20 | co-perf#3 | 2 | 計算
4 顆核心分到的工作量是全部的 30%、25%、25%、20%（沒有序列部分）。加速多少？
== 3.33
---
要等最慢的那顆做完：時間是原來的 0.3，加速 $1/0.3$。負載不平均也是加速打折的原因。

@q pl3-17 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 陷阱
分支模式「成立、成立、不成立、不成立」重複 3 次（共 12 次）。2-bit 飽和計數器（一開始強成立）和 1-bit（一開始預測成立）各錯幾次？
== 8,5
?? 2-bit,1-bit
---

@q pl3-18 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 觀念
Consider the code
```
if (x == 0) y = 1;      /* branch b1 */
if (x != 0) z = 1;      /* branch b2 */
```
which runs inside a loop over an input in which `x` is zero about half of the time, in no regular pattern. The compiler turns each `if` into a conditional branch that skips the assignment, so the outcome of $b_2$ is always the opposite of the outcome of $b_1$ in the same iteration. Two predictors are compared: a plain 2-bit predictor indexed only by the branch address, and a correlating (global-history) predictor that also uses the outcomes of the most recent branches. The loop runs $10^6$ times, so each predictor has plenty of time to train. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A correlating predictor combines recent branch outcomes with the branch address.
[x] Once trained, a correlating predictor predicts $b_2$ correctly every time.
[ ] A correlating predictor uses the values stored in registers to make its predictions.
[x] In a $(2, 2)$ predictor, the outcomes of the last 2 branches select one of 4 two-bit counters for each branch.
[ ] A plain 2-bit predictor indexed only by the branch address can also capture how the outcome of $b_2$ depends on that of $b_1$.
---
「用最近幾個分支的結果加上分支位址」對：全域歷史是一串位元，記錄最近幾個分支跳或不跳。

「訓練後 $b_2$ 永遠猜對」對：$b_1$ 的結果是全域歷史的最後一位；$x$ 是 0 時兩個分支結果一定相反，所以「上一個分支的結果」完全決定 $b_2$。

「用暫存器的值」錯：預測器在取指令階段就要給答案，看不到暫存器內容，只看歷史。

「$(2,2)$ 預測器」對：前一個數字是歷史長度 2（4 種組合），後一個是每個計數器 2 位元；每個分支位址對應 4 個計數器，由歷史選一個。

「只看位址的 2-bit 也抓得到」錯：如果 $x$ 的值時零時非零，$b_2$ 單獨看起來是亂的，只看自己過去的結果抓不到它跟 $b_1$ 的關係。

@q pl3-19 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 情境
In a hot loop that runs $10^8$ times, a short `if` statement chooses between two one-line assignments, and its outcome is completely random: it is taken 50% of the time with no pattern.
```
if (r[i] & 1) v = a; else v = b;
```
On this processor, a mispredicted branch costs about 15 cycles, while a conditional-move instruction, which computes both values and then selects one by the condition without branching, costs 1 extra cycle. The compiler team must decide how to translate such statements, and whether the hardware could do better. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Any branch predictor is right only about half of the time on this branch, on average.
[x] Translating the `if` into a conditional move avoids the misprediction cost.
[x] A conditional move pays for computing both sides, so it is not worthwhile when the bodies of the `if` are long.
[ ] Adding `nop` instructions after the branch reduces the number of mispredictions, because the predictor gets more time to decide.
[ ] Turning the branch predictor off makes this branch cheaper.
---
「平均只猜對一半」對：結果沒有規律，任何預測都等於丟銅板。

「改成條件傳送」對：例如 x86 的 `cmov`；沒有分支，也就沒有猜錯的清空成本。

「兩邊都要算，內容長時不划算」對：多算的成本可能超過省下的猜錯代價。

「加 `nop`」錯：`nop` 不會讓預測變準，只會多浪費週期。

「關掉預測器」錯：沒有預測就得每次等分支結果，成本至少跟猜錯一樣。

@q pl3-20 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 計算
分支佔 20%，預測正確率從 90% 提升到 95%，預測錯誤懲罰 10 拍，基本 CPI 1。CPI 從多少降到多少？
== 1.2,1.1
?? 之前,之後
---
$1+0.2\times0.1\times10=1.2$；$1+0.2\times0.05\times10=1.1$。懲罰越大（管線越深），預測準確率越重要。

@q cd2-18 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
單週期資料路徑執行 `addi $t0, $t1, 5`。RegDst、ALUSrc、RegWrite 各是？
== 0,1,1
?? RegDst,ALUSrc,RegWrite
---

@q cd2-19 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 情境
The single-cycle MIPS datapath, which already supports beq with control signal Branch and the ALU's Zero output, is extended to support bne. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A new control signal BranchNE is added, and PCSrc = (Branch AND Zero) OR (BranchNE AND NOT Zero).
[ ] It is enough to invert the Zero output everywhere.
[x] For bne, the ALU still subtracts the two registers.
[ ] The existing beq circuit already handles bne without any change.
[x] The branch target of bne is computed in the same way as that of beq.

「目標位址算法一樣」對：PC＋4＋（立即值 × 4）。

@q cd2-20 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 觀念

[x] ALUOp＝01，ALU 做減法，看 Zero 判斷兩個暫存器是否相等

@q cd2-21 | co-datapath#2 | 1 | 計算

== -4

@q pl1-18 | co-pipeline#1 | 1 | 計算
管線時脈週期 300 ps，沒有任何停頓時，每秒大約完成幾十億條指令？
== 3.33
---
每 300 ps 完成一條：$1/(300\times10^{-12})\approx3.33\times10^9$。吞吐量看時脈，單條延遲還是 5 級加起來。

@q pl1-19 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 計算
5 級延遲 150、300、200、250、100 ps，每級之間的暫存器 20 ps。把 300 ps 那級拆成兩個 150 ps，新的時脈週期是多少？
== 270
---
原本最慢 300＋20＝320。拆開後最慢的是 250，加 20 得 270。再拆別的級，效益就越來越小（暫存器開銷固定）。

@q pl1-20 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 情境
In a classic 5-stage pipeline (IF, ID, EX, MEM, WB), an instruction in WB writes the register file in the same cycle in which a later instruction in ID reads it. The common design writes the register file in the first half of the cycle and reads it in the second half. For example:
```
add $t0, $t1, $t2     # IF in cycle 1, WB in cycle 5
sub $t3, $t0, $t4
or  $t5, $t6, $t7
and $t8, $t0, $t9     # ID in cycle 5
```
Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With this design, a value written in WB can be read by the instruction in ID during the same cycle.
[x] With this design, an instruction that reads a register written by the instruction three positions earlier gets the new value without having to wait.
[ ] The common solution is to always delay the reading instruction by one cycle.
[x] Even with this design, an instruction that reads a register written by the instruction one or two positions earlier still has a data hazard.
[ ] With this design, no data hazard can occur at all in the pipeline.
---
「前半寫、後半讀，同一拍讀得到」對：寫入先完成，讀的時候已經是新值。

「相隔三條不用停」對：寫的指令在 WB 時，讀的指令剛好在 ID。

「一律停一拍」錯：前半寫後半讀就是為了避免停頓。

「相隔一、兩條仍有危障」對：那時寫的指令還在 EX 或 MEM，還沒到 WB，要靠停頓或之後學到的轉送解決。

「完全沒有資料危障」錯：同上。

@q pl1-21 | co-pipeline#1 | 3 | 計算
load 佔 20%，其中一半緊接著用到載入的值（停 1 拍）；分支佔 15%，每個分支停 1 拍。其他沒有停頓。CPI？
== 1.25
---
$1+0.2\times0.5\times1+0.15\times1=1.25$。

@q pl1-22 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 找錯
A designer pipelines a datapath into five stages whose logic delays are listed below; every pipeline register adds 20 ps of setup and clock-to-output delay.

| stage | S1 | S2 | S3 | S4 | S5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| logic delay (ps) | 200 | 100 | 200 | 300 | 100 |

A student argues: "All stages share one clock, so the clock period is set by the average stage delay, and balancing the stages does not matter." To test the claim, the designer also considers splitting stage S4 into two halves of 150 ps each, which gives six stages. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] The five-stage clock period is 320 ps.
[ ] The arithmetic mean of the five logic delays is 200 ps.
[ ] A clock period of 200 ps accommodates every given stage including register overhead.
[ ] Splitting the 300-ps stage into two 150-ps stages reduces the clock period to 170 ps.
[ ] The fastest stage alone sets the minimum safe period for the entire pipeline.
---
「320 ps」對：最慢的一級 300 ps 加暫存器 20 ps。

「平均 200 ps」錯：總和 900 除以 5 是 180 ps。

「200 ps 就夠」錯：300 ps 那一級加暫存器要 320 ps。

「切開後 170 ps」錯：還有兩級是 200 ps，週期至少 220 ps。

「只看最快的一級」錯：每一級都要在一拍內做完，週期由最慢的一級加暫存器決定。

@q pl2-19 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
有 forwarding 的 5 級管線依序跑 5 條指令：`lw $t1,0($t0)`、`add $t2,$t1,$t3`，後面 3 條都不相依。總共幾拍？
== 10
---
沒有停頓時 $5+4=9$ 拍；lw 後面馬上用 `$t1`，load-use 一定要停 1 拍，共 10 拍。

@q pl2-20 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 陷阱
Consider the consecutive instructions
`add $t0, $t1, $t2`
`sub $t0, $t3, $t4`
`and $t5, $t0, $t6`
in a 5-stage pipeline with forwarding. If `add` enters IF in cycle 1, then `and` is in EX in cycle 5, while `add` is in WB and `sub` is in MEM. At that point, both the EX/MEM and the MEM/WB pipeline registers hold a value destined for `$t0`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The value in EX/MEM, produced by `sub`, must be forwarded to `and`.
[x] The forwarding unit gives priority to EX/MEM and uses MEM/WB only when EX/MEM does not match.
[ ] The value in MEM/WB, produced by `add`, must be forwarded, because `add` executed first.
[x] If `sub` wrote a different register instead, `and` would receive `$t0` forwarded from MEM/WB.
[ ] Since `add` is already in WB, `and` can read `$t0` from the register file without forwarding.
---
「轉送 EX/MEM 裡 `sub` 的結果」對：程式語意上 `and` 要讀的是最近一次寫入 `$t0` 的值，也就是 `sub` 的。

「EX/MEM 優先」對：這就是轉送單元裡「MEM 危障的條件要排除 EX 危障」的原因。

「`add` 先執行所以轉送它的」錯：它的值已經被 `sub` 覆蓋（語意上），用它就錯了。

「`sub` 寫別的暫存器時從 MEM/WB 轉送」對：那時最近寫 `$t0` 的是 `add`，它的結果在 MEM/WB。

「`add` 已到 WB，從暫存器檔讀」錯：`and` 在 ID 讀暫存器時是上一拍，那時 `add` 還在 MEM，還沒寫回；而且就算讀到，也是被 `sub` 取代的舊值。

@q pl2-21 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 找錯
In the textbook five-stage MIPS pipeline, the forwarding unit compares the destination register of an instruction in EX/MEM or MEM/WB with the source registers of the instruction now in EX. A student writes the EX-hazard condition for the first ALU input as:
```
if (EX/MEM.RegWrite and EX/MEM.Rd == ID/EX.Rs)
    ForwardA = 10    # take the first ALU input from EX/MEM
```
The student tests it on `add $t0, $t1, $t2` followed by `sub $t3, $t0, $t4`, and the result is correct. A teaching assistant then runs one test in which an instruction writes `$zero`, which is register 0 and always reads as 0, and another test in which the dependent instruction uses the earlier result as its second source register, Rt. Some of these tests produce wrong results, and the student must fix the condition. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The condition must also require EX/MEM.Rd $\ne 0$.
[x] A result that is destined for `$zero` must never be forwarded.
[ ] Nothing is missing from the student's condition.
[x] A similar condition comparing EX/MEM.Rd with ID/EX.Rt is needed for the second ALU input.
[ ] The condition must also require EX/MEM.MemRead to be 1, so that only values read from memory are forwarded.
---
「要加 Rd ≠ 0」對、「寫 `$zero` 的結果不能轉送」對：`$zero` 永遠是 0，對它的寫入一律忽略；如果把那條指令算出的值轉送出去，讀 `$zero` 的指令就會拿到錯的值。

「沒有漏」錯。

「第二個運算元也要一樣的條件」對：Rs、Rt 各自檢查。

「要求 MemRead」錯：MemRead 是 load 的訊號；load 的資料在 EX/MEM 時還沒讀出來，那是 load-use 停頓要處理的情況，不是轉送條件的一部分。

@q pf1-19 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算

== 1.6
---
可用的週期數 $4\times4\times10^9=1.6\times10^{10}$，除以指令數。

@q pf1-20 | co-perf#1 | 1 | 計算
時脈 2 GHz、平均 CPI 0.8。MIPS（每秒百萬條指令）是多少？
== 2500
---
$2\times10^9/0.8=2.5\times10^9$ 條/秒。CPI 小於 1 表示每拍平均完成超過一條（多發射）。

@q pf1-21 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
新編譯器讓指令數少 20%，但平均 CPI 多 10%。時脈不變，變快幾倍？
== 1.14
== 1.136
---
新時間／舊時間 $=0.8\times1.1=0.88$，加速 $1/0.88\approx1.136$。只看指令數或只看 CPI 都會誤判。

@q cd1-20 | co-datapath#1 | 3 | 計算
Consider the RV32I instruction `sub x10, x11, x12`, which computes x11 minus x12 and writes the result to x10. It is R-type with opcode `0110011` and funct3 `000`, and the layout of its 32 bits is:

| bits | 31–25 | 24–20 | 19–15 | 14–12 | 11–7 | 6–0 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| part | funct7 | rs2 | rs1 | funct3 | rd | opcode |

A student assembling the instruction by hand must fill in each part and write the result in hexadecimal. The student also compares it with `add x10, x11, x12`, which uses the same opcode and funct3, and with the MIPS R-format, which the student learned first and in which rd occupies bits 15 to 11 and the lowest 6 bits are funct, to see which parts line up. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Its funct7 bits are `0100000`, and these are the only bits that differ from the encoding of `add x10, x11, x12`.
[x] Its machine code is `0x40C58533`.
[x] Its rs1 bits, bits 19 to 15, contain `01011`.
[ ] Its machine code is `0x00C58533`.
[ ] Its rd bits are bits 15 to 11, the same position that rd occupies in the MIPS R-format, so the two formats agree there.
---
「funct7 是 0100000、只有這裡跟 add 不同」對：add 和 sub 的 opcode、funct3 都一樣，只靠 funct7 的第 30 位元區分。

「機器碼 `0x40C58533`」對：0100000｜01100（12）｜01011（11）｜000｜01010（10）｜0110011 = 0100 0000 1100 0101 1000 0101 0011 0011。

「rs1 是 01011」對：rs1 是 x11。

「`0x00C58533`」錯：這是 funct7 填成 0000000 的結果，也就是 `add x10, x11, x12`。

「rd 在第 15～11 位元」錯：那是 MIPS R 型的 rd 位置；RISC-V 的 rd 在第 11～7 位元，緊貼在 opcode 上面。

@q cd1-21 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算

== 131068
---
最大正位移 $2^{15}-1=32767$ 條，乘 4。更遠的就要用 j 或先把位址放進暫存器再 jr。

@q ca1-20 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算

?? tag,index,offset

@q ca1-21 | co-cache#1 | 3 | 計算
32 位元位址、直接映射、資料 16 KB、區塊 16 bytes。加上 tag 和 valid 位元，cache 總共多少 KB？
== 18.375

@q ar1-22 | co-arith#1 | 2 | 計算

== FF9C,-100
?? hex,數值
---
最高位元是 1，前面補 1：0xFF9C。$0x9C=156$，$156-256=-100$，擴展前後數值不變。

@q ar4-21 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算

== 41200000

---

@q ar4-22 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 計算
半精度浮點（1 位符號、5 位指數偏移 15、10 位小數）表示 1.5，16 進位是？
== 3E00
== 0x3E00
---
$1.5=1.1_2\times2^0$：指數 $0+15=01111$、小數 1000000000：0 01111 1000000000。格式不同，原則一樣。

@q ar1-23 | co-arith#1 | 3 | 計算
要用二補數表示 $-1000$ 到 $1000$ 的所有整數，至少要幾個位元？
== 11
---
$n$ 位元二補數範圍是 $-2^{n-1}$ 到 $2^{n-1}-1$。10 位元只到 511；11 位元是 $-1024$～$1023$，夠用。正數那邊少一個，要看 $2^{n-1}-1\ge1000$。

@q pf1-22 | co-perf#1 | 3 | 計算
處理器基本 CPI 1，加上等記憶體的停頓後實際 CPI 3。時脈加倍，但記憶體存取的時間（ns）不變，所以停頓的拍數也加倍。整體快幾倍？
== 1.2
---
停頓原本 2 拍，變成 4 拍，CPI 5。時間比：舊 $3T$，新 $5\times\frac T2=2.5T$，$3/2.5=1.2$。時脈加倍只快 20%，因為記憶體沒有跟著變快。

@q ar2-21 | co-arith#2 | 2 | 計算
16 位元加法器，每個位元的進位要 2 個閘延遲。ripple carry 的最壞延遲是多少？改成 carry-select（低 8 位 ripple；高 8 位用兩組 ripple 分別假設進位是 0 和 1，最後用 2 閘延遲的多工器挑）呢？
== 32,18
?? ripple,carry-select
---
ripple：$16\times2$。carry-select：高低兩半同時算，各 $8\times2=16$，再加多工器 2。用多一倍的高半部硬體換速度。

@q ar2-22 | co-arith#2 | 3 | 陷阱
4 位元加法 1100 ＋ 0110。當成無號數有沒有溢位？當成二補數有號數呢？
== 有,沒有
?? 無號,有號
---
結果 1 0010，最高位進位出去 1：無號 $12+6=18$ 超過 15，溢位。有號 $-4+6=2$ 正確，進入最高位的進位 1、出去的也 1，兩者相同，沒有溢位。兩種解讀用不同的判斷方式。

@q ar2-23 | co-arith#2 | 1 | 計算
用加法器算 4 位元的 0101 − 0011（把減數取反再加 1）。結果的 4 位元是？
== 0010
---
$0101+1100+1=1\,0010$，取低 4 位 0010（$5-3=2$）。對無號減法來說，最高位進位 1 表示沒有借位。

@q ar3-21 | co-arith#3 | 1 | 計算
用移位相加算無號 1011 × 0110，8 位元結果？
== 01000010
---
$11\times6=66$。乘數的位元 1、2 是 1：$1011\ll1+1011\ll2=10110+101100=1000010$。

@q ar3-22 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 計算
兩個 32 位元**無號**數 0xFFFFFFFF × 0xFFFFFFFF，64 位元乘積的高 32 位（有些處理器放在 Hi 暫存器）是？
== FFFFFFFE
== 0xFFFFFFFE
---
$(2^{32}-1)^2=2^{64}-2^{33}+1$ ＝ 0xFFFFFFFE00000001。同樣的位元當成有號數是 $(-1)\times(-1)=1$，Hi 會是 0，所以 mult 和 multu 要分開。

@q pf2-21 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算
三個程式各做一樣多的運算，執行速率分別是每秒 2、3、6 億次。整體平均速率（總運算量 ÷ 總時間）是多少？
== 3
---
速率要用調和平均：$\frac3{1/2+1/3+1/6}=3$（億次／秒）。用算術平均會得到 3.67，高估了。

@q pf2-22 | co-perf#2 | 2 | 計算

== 2.62
---
$(1.5\times2\times6)^{1/3}=18^{1/3}\approx2.62$。

@q pf3-21 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 情境
70% 的時間丟給 GPU 可以快 50 倍，但搬資料過去再搬回來要花原本時間的 5%。整體加速多少？
== 2.75
---
新時間 $0.3+0.7/50+0.05=0.364$，加速 $1/0.364\approx2.75$。搬資料的開銷有時比加速的部分還重要。

@q pl3-21 | co-pipeline#3 | 3 | 計算
分支佔 20%。BTB 查得到的比例 90%；查得到時預測正確率 90%，預測錯罰 3 拍；BTB 查不到罰 2 拍。基本 CPI 1，CPI 是多少？
== 1.094
---
每個分支平均懲罰 $0.9\times0.1\times3+0.1\times2=0.27+0.2=0.47$，乘上 0.2 得 0.094。

@q il3-21 | co-ilp#3 | 2 | 追蹤
Tomasulo 依序 issue：`ADD F2,F4,F6`（進 Add1）、`MUL F8,F2,F4`（進 Mult1）、`ADD F2,F8,F6`（進 Add2），三條都還沒完成。暫存器狀態 F2 是誰？Mult1 的 $Q_j$ 是誰？

@q pf2-23 | co-perf#2 | 3 | 比較
兩個程式：機器 A 跑 1 秒和 100 秒，機器 B 都跑 10 秒。看總時間誰快？看執行時間的幾何平均呢？
== B,一樣
?? 總時間,幾何平均
---
總時間 A 101、B 20，B 快很多。幾何平均 A $\sqrt{1\times100}=10$、B 10，一樣。幾何平均不在乎「哪個程式佔大部分時間」，如果使用者真正在意的是總時間，就要用總時間（或加權）來比。

@q il1-21 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 陷阱
Two loops start from the same list $x = [1, 2, 3, 4]$ and run $k$ in ascending order from 0 through 2:
```
loop one:  for (k = 0; k <= 2; k++) x[k+1] = 2 * x[k];
loop two:  for (k = 0; k <= 2; k++) x[k]   = 2 * x[k+1];
```
A compiler proposes a transformation that makes every read use an untouched copy of the initial values, so that all iterations could run in parallel. A student traces both loops by hand, first in their original form and then with the copy, and compares the final lists. A classmate argues that loop one has only a name dependence across iterations, since every iteration writes a different element, so the copy should be harmless for it as well. The compiler must decide which loop it may transform this way. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Loop one's original final list of elements is [1,2,4,8].
[x] Loop two's original final list of elements is [4,6,8,4].
[x] The untouched-copy transformation preserves loop two's result.
[ ] The untouched-copy transformation preserves loop one's result.
[ ] Loop one's cross-iteration dependence is only a name dependence, since every write has a different index.
---
「第一圈組結果」對：新 x[1] 傳到下一圈，再一路產生四、八。「第二圈組結果」對：各圈讀原先右鄰，依次寫四、六、八，末元素不改。「第二個可讀複本」對：它原本就先讀右鄰、下一圈才覆寫該位置，複本保存所需舊值。「第一個也可」錯：會改成 [1,2,4,6]，破壞真資料鏈。「不同寫索引就只是假相依」錯：還要檢查別圈讀的位置，第一個有前寫後讀的 RAW。

@q il1-22 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 情境
In a simple machine model, an ordinary load from address 0 causes a fault that the user can observe, such as a crash. The original code reads through the address `p` only after checking it:
```
if (p != 0) y = load(p);
```
To hide the load latency, a compiler proposes moving an ordinary load of `*p` above the test and executing it unconditionally, without proving that $p \ne 0$ and without using a special load that defers faults. In a test, `p` is 0 in about 2% of the 10000 calls, and the program must behave exactly as before on every call. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For p equal to zero, the original code performs no load.
[x] The proposed transformation can introduce an observable fault absent from the original execution.
[x] Preserving only the final value of y is insufficient to establish this transformation's correctness.
[ ] Discarding the loaded value afterward necessarily removes the already-observed fault.
[ ] Merely assigning the load result a new register name proves the transformation safe for p zero.
---
「原本零時不載入」對：條件為假。「可能新增故障」對：新版本無條件執行，零位址立即出錯。「只比 y 不夠」對：是否產生可觀察例外也是語意。「事後丟值就撤故障」錯：題設是一般立即可觀察的載入，沒有延後故障機制。「換名就安全」錯：換名解暫存器名稱限制，不能證明位址有效或取消控制相依造成的例外風險。

@q il1-23 | co-ilp#1 | 2 | 計算
一段程式 12 條指令，每條延遲 1 拍，相依關係最長的那條鏈有 4 條指令。即使有無限多執行單元，平均每拍最多完成幾條？
== 3
---
最少要 4 拍（關鍵鏈長度），12 條在 4 拍內完成，平均 3 條。這是這段程式本身的指令層級平行度上限。

@q il2-22 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 情境
A classic scoreboard reserves an add functional unit from issue through result writeback. Two such units are occupied. Three independent ADDs have distinct destinations; the third reaches issue before either unit is released. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The third ADD stalls at issue for a structural reason.
[x] Releasing either occupied add unit can remove this structural blockage.
[ ] Both occupied units must be released before the third ADD can issue.
[ ] The third ADD's distinct destination creates a WAW hazard with every earlier ADD.
[ ] A fourth instruction can bypass the blocked third instruction in an in-order issue policy.
---
「結構卡發出」對：沒有可分配的加法單元。「任一釋放可解除」對：只需一個空位，其他條件題設已滿足。「兩個都要空」錯：一條加法不需同時占兩單元。「不同目的也 WAW」錯：同目的才有該種寫衝突。「第四條繞過」錯：循序發出不能跳過尚未發出的第三條。

@q il5-22 | co-ilp#5 | 3 | 計算
VLIW 每個指令包有 2 個記憶體槽、2 個實數運算槽、1 個整數／分支槽。迴圈本體有 3 個載入、1 個存回、2 個實數加法、1 個整數加、1 個分支（假設都沒有相依）。至少要幾個指令包？
== 2
---
記憶體運算 4 個、每包 2 個 → 至少 2 包；整數加和分支共用 1 個槽 → 也要 2 包；實數加法 2 個 1 包就夠。瓶頸是記憶體槽和整數槽，答案 2。

@q il5-23 | co-ilp#5 | 1 | 計算
用一次處理 8 個 float 的 SIMD 指令做兩個長度 1000 的陣列相加。大約要幾條加法指令？
== 125
---
$1000/8$。純量版要 1000 條。資料平行讓指令數（和取指、解碼的成本）大幅下降。

@q vc1-21 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 3 | 觀念
4 cores each have a private 32 KiB L1 cache with 64-byte blocks and keep these caches coherent by monitoring a shared bus that carries physical addresses: when one core writes a block, the others see its physical address on the bus and must check their own tags. The designers compare a VIPT L1 cache, which is indexed with virtual-address bits that lie inside the 4 KiB page and tagged with the physical page number, against a VIVT L1 cache, which uses virtual addresses for both the index and the tag. Which of the following statements about using a VIPT rather than a VIVT L1 cache are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] With a VIPT cache, the tags are physical, so a physical address seen on the bus can be compared directly.
[ ] With a VIVT cache, a physical address seen on the bus can be compared directly with the tags.
[ ] With a VIPT cache, the index must first be translated back from the physical address.
[ ] The choice between VIPT and VIVT makes no difference for bus monitoring.
[ ] With a VIPT cache, no tags are needed, because the index already identifies the block.
---
匯流排上傳的是實體位址；「VIPT 的 tag 是實體的」，可以直接比對。VIVT 的 tag 是虛擬的，要把實體位址反查回虛擬位址才能比對。VIPT 的 index 落在頁內位移，實體位址的同一段就是 index，不必反查。很多區塊共用同一個 index，一定要 tag 才能分辨。

@q vc1-22 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 2 | 計算
頁 8 KB、64 KB 4-way VIPT cache、區塊 64 bytes。index 加 offset 中，有幾個位元來自虛擬頁號？
== 1
---
每一路 16 KB，index＋offset 共 14 位元；頁內位移 13 位元，多出 1 位元來自虛擬頁號，同一個實體位址可能出現在 2 個位置（同義詞）。

@q vc2-21 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 2 | 計算
只有一顆 CPU 用到 X。它先讀 X 再寫 X。MSI 協定和 MESI 協定各需要幾次匯流排交易？
== 2,1
?? MSI,MESI
---
MSI：讀進來是 S，寫的時候要再發一次「升級／invalidate」。MESI：讀進來發現沒人有，直接是 E，之後寫入靜悄悄變 M。

@q vc2-22 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 3 | 計算
directory 協定：某區塊目前有 3 個其他 cache 持有共享副本。一顆 CPU 要寫入（寫 miss）。至少要幾則訊息（請求、invalidate、ack、資料回覆各算一則）？
== 8
---
向 home 送請求 1；home 對 3 個持有者送 invalidate 3；3 個 ack 3；資料（含寫入權）回覆 1。共享者越多，寫入越貴。

@q vc3-21 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 3 | 情境
ARM（弱記憶體模型）上：P1 `data=1; flag=1;`，P2 `while(!flag); r=data;`。沒有任何屏障指令時 r 可能是 0 嗎？兩邊各加一個「之前的存取完成後才做之後的」排序指令（P1 在兩個寫之間、P2 在讀 flag 和讀 data 之間）之後呢？
== 可能,不可能
?? 沒屏障,有屏障
---
ARM 允許寫→寫、讀→讀重排，P2 可能先看到 flag 再看到舊的 data。排序指令（fence）把兩邊的順序都固定住。

@q vc3-22 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 2 | 觀念
Release consistency divides memory operations into ordinary reads and writes and synchronization operations: an acquire, such as taking a lock, and a release, such as freeing it. A program on a 16-core machine with 64-byte blocks updates a shared table of 1000 entries only inside critical sections protected by a lock, and a student studies how much reordering the hardware may do under release consistency compared with sequential consistency (SC) and with TSO. The student also wants to know whether such a correctly synchronized program can tell the difference. Which of the following statements about release consistency are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Only synchronization operations (acquire and release) must be strictly ordered.
[x] Ordinary accesses between two synchronization operations may be reordered freely.
[x] A program that uses locks correctly behaves as if it ran under SC.
[x] It allows the hardware to reorder more than TSO does.
[ ] Every read and write must still follow program order, just as under SC.
---
「只有同步操作要排好」，「兩次同步之間的一般存取可以任意重排」；正確加鎖的程式「看起來就像 SC」。它比 TSO 放寬得更多，硬體能重排的更多。一般存取不必照程式順序。

@q vc4-21 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 計算
16 條執行緒在等 ticket lock（都在讀同一個 now_serving）。持有者放鎖、把 now_serving 加 1 時，大約造成幾次 cache miss？改用 MCS 鎖呢？
== 16,1
?? ticket,MCS
---
ticket lock：now_serving 被寫入，16 個等待者的副本全失效，各自重新讀一次。MCS：每人在自己的節點上等，放鎖只寫下一個人的旗子，1 次。

@q vc4-22 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 3 | 觀念
3 threads repeatedly synchronize at the same barrier after completing one unit of work. A proposed implementation increments an atomic arrival count and lets the last arriver reset it to 0. Other threads wait until they observe zero. The count alone contains no indication of which round is being completed. Consider a fast thread that immediately starts its next unit while another thread has not yet observed the reset from the previous round.

The replacement is a sense-reversing barrier. Initially the global sense and every thread's private sense are false. On entering a round, each thread flips its private sense. The last arriver resets the count before publishing the new global sense, and the publication has the required memory ordering. Each other thread spins until the global sense matches its own private sense. All three participate in both rounds and no thread exits early. Follow the transitions through 2 consecutive rounds. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If the counter is simply reset, a fast thread may enter the next round early and corrupt the count.
[x] A sense-reversing barrier flips a global flag each round.
[x] Each thread leaves when the global sense equals its own sense.
[x] The next round then uses the opposite value.
[ ] Sense reversal mainly saves power by letting waiting threads sleep instead of spinning.
---
「只歸零可混輪」對：若快者在慢者看見零之前把下輪計數加成一，慢者可能錯過釋放訊號。「每輪翻全域旗標」對：第一輪各自翻成 true，最後到者發布 true；第二輪改為 false。「等待與自己相同」對：每緒保存本輪目標，觀察到對應 sense 才離開。「下一輪反值」對：前後輪的目標不同，不能只看可能已重用的計數。「主要為睡眠省電」錯：題設仍自旋，核心用途是區別同步輪次；必要的原子操作與記憶體排序也不能省略。

@q ca1-22 | co-cache#1 | 1 | 計算
直接映射 cache 有 8 個區塊。區塊位址依序 0、8、16、0、8。幾次 miss？
== 5
---
三個位址除以 8 都餘 0，搶同一格，每次都把前一個踢掉，全部 miss。

@q ca1-23 | co-cache#1 | 2 | 計算
4 KB 直接映射 cache、區塊 32 bytes，32 位元位址。位址 0x0000ABCD 的 tag、index、offset（十進位）？
== 10,94,13
?? tag,index,offset
---
offset 5 位元：0x0D＝13；index $4096/32=128$ 格、7 位元：$(0xABCD\gg5)\&0x7F=94$；tag 是其餘高位：$0xABCD\gg12=10$。

@q ca5-22 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算
DRAM 有 8192 列，每 64 ms 要全部刷新一次，每刷新一列佔用 50 ns。刷新佔掉多少比例的時間（%）？
== 0.64
---
$8192\times50$ ns $=409.6\ \mu$s，除以 64 ms。刷新期間那個 bank 不能被存取。

@q ca5-23 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算
8 個 bank 交錯（字位址 mod 8），程式每隔 2 個字讀一次（0、2、4、…）。實際只用到幾個 bank？
== 4
---
只會落在偶數號的 bank 0、2、4、6，另一半閒著，交錯的效益減半。

@q cd1-22 | co-datapath#1 | 1 | 計算
`addi $t0, $zero, -5` 的 16 位元立即值欄位（以 hex 表示）？
== FFFB
== 0xFFFB
---
$-5$ 的 16 位元二補數：$5=$ 0x0005，取反 0xFFFA，加 1 得 0xFFFB。執行時 sign-extend 成 32 位元。

@q ca2-23 | co-cache#2 | 2 | 計算
write-back cache：2% 的存取會 miss、讀進一個區塊 100 拍；被換出的區塊有 30% 是 dirty，寫回要再多 100 拍（沒有 write buffer）。平均每次存取因 miss 多花幾拍？
== 2.6
---
每次 miss 平均 $100+0.3\times100=130$ 拍，乘 2%。加一個 write buffer 讓寫回和讀取重疊，就能回到 2 拍。

@q ca2-24 | co-cache#2 | 3 | 情境
A write-through buffer combines eight consecutive four-byte stores that fill one aligned 32-byte block into one block transaction. The eight stores write distinct words, and there are no intervening reads or ordering constraints. Compare with eight separate word transactions. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The number of write transactions decreases from eight to one.
[ ] The total useful data written drops from 32 bytes to 4 bytes.
[ ] Combining these stores increases cache data storage by 32 bytes.
[x] Combining can reduce per-transaction overhead.
[ ] Combining guarantees that future reads have no cache misses.
---
「八次變一次」對：八個字合成一筆整塊交易。「資料變 4 位元組」錯：八個不同的字都要寫，仍是 8×4＝32 位元組，只是一次送。「增加快取容量」錯：只是緩衝與傳送方式改變。「減每筆開銷」對：例如位址與啟動成本可少付幾次。「讀永不失誤」錯：寫入合併不提供這種保證。

@q ca3-23 | co-cache#3 | 3 | 計算
cache 共 4 個區塊。區塊位址 0、1、2、3、4、0、2。2-way 組關聯（2 組、LRU）和全關聯（LRU）各幾次 miss？
== 7,6
?? 2-way,全關聯
---
2-way：偶數 0、2、4 搶第 0 組的 2 個位置，輪流踢掉，全部 miss；第 1 組的 1、3 也是第一次，共 7 次。全關聯：前 5 個都 miss，0 回來時又 miss（被 4 擠掉），最後 2 命中，共 6 次。差的那 1 次就是 conflict miss。

@q ca4-23 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 計算
三層 cache：L1 命中 1 拍、miss rate 5%；L2 命中 10 拍、local miss rate 20%；L3 命中 30 拍、local miss rate 25%；記憶體 200 拍。AMAT？
== 2.3
---
從最裡面往外算：L3 的平均 $30+0.25\times200=80$；L2 的平均 $10+0.2\times80=26$；AMAT $=1+0.05\times26=2.3$。

@q pl2-22 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
5 級管線有 forwarding。ALU 的每個運算元前面的多工器有幾個輸入來源？
== 3
---
暫存器檔讀出的值（ID/EX）、從 EX/MEM 轉送、從 MEM/WB 轉送。兩個運算元各一個這樣的多工器。

@q pl4-23 | co-pipeline#4 | 2 | 計算
平均每 $10^4$ 條指令發生一次例外，每次清管線損失 4 拍、處理程式 500 拍。CPI 增加多少？
== 0.0504
== 0.05
---
$(500+4)/10^4$。清管線的成本相對處理程式本身通常很小。

@q cd2-22 | co-datapath#2 | 1 | 計算
單週期資料路徑執行 `j`（跳躍）。RegWrite、MemWrite、Jump 三個控制訊號各是？
== 0,0,1

@q cd3-22 | co-datapath#3 | 1 | 計算

---

== 50.25

@q il4-22 | co-ilp#4 | 2 | 計算
實體暫存器 64 個、架構暫存器 32 個。目前 ROB 裡有 20 條還沒 commit、各自配了一個新的實體暫存器。可用（free list 上）的實體暫存器還有幾個？
== 12
---
32 個存目前的架構狀態，20 個被還沒 commit 的指令佔用，$64-32-20=12$。用完時新指令就無法換名、要停下來等。

@q il4-23 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 追蹤
Memory x initially contains 5. An older store will write 9 to x, but a younger speculative load reads five before the store address is known; a dependent add computes the loaded value plus 1. Before either younger result commits, hardware detects the address overlap and replays the load and dependent add after the store. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The speculative add's first result is six.
[x] The replayed load obtains nine.
[x] The corrected add result is ten.
[ ] In-order commit alone makes the old loaded value five correct without replay or equivalent correction.
[ ] Correct recovery must cancel the valid older store solely to preserve the speculative load's value.
---
「先得六」對：5＋1。「重做讀九」對：老 store 的九必須先對此讀生效，可由正確轉送或記憶體取得。「最後十」對：9＋1。「只依序交就自動修值」錯：順序規則不會把已算錯的五變九，仍需重做或等價更正。「取消老 store 保留猜值」錯：程式語意要求先寫九再讀，應修正年輕推測工作而非刪掉有效老操作。

@q ca6-23 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 計算
把 $1024\times1024$ 的 double 二維陣列 A 行列互換後寫到 B：`B[j][i] = A[i][j]`，i 外層、j 內層。區塊 64 bytes，cache 遠小於一整行 B 的區塊總量。平均每個元素大約幾次 miss？
== 1.125
---
讀 A 是循序的，8 個 double 一個區塊，$\frac18$；寫 B 每次跳一整列（8 KB），每次都是新區塊，而且下一輪回來時早被擠掉，約 1 次。分塊（tiling）處理可以讓兩邊都接近 $\frac18$。

@q pl3-23 | co-pipeline#3 | 2 | 計算
有一個延遲槽（delayed branch）的管線，分支佔 20%。編譯器能在 60% 的延遲槽放有用的指令，其餘放 nop。基本 CPI 1，考慮浪費掉的槽，有效 CPI？
== 1.08
---
40% 的分支浪費 1 拍：$1+0.2\times0.4$。管線變深、一次發多條指令後，一個延遲槽不夠用，現代架構大多不再採用。

@q pf4-23 | co-perf#4 | 1 | 計算
晶片功耗 50 W，每秒做 $10^{11}$ 次運算。每次運算平均耗多少 nJ？
== 0.5
---
$50/10^{11}=5\times10^{-10}$ J。「每瓦效能」常用這種每次運算能量來比較。

@q pf1-23 | co-perf#1 | 2 | 計算
程式跑 5 秒：CPU 計算 3 秒、等 I/O 2 秒（不重疊）。換一顆快 2 倍的 CPU，整體變快幾倍？
== 1.43
== 1.4
---

@q ar3-23 | co-arith#3 | 2 | 計算
兩個 16 位元**有號**整數相乘，乘積最少要幾個位元才一定放得下？
== 32
---
絕對值最大的是 $(-32768)\times(-32768)=2^{30}$，正數 $2^{30}$ 需要 31 個數值位元加 1 個符號位元。所以 $n$ 位元乘 $n$ 位元要 $2n$ 位元的結果。

@q ar4-23 | co-arith#4 | 3 | 陷阱
`float x = 16777217;`（$2^{24}+1$），印出來是多少？
== 16777216
---
單精度只有 24 個有效位元（含隱藏位元）。$2^{24}$ 以上相鄰兩個可表示的數差 2，$2^{24}+1$ 剛好在中間，四捨五入到偶數得 $2^{24}$。用 float 當計數器，超過一千六百多萬就開始「加 1 沒反應」。


@q il3-22 | co-ilp#3 | 3 | 情境
一條 load cache miss 要 100 拍，處理器每拍 issue 一條指令。希望 miss 期間後面不相依的指令都能繼續 issue，保留站（或指令視窗）大約要幾項？
== 100
---
100 拍內會 issue 約 100 條，都要有地方等待或執行。視窗不夠大時就會卡住；這也是為什麼記憶體延遲越長，處理器的視窗越做越大。



@q cd1-23 | co-datapath#1 | 2 | 計算

== 100400
== 0x100400
---
目標位址的高 4 位元沿用 PC＋4 的，低 2 位元一定是 0，所以欄位存的是位址的第 2～27 位元：0x00401000 右移 2 位＝0x100400。


@q cd2-23 | co-datapath#2 | 3 | 情境
We want a single-cycle RISC-V datapath to support `jalr rd, imm(rs1)`, which writes PC + 4 into rd and jumps to the address rs1 + imm with the lowest bit cleared to 0. A function return is `jalr x0, 0(ra)`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The ALU can compute rs1 + imm, so the multiplexer that selects the next PC needs an input from the ALU result.
[x] `jalr` writes PC + 4 into rd; for a function return, rd is x0, so the write has no effect.
[ ] The target of `jalr` is PC + imm, just like the target of a branch.
[ ] `jalr` is a J-type instruction, so it cannot use any register to form its target.
[x] Because the lowest bit of the target is cleared, `jalr` can never jump to an odd address.
---
「ALU 算 rs1＋imm、PC 多工器要接 ALU 結果」對：目標來自暫存器加立即值，跟 `lw` 算位址的路徑一樣（ALUSrc 選立即值），只是結果要送去 PC。

「寫 PC＋4 到 rd，回傳時 rd 是 x0」對：寫入 x0 會被丟掉，所以 `ret` 不改任何暫存器。

「目標是 PC＋imm」錯：那是分支和 `jal`；`jalr` 的目標取決於暫存器內容，所以才能從函式回到任何呼叫點。

「J 型、不能用暫存器」錯：`jalr` 是 I 型，正是靠 rs1 決定目標。

「不會跳到奇數位址」對：規格就是把最低位元清成 0。指令至少以 2 bytes 對齊（有壓縮指令時），所以這樣不會損失可跳的位置。


@q pl1-23 | co-pipeline#1 | 2 | 計算
5 級管線只有一個記憶體埠，load/store 在 MEM 時，IF 就不能取指令、要停 1 拍。load/store 佔 30%，其他沒有停頓。CPI？
== 1.3
---
每條 load/store 造成 1 拍結構危障：$1+0.3$。分開指令、資料 cache 就能消除。


@q vc1-23 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 3 | 計算
程式隨機存取 1000 個不同的頁（均勻），TLB 只有 64 項、全關聯。穩定後 TLB 沒命中的比例大約多少？
== 93.6%
== 0.936
---
TLB 裡最多 64 頁，隨機選到其中之一的機率 $64/1000$，miss 約 $1-0.064$。工作集遠大於 TLB 涵蓋範圍時，TLB 幾乎沒用，大頁就很重要。


@q vc2-23 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 2 | 追蹤
MESI，三顆 CPU 一開始都沒有 X。依序：P1 讀、P1 寫、P2 讀、P3 寫、P2 讀。最後 P1、P2、P3 的狀態？
== I,S,S
---
P1 讀 E、寫 M（不用匯流排）。P2 讀：P1 提供並變 S，P2 S。P3 寫：其他都 I，P3 M。P2 讀：P3 提供並變 S，P2 S；P1 一直是 I。


@q vc3-23 | co-vm-coherence#3 | 1 | 計算
兩條執行緒各有 3 個操作（各自順序固定），SC 下有幾種交錯？
== 20
---
從 6 個位置中選 3 個給第一條：$\binom63$。


@q vc4-23 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 情境
A two-node NUMA machine allocates each physical page on the node whose thread first writes it. A main thread running on node zero initializes four previously unallocated pages. Each page contains 4096 bytes. Workers on node one subsequently perform all useful computation, reading each page one hundred times. For the measured requests, assume every read reaches main memory: cache hits are outside this simplified experiment. Local memory requests cost 80 ns and remote requests cost 160 ns, with requests handled serially and no overlap.

No automatic page migration occurs in the initial run. Compare that run with two alternatives: the workers perform the first writes themselves, or the initialized pages are explicitly migrated to node one before measurement. Initialization and migration costs are excluded from the measured read interval. The useful arithmetic and addresses read are identical in all runs, so only placement differs. Use the specified first-touch policy rather than assuming that shared addressing implies uniform access costs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] All pages end up on node 0, so every access from node 1 is remote.
[x] Letting the computing threads initialize their own parts places those pages on node 1.
[x] Migrating the pages to node 1 after initialization also helps.
[x] The problem comes from the first-touch placement policy, not from the computation itself.
[ ] Because all memory is shared, remote and local accesses on NUMA cost exactly the same time.
---
「頁都在零、節點一遠端」對：四張頁首次寫入皆由節點零執行，量測又明定每次到主記憶體。「計算者自己初始化」對：首次觸碰改在一便配置在一。「搬頁到一有助」對：四百次串行讀，遠端需 400×160＝64000 ns，本地需 400×80＝32000 ns，不計搬移費時減半。「問題由首次配置造成」對：三次實驗計算與位址相同，差別是頁的家。「共用所以等延遲」錯：本題本地八十與遠端一百六十 ns 明確不同；不把快取命中也稱成遠端記憶體讀。


@q pf3-23 | co-perf#3 | 3 | 計算
程式 80% 可平行。序列部分在一顆快 2 倍的大核心上跑，平行部分分給 8 顆小核心（和原本一樣快）。整體加速多少？
== 5
---
時間 $0.2/2+0.8/8=0.1+0.1=0.2$。把序列部分放到大核心，是打破 Amdahl 限制的一種做法（異質多核）。



@q il5-24 | co-ilp#5 | 2 | 計算
GPU 一組 32 條執行緒一起執行。分支中 1 條走很慢的路（1000 拍），31 條走快的路（10 拍）。這組花多少拍？
== 1010
---
兩條路都要依序執行，整組被最慢的那條拖住。寫 GPU 程式要盡量讓同一組執行緒走相同的路。


@q cd3-23 | co-datapath#3 | 1 | 計算
多週期 MIPS 執行一條 lw，共用的那一個記憶體被存取幾次？
== 2
---
第 1 拍取指令、第 4 拍讀資料。多週期可以只用一個記憶體，因為兩次存取在不同的拍。


@q pl2-23 | co-pipeline#2 | 2 | 計算
有 forwarding 的 5 級管線：`lw $1,0($4)`、`lw $2,4($4)`、`add $3,$1,$2`。要停幾拍？
== 1
---
add 用到的 $2 由前一條 lw 載入，load-use 停 1 拍；$1 是兩條之前載入的，停完之後可以從 MEM/WB 轉送。


@q il2-23 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
In a scoreboard with no forwarding, a multiply writes f0 at the end of cycle seven. Two already-issued instructions needing f0 need that value; their other sources are available. Register-file ports permit both reads together, and newly written data may be read beginning in the next cycle. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both instructions needing f0 may read their operands in cycle eight.
[x] The multiply does not need to execute again for the second instruction needing f0.
[ ] One instruction needing f0 must finish execution before the other may read f0.
[ ] Both instructions needing f0 may read the new value in cycle seven under the stated timing rule.
[ ] The first possible joint read is cycle nine solely because there are two instructions needing f0.
---
「第八拍同讀」對：第七拍結束寫好，下一拍可讀且埠足夠。「不用重算」對：兩者讀同一個已寫入值。「先完成一個」錯：兩者獨立，不需互等。「第七拍讀新值」錯：違反明定的時序，也沒有轉送。「第九拍才行」錯：題設允許同拍兩讀，消費者數量沒有再多加一拍。


@q il3-23 | co-ilp#3 | 1 | 追蹤
Tomasulo：`LD F2,0(R1)` 進 Load1（還沒完成），接著 `ADD F4,F2,F2` issue 到 Add1。Add1 的 $Q_j$、$Q_k$？
== Load1,Load1
---
兩個來源都是 F2，都等 Load1。Load1 廣播時兩個欄位同時拿到值。



@q ar1-24 | co-arith#1 | 1 | 計算
16 位元二補數 0x8001 代表的整數是？
== -32767
---
最高位是 1，值為 $0x8001-2^{16}=32769-65536$。


@q ar2-24 | co-arith#2 | 2 | 計算
4 位元 carry lookahead 用 $g_i=a_ib_i$、$p_i=a_i+b_i$。$a=1010$、$b=0101$ 時，整組的 $G$ 和 $P$？
== 0,1
?? G,P
---
每個位元恰好一個 1：$g$ 全 0、$p$ 全 1。$G=0$（這組自己不產生進位）、$P=1$（進來的進位會一路傳出去）。


@q ar3-24 | co-arith#3 | 3 | 陷阱
8 位元 Booth（radix-2）乘數是 01010101。要做幾次加或減？普通的移位相加要做幾次加？
== 8,4
?? Booth,移位相加
---
每一對相鄰位元都不同，Booth 每一位都產生 $\pm1$，8 次；移位相加只看 1 的個數，4 次。Booth 對連續的 1 有利，對交錯的位元反而更差。


@q ar4-24 | co-arith#4 | 2 | 計算
單精度 0x40490FDB 代表的數大約是？（取 4 位小數）
== 3.1416
---
符號 0、指數 0x80＝128 → $2^1$、尾數 1.5707963…，乘起來約 3.1415927，最接近 π 的單精度數。


@q pl3-24 | co-pipeline#3 | 1 | 追蹤
2-bit 飽和計數器（0、1 預測不跳，2、3 預測跳），從 0 開始，依序遇到「跳、跳、不跳」。最後狀態？
== 1
---
跳：0→1、1→2；不跳：2→1。現在是「弱不跳」，下一次會預測不跳。


@q ca1-24 | co-cache#1 | 1 | 計算
256 KB、8-way、區塊 64 bytes 的 cache 有幾組？
== 512
---
$256\text{K}/(64\times8)=512$，index 9 位元。


@q ca4-24 | co-cache#4 | 3 | 計算
基本 CPI 1；每條指令平均 1.5 次記憶體存取；L1 miss rate 4%；L2 命中要 10 拍、local miss rate 25%；記憶體 100 拍。CPI？
== 3.1
---
每次 L1 miss 平均多 $10+0.25\times100=35$ 拍。每條指令 $1.5\times0.04\times35=2.1$，CPI $=1+2.1$。


@q pl1-24 | co-pipeline#1 | 1 | 計算
同樣的時脈下，不做管線時每條指令 5 拍（CPI 5），5 級管線因為停頓 CPI 是 1.25。加速多少倍？
== 4
---
$5/1.25$。理想是 5 倍，停頓吃掉一部分。


@q pf2-24 | co-perf#2 | 1 | 計算
程式跑了 $2\times10^9$ 條指令、花了 0.5 秒。MIPS 是多少？
== 4000
---
$2\times10^9/0.5/10^6$。只能拿來比同一個指令集上的同一個程式。


@q il2-24 | co-ilp#2 | 3 | 追蹤
Scoreboard 只有一個整數單元（負責 load）。時序規則：issue、讀運算元、執行完成、寫回各隔至少 1 拍，功能單元在寫回之後的下一拍才能再 issue。兩條不相依的 LD（執行 1 拍），第一條在第 1 拍 issue，第二條最早第幾拍 issue？
== 5
---
第一條：讀 2、完成 3、寫回 4，單元第 5 拍才空出來。結構危障讓第二條在 issue 卡 4 拍；多一個 load 單元或把單元管線化就能重疊。


@q ca3-24 | co-cache#3 | 1 | 計算
程式總共用到 1 MB 的不同資料，區塊 64 bytes。至少會有幾次 compulsory miss？
== 16384
---
$2^{20}/2^6=2^{14}$ 個區塊，每個第一次碰到都 miss。加大區塊可以減少這種 miss（如果資料有空間區域性）。


@q ca5-24 | co-cache#5 | 2 | 計算
DRAM 有 4 個 bank，每次存取讓那個 bank 忙 40 ns。存取平均分散到 4 個 bank 時，整體最快每幾 ns 能開始一次新的存取？
== 10
---
4 個 bank 輪流工作，$40/4$。存取集中在同一個 bank 時就退回每 40 ns 一次。


@q ca6-24 | co-cache#6 | 3 | 情境
A particle record contains eight four-byte members, including x, and has no padding. A loop reads only x for 1024 particles. Compare contiguous 32-byte records with a separate contiguous x array. Both begin on 64-byte boundaries; the empty cache is large enough. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] A block in the record layout contains eight requested x values.
[ ] A block in the separate x array contains eight requested values.
[ ] Both layouts require 512 distinct blocks for this loop.
[x] The separate x array needs 64 blocks.
[ ] Separating x reduces the size of each x value from four bytes to one byte.
---
「紀錄布局每塊八個 x」錯：每塊只放兩筆 32 位元組的紀錄，所以只有兩個 x。「分開每塊八個」錯：64÷4＝16 個。「都是 512 塊」錯：紀錄布局需 1024÷2＝512，分開只需 1024÷16＝64。「64 塊」對。「值縮成一位元組」錯：資料型別不變，差別是每塊帶進多少用不到的成員。


@q cd1-24 | co-datapath#1 | 1 | 計算
`lw $t0, -8($sp)` 的 16 位元立即值欄位（以 hex 表示）？
== FFF8
== 0xFFF8
---
$-8$ 的二補數：0x0008 取反加 1 得 0xFFF8。位址＝`$sp`＋sign-extend(0xFFF8)＝`$sp`－8。


@q cd2-24 | co-datapath#2 | 2 | 計算
單週期：取指 200 ps、讀暫存器 100 ps、ALU 150 ps、資料記憶體 250 ps、寫暫存器 100 ps。lw 和 beq 各需要多久？時脈要多長？
== 800,450,800
?? lw,beq,時脈
---
lw 全部都走：$200+100+150+250+100$；beq 只要取指、讀暫存器、比較：450。時脈要配合最慢的 lw，所以 beq 浪費了 350 ps。


@q il4-24 | co-ilp#4 | 3 | 觀念
An older instruction prevents a branch from committing until cycle 60. The branch detects a misprediction in cycle 10. Compare recovery on detection with recovery at commit; each policy fetches the correct path one cycle after its recovery event. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Recovery on detection can fetch the correct path in cycle 11.
[x] Recovery at commit first fetches the correct path in cycle 61.
[x] A saved rename-state checkpoint can support recovery without committing younger wrong-path instructions.
[ ] The early policy must commit wrong-path instructions to reclaim them.
[ ] The two policies first fetch the correct path in the same cycle under these assumptions.
---
「第十一拍」對：第十拍發現後一拍取指。「第六十一拍」對：等第六十拍提交再加一，晚了五十拍。「換名快照支援恢復」對：回復正確對應、丟棄年輕錯路徑即可，恢復與提交不是同一事件。「必須先提交錯路徑」錯：錯路徑正是不能提交的工作。「同拍」錯：較老指令的等待使兩政策相差五十拍。


@q pf3-24 | co-perf#3 | 1 | 計算
Gustafson：用 16 核心跑時，序列部分佔執行時間的 20%。擴展加速是多少？
== 13
---
$16-0.2\times15=13$。問題規模跟著變大時，序列比例固定，加速仍隨核心數成長。


@q pf4-24 | co-perf#4 | 2 | 計算
動態功率 $P=\alpha CV^2f$，$\alpha=0.5$、$C=2$ nF、$V=1$ V、$f=2$ GHz。功率幾 W？
== 2
---
$0.5\times2\times10^{-9}\times1^2\times2\times10^9=2$。$\alpha$ 是每拍實際切換的比例。


@q pl2-24 | co-pipeline#2 | 3 | 觀念
A five-stage pipeline has the usual forwarding paths into EX, plus one extra path from the MEM/WB register to the store-data input of the data memory. It executes this pair:
```
lw  $t0, 0($s1)
sw  $t0, 0($s2)     # store the loaded value elsewhere
```
If `lw` enters IF in cycle 1, its loaded word is available only at the end of cycle 4, when `sw` is in EX. A student wants to know whether this pair needs a stall, and how the answer changes for other instructions that use `$t0` right after the load, such as an `add` or a `sw` that uses `$t0` as its base address. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] `sw` can receive the value from the MEM/WB register of `lw` in time, so no stall is needed.
[ ] A one-cycle stall is always required, because this is a load-use hazard and the extra path only helps instructions that are two apart.
[x] For `add $t2, $t0, $t1` instead, one stall would be required, since `add` needs `$t0` in EX.
[x] If the next instruction were `sw $t1, 0($t0)` instead, using `$t0` as the base address, a one-cycle stall would be required.
[ ] `lw` and `sw` that use the same register can never be adjacent in a program.
---
「`sw` 到 MEM 才要存的值，可以轉送不停」對：`lw` 在 MEM 結束時拿到資料，下一拍 `sw` 剛好在 MEM，從 MEM/WB 送過去就來得及（需要這條轉送路徑）。

「一定要停一拍」錯：load-use 要停是因為使用者在 EX 就要值；這裡晚了一拍才用。

「換成 `add` 要停一拍」對：`add` 在 EX 就要用，那時 `lw` 才在 MEM，資料還沒出來。

「`$t0` 當基底位址也要停」對：位址在 EX 計算，同樣太早。

「不能相鄰」錯：完全合法，只是硬體要處理。


@q vc2-24 | co-vm-coherence#2 | 2 | 計算
P2 有 X 的副本。P1 連續寫 X 10 次後，P2 才讀一次。write-invalidate 和 write-update 各大約產生幾次匯流排交易？
== 2,10
?? invalidate,update
---
invalidate：第一次寫發 1 次 invalidate，之後 P1 獨佔可以靜悄悄寫；P2 讀時 miss 1 次。update：每次寫都要把新值送給 P2，10 次。


@q vc4-24 | co-vm-coherence#4 | 2 | 計算
16 條執行緒用樹狀屏障（每個節點等兩個孩子到齊再通知父親）。從最後一條到達到所有人都被放行，大約要經過幾層？
== 4
---
$\lg16=4$ 層往上回報，放行再往下 4 層。比所有人搶同一個計數器（16 次序列化的原子操作）好得多。


@q cd3-24 | co-datapath#3 | 1 | 計算
微程式控制用「分派表」依 opcode 跳到該指令的微程式。opcode 6 位元，分派表最多幾項？
== 64
---
$2^6$。每一項存那個 opcode 的微程式起始位址。


@q il1-24 | co-ilp#1 | 3 | 計算
`for (k=0;k<n;k++) s += x[k];` 的加法延遲 3 拍、每拍可以發出一個加法。只用一個累加器時，每 3 拍才做一次加法。至少要用幾個獨立的累加器（最後再加起來），才能每拍都做一次加法？
== 3
---
每個累加器每 3 拍才能接下一次加法，3 個輪流就能填滿管線。這是迴圈展開加上換名的典型用法（實數加法換順序可能讓結果有些微差異）。


@q vc1-24 | co-vm-coherence#1 | 2 | 計算
32 KB、4-way 的 VIPT cache，頁 4 KB。index 有 1 個位元來自虛擬頁號。作業系統讓虛擬頁號和實體頁號的那個位元一致來避免同義詞，實體頁要分成幾類？
== 2
---
每一路 8 KB＝2 頁，多出的 1 位元有 0、1 兩種值。配置實體頁時讓虛擬和實體位址的這個位元相同。


@q il3-24 | co-ilp#3 | 2 | 計算
Tomasulo 有 12 個保留站和 8 個 load 緩衝區，每個都要有唯一的標籤在 CDB 上廣播（另需一個值代表「沒有在等」）。標籤至少要幾位元？
== 5
---
共 $12+8+1=21$ 種值，$2^5=32\ge21$。標籤越多、CDB 和每個比較器就越寬。


@q pf1-24 | co-perf#1 | 1 | 計算
一次乘法指令要 10 個時脈週期；改成 3 條各 1 個時脈的移位、加法指令完成同樣的事。這個操作快幾倍？
== 3.33
== 3.3
---
$10/3$。指令數變多但總週期變少——比效能要看總時間，不能只看指令數。
